  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT图像增强评估主要依赖感知和临床有效性有限的图像质量指标，且现有数据集局限于孤立的修复任务，缺乏统一的多类型退化基准。

Method: SeSpeCT框架：1）利用医学视觉语言模型的语义先验，在嵌入空间中构建免训练的语义质量轴（基于放射学文本提示）；2）结合频域特征捕获退化特异性频率模式；3）联合预测退化类型和严重程度。

Result: 在单退化与混合退化设置下，SeSpeCT均一致优于所评估的基线方法。

Conclusion: CT-DegradBench为统一测评提供了标准框架，SeSpeCT证明了语义-频域融合在CT退化分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) images are frequently degraded by acquisition artifacts, including noise, blur, streaking, aliasing, and metal artifacts. Yet CT enhancement is still largely evaluated using image quality metrics with limited perceptual and clinical validity, while existing datasets remain focused on isolated restoration tasks, hindering unified benchmarking across diverse degradation types. We present CT-DegradBench, a dataset and benchmark for CT degradation detection and severity estimation under controlled single- and mixed-artifact settings. CT-DegradBench enables systematic evaluation across multiple degradation families and severity levels within a common experimental framework. We further propose SeSpeCT (Semantic-Spectral CT degradation estimation), a framework that combines semantic priors from medical vision-language models with complementary frequency-domain cues for artifact analysis. SeSpeCT constructs a training-free semantic quality axis in the multimodal embedding space using radiology-informed text prompts, without task-specific fine-tuning, and combines it with spectral features that capture degradation-specific frequency patterns. The resulting representation enables joint prediction of artifact type and severity. Experimental results show that SeSpeCT consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines under both single- and mixed-degradation settings. The framework is available at https://github.com/yousranb/CT-DEGRADBENCH.

</details>


### [39] [Multi-hop Relational Contrastive Learning: Extending Spatial Contrastive Pre-training Beyond Pairwise Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16456)
*Sheikh Tanvir Ahmed,Md. Tanvir Raihan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多跳关系对比学习（MRCL）框架，通过在场景图上追踪k跳路径来捕获隐式空间依赖关系，超越了仅建模成对关系的对比预训练方法。该框架定义了节点、边和多跳路径的多级对比目标，在GQA子集上实现了空间感知表示，提升了基于内容的图检索（NDCG@5=0.748）及下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比预训练方法大多仅建模成对关系，忽略了场景理解中更丰富的组合性和多跳交互，缺乏对隐式空间依赖关系的捕获能力。

Method: MRCL框架：从检测对象构建场景图，沿k跳路径追踪隐式空间依赖；定义多级对比目标（节点级、边级、多跳路径级），使嵌入在保持对象语义稳定性的同时响应用局布局。

Result: 在GQA子集上，MRCL在基于内容的图检索任务中NDCG@5达到0.748，并在空间关系识别和图问答等下游任务中持续获益。

Conclusion: 多跳关系监督比仅成对方法提供更丰富的结构指导，能产生更鲁棒、更具组合性和几何感知能力的视觉表示。

Abstract: Understanding how objects relate to each other in space is fundamental to scene understanding, yet most contrastive pre-training approaches only model pairwise relationships, leaving richer compositional and multi-hop interactions largely unexplored. We introduce Multi-Hop Relational Contrastive Learning (MRCL), a framework that extends spatial contrastive learning to graph-structured scene representations. By tracing k-hop paths through scene graphs built from detected objects, MRCL captures implicit spatial dependencies that go well beyond what direct object pairs can express. We define a multi-level contrastive objective spanning nodes, edges, and multi-hop paths, encouraging embeddings that remain stable across object semantics while staying responsive to spatial layout. On a GQA subset, MRCL produces spatially-aware representations that improve content-based graph retrieval (NDCG@5 = 0.748) and consistently benefit downstream tasks, including spatial relationship recognition and graph-based question answering. Together, these results suggest that multi-hop relational supervision offers substantially richer structural guidance than pairwise-only methods, leading to visual representations that are more robust, compositional, and geometry-aware.

</details>


### [40] [Conservative AI for Safety-Sensitive Medical Image Restoration: Residual-Bounded CT-CTA Enhancement for Intracranial Aneurysm-Relevant Signal Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16458)
*Weijun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差有界的2.5D图像恢复框架，用于颅内CT/CTA影像，通过编辑控制图限制修改幅度和空间范围，在提升图像质量的同时避免对临床关键区域（如小血管和动脉瘤相关结构）的不可控修改。在50例分布外CT-CTA病例中，模型平均目标增益0.0635，PSNR 37.51 dB，医源性编辑率4.0%，1000次蒙特卡洛模拟中85.4%保持净正向，无稳定负向案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像恢复模型在医学影像（尤其是颅内CT/CTA）中应用时，需要在不失控修改临床关键区域（如小血管和动脉瘤相关线索）的前提下提升图像质量。现有模型可能对高对比度解剖边界附近的重要结构产生不可控修改，存在安全风险。

Method: 提出残差有界（residual-bounded）的2.5D恢复框架：模型对退化CT/CTA输入学习一个残差，通过编辑控制图（edit-control map）限制残差的幅度和空间范围，仅对原始中心切片施加受控的修改。采用合成退化数据进行训练，并使用动脉瘤相关的图像恢复矩阵进行评估。

Result: 在50例分布外CT-CTA病例中，有界模型平均目标增益0.0635，PSNR 37.51 dB，医源性编辑率4.0%。1000次蒙特卡洛测试中85.4%保持净正向，无稳定负向案例。外部低剂量CT测试表明模型方向有益且修改足迹显著小于基线。有意义编辑集中在脑和颅骨区域，无关解剖区域变化可忽略。

Conclusion: 残差有界恢复在边界敏感的血管成像中是可行的，提供了初步计算证据。但尚未建立临床诊断性能，需经过专家评审和前瞻性验证后方可用于临床。

Abstract: Image restoration models are increasingly applied to degraded medical scans, but in safety-sensitive settings they must improve image quality without uncontrolled modification of clinically important regions. This is especially relevant for intracranial CT and CT angiography (CTA), where small vessels and aneurysm-relevant cues lie near high-contrast anatomical boundaries. We frame medical image restoration as a conservative AI problem and present a residual-bounded 2.5D restoration framework trained on synthetically degraded CT/CTA inputs. The model adds a learned residual to the original center slice through an edit-control map that limits the magnitude and spatial extent of modification. We evaluate the framework using an aneurysm-relevant image-recovery matrix, paired comparison against a Gaussian baseline, Monte Carlo stability testing, anatomical localization of meaningful edits, and external evaluation on low-dose CT. On 50 out-of-distribution CT-CTA cases, the bounded model achieved a mean target gain of 0.0635, a mean PSNR of 37.51 dB, and an iatrogenic-edit rate of 4.0%. Across 1,000 Monte Carlo runs, it remained net positive in 85.4% of runs with no stably negative cases. On external low-dose CT, the model was directionally beneficial and produced a substantially smaller modification footprint than the baseline. Meaningful edits concentrated in brain and skull regions while unrelated anatomy showed negligible change. These findings provide preliminary computational evidence that residual-bounded restoration is feasible in boundary-sensitive vascular imaging, but they do not establish clinical diagnostic performance and require expert review and prospective validation before clinical use.

</details>


### [41] [REC-RL: Referring expression counting via Gaussian and range-based reward optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16460)
*Hui Liu,Yunlai Teng,Kunlong Bai,Pengfei Qi,Haotian Yan,Liang Li,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REC-RL框架，通过强化学习优化指代表达式计数的视觉推理过程，采用"思考-范围-回答"范式和双轻量级奖励机制，在多个基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REC方法依赖基于规则的强化学习，奖励仅关注最终准确率，忽视了中间推理过程的质量。

Method: 提出REC-RL框架，采用"思考-范围-回答"范式；使用GRPO算法；设计两种轻量级奖励：基于范围的区间监督与高斯精度引导的准确率奖励，以及强制结构化输出的格式奖励。

Result: 在多个基准上相较于强基线模型取得一致改进，具备良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将中间聚焦预测建模为内部决策过程，避免了额外标注，且与人类感知更对齐，有效提升了REC的性能和推理质量。

Abstract: Referring expression counting (REC) is an intention-driven task that requires context-aware visual reasoning. While recent vision-language models incorporate language for visual understanding, most existing REC methods rely on rulebased reinforcement learning with rewards focused primarily on final accuracy, overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. We propose REC-RL, a reinforcement learning framework that introduces a think-range-answer paradigm to explicitly optimize the visual reasoning process. RECRL employs Group Relative Policy Optimization and two lightweight rewards: an accuracy reward that combines range-based interval supervision with Gaussian-based precision guidance, and a format reward that enforces structured outputs. By modeling intermediate focus prediction as internal decision-making, REC-RL avoids additional annotations and better aligns with human perception. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and robust generalization across benchmarks.

</details>


### [42] [Mechanistically Interpretable Neural Encoding Reveals Fine-Grained Functional Selectivity in Human Visual Cortex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16468)
*Idan Daniel Grosbard,Mor Geva,Galit Yovel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MINE框架，通过机械可解释性工具将编码模型从黑箱打开，定位自然图像中驱动每个体素反应的视觉特征，并生成语义可解释的描述，通过反事实编辑和图像生成进行因果验证，揭示了类别选择脑区内细粒度的体素功能结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将编码模型视为黑箱，仅通过相关性预测皮层反应，无法揭示哪些具体图像特征驱动每个体素的反应。

Method: MINE框架使用语言对齐的图像表示预测体素反应，应用机械可解释性工具定位关键特征，生成语义可解释的描述，并泛化为体素功能画像。通过图像生成和反事实编辑进行因果验证。

Result: 生成的图像能准确匹配原始图像引发的体素反应；反事实插入或移除预测特征能使体素激活按预期方向变化；体素功能画像引导的编辑产生更强的激活变化；MINE恢复已知的类别偏好并揭示体素间的细粒度结构。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性为发现和因果验证神经功能的细粒度假设提供了有效路径。

Abstract: A central goal in understanding human vision is to uncover the visual features that drive neuronal activity. A growing body of work has used artificial neural networks as encoding models to predict cortical responses to natural images, revealing the visual content that activates category-selective regions. However, existing approaches are largely correlational and treat the encoder as a black box, leaving open which image features drive each voxel's response. We introduce Mechanistically Interpretable Neural Encoding (MINE), a framework that opens this black box by applying mechanistic-interpretability tools to localize the features within natural images that drive millimeter-scale (voxel-level) activity. MINE predicts each voxel's response using language-aligned image representations, and produces semantically interpretable descriptions of the features critical for the voxel's activation. We further generalize these per-image features into per-voxel functional profiles. To validate the per-image descriptions, we show they are sufficient to generate images that elicit voxel responses matching the responses to the original images, more accurately than images generated from random or low-attribution controls. Moreover, counterfactually inserting or removing the predicted features from images shifts activation in the expected direction, providing causal evidence. Counterfactual editing guided by the per-voxel activation profiles produces even stronger activation shifts, indicating that the profiles faithfully capture each voxel's selectivity. Finally, we apply MINE to well-studied category-selective brain regions, showing it recovers their known categorical preferences while revealing fine-grained unique voxel structure within each region. Overall, our results establish mechanistic interpretability as a path to discover and causally validate fine-grained hypotheses about neural function.

</details>


### [43] [SeamCam: Quantifying Seamless Camouflage via Multi-Cue Visual Detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16515)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Abolfazl Meyarian,Mridul Khurana,Shuheng Wang,Pouyan Navard,Cheng Zhang,Anuj Karpatne,Wei-Lun Chao,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeamCam，一种基于视觉定位的量化动物伪装评估指标，通过计算最大可恢复定位信号来度量动物的可检测性，在人类实验中达到78.82%的一致率，并利用该指标作为偏好信号通过DPO微调扩散模型生成伪装图像，同时构建了CamFG-1.5k数据集以支持无偏基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺乏标准化的量化指标来评估动物伪装效果，通常仅依赖主观描述"无缝融合"，因此需要建立可重复、可量化的伪装评估方法。

Method: 将伪装评估构建为视觉定位问题：给定图像和目标物种，生成类别条件检测提议，提取分割掩码，选择与真实掩码IoU最高的子集联合，SeamCam分数为1减去该最大可恢复定位信号。利用该分数作为DPO偏好信号微调扩散修复模型生成伪装图案。

Result: 在94名参与者、2390组对比的人类二选一实验中，SeamCam与人类判断一致率达78.82%，比现有最优方法提升约25%。

Conclusion: SeamCam是一种有效的伪装量化指标，可作为扩散模型训练的目标信号，结合CamFG-1.5k数据集可实现更可靠的伪装生成与评估。

Abstract: Animals are described as effectively camouflaged when they blend seamlessly with their surrounding, yet no standardized quantitative measure of this seamlessness exists. We address this gap by framing camouflage evaluation as a visual localization problem: a well-camouflaged animal is one that remains difficult to detect even when its category is known. We introduce SeamCam (Seamless Camouflage), a metric that quantifies how detectable an animal is from the available visual evidence. Given an image and a target species, SeamCam generates category-conditioned detection proposals, extracts segmentation masks, and identifies the subset whose collective union yields the highest IoU with the ground-truth mask. The SeamCam score is one minus this maximum recoverable localization signal, where a higher score indicates stronger camouflage (i.e., lower detectability). In a human two-alternative forced-choice study with 94 participants and 2,390 comparisons, SeamCam achieves 78.82% agreement with human camouflage difficulty judgments, outperforming state-of-the-art by about 25%. We then demonstrate SeamCam's utility as a preference signal for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to fine-tune a diffusion-based inpainting model for camouflage generation. This offers an affordable training approach with an objective explicitly suited for camouflage generation, unlike typical diffusion models. To support rigorous benchmarking, we further introduce CamFG-1.5k, a curated dataset of 1,521 high-resolution images in which animals are fully visible prior to camouflage generation, enabling unbiased evaluation by controlling for occlusion artifacts present in existing datasets. https://7amin.github.io/SeamCam/

</details>


### [44] [DepthPolyp: Pseudo-Depth Guided Lightweight Segmentation for Real-Time Colonoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16519)
*Zhuoyu Wu,Wenhui Ou,Lexi Zhang,Pei-Sze Tan,Dongjun Wu,Junhe Zhao,Wenqi Fang,Raphaël C. -W. Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了DepthPolyp，一种基于伪深度引导的多任务学习与高效特征调制的轻量级息肉分割框架，仅3.57M参数、0.86 GMACs，在移动设备上超过180 FPS，性能优于大20倍以上的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有息肉分割方法在真实临床环境中因运动模糊、镜面反射和光照不稳定等问题性能严重下降，需要一种轻量级且鲁棒的分割框架。

Method: 采用伪深度引导的多任务学习架构，包含层次化Ghost分解（紧凑特征生成）、交错Shuffle融合（低成本跨尺度交互）和动态组门控（自适应组特征加权）三个核心模块。

Result: 在退化和干净目标域上均优于轻量级基线，与大量模型竞争。在PolypGen真实手术视频评估中，性能优于大20倍以上的模型，同时保持实时推理速度。

Conclusion: DepthPolyp能以极小参数量实现强跨域泛化和实时推理，非常适合资源受限临床环境中的实时部署。

Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation in colonoscopy is essential for early colorectal cancer detection, yet real-world clinical environments pose persistent challenges such as motion blur, specular reflections, and illumination instability. Most existing methods are optimized on clean benchmark images and suffer noticeable performance degradation when deployed in authentic surgical scenarios. We propose DepthPolyp, a lightweight and robust segmentation framework based on pseudo-depth-guided multi-task learning and efficient feature modulation. The architecture combines hierarchical Ghost factorization for compact feature generation, Interleaved Shuffle Fusion for low-cost cross-scale interaction, and Dynamic Group Gating for adaptive group-wise feature weighting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DepthPolyp achieves strong cross-dataset generalization when trained on degraded data and evaluated on both clean and noisy target domains, consistently outperforming lightweight baselines and remaining competitive with substantially larger models. In real surgical video evaluation on PolypGen, DepthPolyp achieves better segmentation performance than models up to $20\times$ larger while preserving real-time inference speed. With only 3.57M parameters and 0.86 GMACs, the proposed method runs at over 180 FPS on mobile devices, making it well suited for real-time deployment in resource-constrained clinical environments. Code and pretrained weights are available at: https://github.com/ReaganWu/DepthPolyp/

</details>


### [45] [SWoMo: Neuro-Symbolic World Model for Cataract Surgery Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16530)
*Ssharvien Kumar Sivakumar,Akwele Johnson,Anirudh Dhingra,Yannik Frisch,Ghazal Ghazaei,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWoMo是一种神经符号世界模型，通过解耦运动生成与视觉逼真度，实现了白内障手术模拟中的视觉真实感、物理交互和分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术模拟方法在临床应用中缺乏视觉真实感、物理交互以及训练分布外场景的模拟能力。

Method: 采用神经符号架构：符号部分（基于规则的模拟器+场景图）建模运动动力学和工具-组织交互；扩散模型生成逼真视觉外观（纹理、组织形变）。提出逆向配对策略，从真实手术视频重建模拟场景以训练视频扩散模型实现仿真到真实的翻译。

Result: 在视觉质量、物理交互泛化、下游阶段检测提升和无监督视频风格迁移等任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SWoMo通过解耦运动与视觉生成，更有效地满足了临床手术模拟的关键标准，具备良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Realistic surgical simulation plays a crucial role in training novice surgeons and in the development of autonomous agents. World models can scale such simulation environments to realistic and diverse procedures by predicting future patient states conditioned on current observations and surgical actions. However, current state-of-the-art approaches often fail to satisfy key criteria required for clinical applicability, including visual realism, physically grounded interactions, and the ability to simulate scenarios beyond the training distribution. Hence, we introduce SWoMo, a neuro-symbolic world model for cataract surgery simulation that decouples motion generation from visual realism. The symbolic component, consisting of a rule-based simulator and scene graph representations, models motion dynamics and tool-tissue interactions, while a diffusion model produces realistic visual appearance, including textures and tissue deformations. We propose an inverse pairing strategy that reconstructs real surgical videos in the simulator to obtain paired simulated and real videos, which are then used to train our video diffusion model for the reverse objective of sim-to-real translation. Our experiments show both qualitative and quantitative improvements over prior work. We demonstrate that our simulator further satisfies the key criteria, including generalisation to unseen interaction geometries, improvements in downstream phase detection, and unsupervised video style transfer. The code, data, and model weights are available at: https://ssharvienkumar.github.io/SWoMo/

</details>


### [46] [Attention-Aware Transformer-Based Aggregation Network for Video Periocular Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16550)
*Luiz G F Carreira,Breno A Mariano,Victor H C de Melo,David Menotti,William Robson Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的监控场景下视频眼周识别方法，使用深度CNN提取特征，并采用仅编码器Transformer聚合帧级特征，在COX Face数据集上达到99.8% TPR@1e⁻¹和96.6% Rank-5的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非受控监控场景中，传统人脸或虹膜识别难以应用，而眼周区域作为人脸最具区分力的区域之一，适合作为生物特征模态进行身份识别。

Method: 框架由两个模块组成：特征嵌入模块（深度CNN将眼周数据映射为特征向量）和聚合模块（仅编码器Transformer自适应地学习将帧级特征聚合为单一视频表示以及静态参考图像的特征向量）。

Result: 在COX Face数据集上，该方法一致优于朴素聚合方案，最佳场景下达到99.8%的TPR@1e⁻¹和96.6%的Rank-5。

Conclusion: 提出的注意力感知框架在监控视频眼周识别任务中表现鲁棒，验证了基于注意力机制进行帧级特征聚合的有效性。

Abstract: Video periocular recognition is the task of recognizing an individual's identity based on the region around an individual's eyes. The periocular area is one of the most discriminative regions of the human face, making it suitable for recognition tasks. Its use as a biometric modality has emerged as an alternative, especially in surveillance scenarios where conventional biometric traits such as face or iris recognition become unfeasible due to unconstrained acquisition conditions. This paper proposes an attention-aware approach for video-based periocular recognition in surveillance environments. The framework consists of two main modules: feature embedding and aggregation. The feature embedding module is a deep convolutional neural network that maps periocular data to feature vectors. The aggregation module is an encoder-only transformer that adaptively learns to aggregate frame-level features into a single video representation and a feature vector for the still reference image. Experiments on the publicly available COX Face dataset show the robustness of the proposed method, consistently outperforming naive aggregation schemes. In the best scenario, the approach achieves $99.8\%$ of TPR@$1e^{-1}$ and $96.6\%$ of Rank-5.

</details>


### [47] [TriALS: Triphasic-Aided Liver Lesion Segmentation Benchmark in Non-Contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16572)
*Marawan Elbatel,Mohamed Ghonim,Jiaji Mao,Zhuosheng Lin,Katharina Eckstein,Andrés Martínez Mora,Jonathan Deissler,Maximilian Rokuss,Constantin Ulrich,Zdravko Marinov,Wenhui Deng,Baoxun Li,Huijun Hu,Jun Shen,Mohanad Ghonim,Khadiga Omar Nassar,Mariam Elbakry,Menna Dyab,Amr Muhammad Abdo Salem,Nouran Elghitany,Noha Elghitany,Yi Qin,Xuanqi Huang,Haonan Wang,Shao-Woo Yen,Ahmed Elghamry Saba,Salma Ahmad,Xinyan Fang,Jiahao Zhang,Xiaodi Wang,Xinghua Ma,Gongning Luo,Jessica C. Delmoral,João Manuel R. S. Tavares,Ankan Deria,Adinath Dukre,Yutong Xie,Imran Razzak,Dongwook Kim,Matthew Choi,Hanxiao Zhang,Minghui Zhang,Xin You,Abdul Qayyum,Steven A. Niederer,Moona Mazher,Rachika E. Hamadache,Ricardo Montoya-del-Angel,Robert Martí,Xavier Lladó,Toufiq Musah,Livingstone Eli Ayivor,Enrique Almar-Munoz,Agnes Mayr,Kaouther Mouheb,Esther E. Bron,Stefan Klein,Ahmed Abouelhoda,Amira Adel,Susan Adil Ali,Rainer Stiefelhagen,Klaus H. Maier-Hein,Fabian Isensee,Aya Yassin,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TriALS挑战赛，用于在对比剂受限条件下自动分割肝病灶。研究基于一个多中心150例四期CT（600张扫描）数据集，来自埃及和中国多家机构。在70例测试集上，最优方法在静脉期达到0.754 Dice（与人类水平相当），但在非对比增强CT上降至0.57。外部验证中，领先方法在非对比增强CT上的Dice比现成模型高出28%。算法性能主要由训练数据规模和预训练策略决定。跨年度比较揭示了非对比增强CT上存在一个仅靠扩展预训练无法克服的持续性感知障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非洲和亚洲等资源匮乏地区，对比剂常不可用，因此非对比增强CT的肝病灶自动分割在临床上具有重要价值但面临根本性挑战。已有研究因缺乏标注的非对比增强CT基准数据而进展有限。

Method: 构建了一个多中心150例四期CT（600张扫描）数据集，来自埃及和中国的多个机构。组织TriALS挑战赛，在70例来自三家机构的测试集（包括独立外部队列）上评估多种算法。采用Dice系数等指标评价性能，并分析训练数据规模、预训练策略等影响因素。

Result: 最优方法在静脉期Dice达0.754（与人类水平一致），但在非对比增强CT上降至0.57。外部验证中领先方法在非对比增强CT上的Dice比现成模型高出28%。算法性能主要由训练数据规模和预训练策略决定。跨年度比较发现非对比增强CT上存在仅靠扩展预训练无法克服的感知障碍。

Conclusion: 尽管在对比增强CT上取得了接近人类水平的性能，非对比增强CT上的肝病灶分割仍面临重大挑战，现有预训练扩展策略无法解决这一问题，需要新的方法论突破。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of liver lesions on non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) is clinically important but fundamentally challenging, particularly in low-resource settings across Africa and Asia where contrast agents are frequently unavailable. Progress has been limited by the absence of annotated NCCT benchmarks. Here we describe the TriALS challenge for automated liver lesion segmentation under contrast-limited conditions, supported by a multi-centre dataset of 150 cases with four-phase CT acquisitions (600 volumes) from Egyptian and Chinese institutions. Algorithms were evaluated on 70 cases from three institutions, including an independent external cohort. The top-performing method achieved a mean venous-phase Dice of 0.754, consistent with human-level performance, yet dropped to 0.57 on NCCT. On external validation, the leading method outperformed off-the-shelf models by up to 28% in Dice on NCCT. Algorithm performance was most strongly predicted by training data scale and pre-training strategy. A cross-year comparison exposed a persistent perceptual barrier on NCCT that scaling pre-training alone cannot overcome. Data, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/xmed-lab/TriALS.

</details>


### [48] [Attend Locally, Remember Linearly: Linear Attention as Cross-Frame Memory for Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16579)
*Kunyang Li,Mubarak Shah,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自回归视频扩散模型因softmax自注意力导致的计算复杂度和内存随序列长度二次增长的问题，本文提出ARL2混合注意力模块，将跨帧注意力替换为固定大小的循环状态，实现线性时间复杂度和恒定内存占用，同时提升时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型依赖softmax自注意力，导致序列长度二次复杂度和键值缓存带来的线性内存增长，限制了长视频生成的扩展性。现有方法（如稀疏注意力、KV缓存压缩）虽降低单步成本，但未解决线性内存增长和流式上下文管理问题。

Method: 提出ARL2混合注意力模块，将自注意力分解为两个分支：帧内softmax分支（捕获空间细节和局部依赖）和帧间门控循环线性分支（通过固定大小的循环状态维护流式上下文）。关键设计包括：仅在去噪完成后更新循环状态以避免噪声干扰、所有token共享相同预更新状态以避免信息不对称。采用两阶段训练方案将预训练AR视频扩散模型转换为混合线性注意力架构。

Result: 75%的层被替换为混合线性注意力后，模型实现最高2.26倍实际加速和54%内存减少，同时保持可比质量并提升时间一致性。

Conclusion: 本文首次将预训练AR视频扩散模型转换为混合线性注意力架构，通过固定大小的循环状态替代二次跨帧注意力，有效解决了长视频生成的可扩展性瓶颈，实现了线性时间缩放和恒定内存，为流式视频生成提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion is a powerful paradigm for streaming and interactive video generation. However, its reliance on softmax self-attention leads to quadratic compute complexity in sequence length and memory usage due to key-value caching, which limits its scalability to long video horizons. Existing remedies (e.g., sparse attention and KV-cache compression) reduce per-step cost but still rely on a linearly growing cache or irreversibly discard past context, and thus fail to address linear memory growth and streaming context management. To address this scalability bottleneck, we propose ARL2 (Attend Locally, Remember Linearly), a hybrid attention module that replaces quadratic cross-frame attention with a fixed-size recurrent state. We decompose self-attention into two branches: an intra-frame softmax branch for spatial detail and local dependencies, and an inter-frame gated recurrent linear branch that maintains a fixed-size state for streaming context. Our key insight is that softmax attention captures fine-grained local interactions, while a recurrent state provides controllable long-range memory. This design achieves linear-time scaling with constant memory while improving temporal consistency over the full-softmax model. To prevent noisy intermediate states from corrupting memory, we update the recurrent state only after the denoised pass. To avoid within-frame information asymmetry, all tokens share the same pre-update state rather than sequential updates. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to convert a pretrained AR video diffusion model into a hybrid linear attention architecture, through an efficient two-stage training scheme for AR video. With 75% of layers replaced by hybrid linear attention, the model achieves up to 2.26 wall-clock speedup and 54% memory reduction, while maintaining comparable quality with improving temporal consistency.

</details>


### [49] [Controlla: Learning Controllability via Graph-Constrained Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16603)
*Jamuna S. Murthy,Amin Karimi Monsefi,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Controlla框架，通过图约束最优传输实现可控多模态生成中的结构化潜在几何控制，并在自建基准上验证了身份保持、跨模态对齐和轨迹一致性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控多模态生成方法依赖推理时条件化（如提示、引导），未显式结构化语义属性的演化路径，导致身份漂移和跨模态行为不一致。

Method: 采用模块化因子化控制框架，从多模态输入中解耦身份因子与属性因子，通过图约束最优传输将属性轨迹对齐到图先验，确保图一致性的同时保留参考身份。

Result: 在自建AffectHuman-43K基准上，相比基线方法在可控性、身份保持和跨模态对齐指标上取得一致改进，并验证了图敏感性、可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将可控性建模为结构化潜在几何性质，图约束最优传输是有效的对齐机制，且几何感知指标能更好评估轨迹一致性与潜在解耦度。

Abstract: Controllable multimodal generation is commonly formulated as an inference-time conditioning problem using prompts, guidance, or auxiliary modules. While effective, such approaches do not explicitly structure how semantic attributes evolve, which can lead to identity drift and inconsistent cross-modal behavior. We propose Controlla, a modular factorized-control framework that treats controllability as a property of structured latent geometry. Controlla learns identity and attribute factors from multimodal inputs and aligns them with graph priors using graph-constrained optimal transport, encouraging attributes to follow graph-consistent trajectories while preserving reference identity. To evaluate this setting, we construct AffectHuman-43K, a leakage-aware multimodal benchmark for reference-grounded affective control, and introduce geometry-aware metrics for trajectory consistency and latent disentanglement. Experiments show consistent improvements in controllability, identity preservation, and cross-modal alignment, with additional analyses on graph sensitivity, extensibility, and robustness.

</details>


### [50] [SCARED-C: Corrected Camera Poses for Endoscopic Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16628)
*John J. Han,Adam Schmidt,Max Allan,Jie Ying Wu,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCARED-C 是 SCARED 数据集的修正版本，通过 COLMAP 重新估计相机姿态并结合尺度恢复，将可靠的 RGB-D 样本对从 35 个扩展到 17,135 个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 SCARED 数据集中非关键帧图像的深度图依赖机器人运动学，存在较大位姿误差，导致仅有 35 个关键帧具有可靠标签，限制了数据可用性。本文旨在修正这一问题，大幅扩展可靠标注数据量。

Method: 使用 COLMAP（一种运动恢复结构系统）重新估计所有帧的相机位姿，然后通过尺度恢复步骤，利用真实关键帧深度图将重建结果对齐到度量空间。

Result: 将可靠的 RGB-D 对从原始的 35 个关键帧扩展到 17,135 个，并通过立体视差评估和单目深度估计实验验证了修正后姿态的准确性。

Conclusion: SCARED-C 有效解决了原始数据集中非关键帧深度标签不可靠的问题，大幅扩充了可用于内窥镜深度估计的训练数据，并已开源发布。

Abstract: The SCARED dataset is a widely used benchmark for endoscopic depth estimation, offering ground-truth 3D reconstructions captured with a structured light sensor. However, the depth maps for non-keyframe images rely on robot kinematics that introduce substantial pose errors, limiting the reliably labeled portion of the dataset to 35 keyframes. We present SCARED-C, a corrected version of the SCARED dataset that expands the number of reliable RGB-D pairs from 35 to 17,135. Our pipeline applies COLMAP, a Structure-from-Motion system, to re-estimate camera poses for all frames, followed by a scale recovery step that aligns the resulting reconstructions to metric space using the ground-truth keyframe depth maps. We validate the corrected poses through (1) stereo disparity evaluation and (2) monocular depth estimation experiments. The corrected dataset and code are publicly released to the community.

</details>


### [51] [Right Predictions, Misleading Explanations: On the Vulnerability of Vision-Language Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16651)
*Narges Babadi,Hadis Karimipour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言模型（尤其是CLIP模型）中解释热力图存在一个关键漏洞：可以通过对抗性扰动系统地操纵解释结果，而不会改变模型原始预测。作者提出了一种名为X-Shift的灰盒攻击方法，通过扰动patch级视觉表示，将解释热力图重定向到语义无关区域。该方法在ImageNet-1k、MS-COCO和Flickr30K数据集上验证了一致的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释机制被广泛用于支持视觉-语言模型的透明性和可信度，但其鲁棒性尚不明确。本文旨在探究CLIP模型中的解释热力图在对抗条件下是否能忠实反映模型推理过程，以及是否存在可被利用的漏洞。

Method: 提出X-Shift灰盒攻击方法：在保持模型预测不变的前提下，通过扰动patch级视觉表示，将解释热力图（如梯度类注意力图）定向偏移至语义无关区域。攻击无需修改模型参数，且可泛化至多种CLIP架构和解释方法。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k、MS-COCO和Flickr30K上，X-Shift在保持预测稳定性的同时，一致地降低了解释对齐度。标准对抗攻击即使使用更大的扰动预算，也无法复现类似的解释偏移行为。

Conclusion: 当前视觉-语言模型的解释机制存在根本性局限：预测行为与解释忠实度之间可被解耦。这严重质疑了将解释热力图作为高影响力应用中模型可信度可靠指标的有效性。

Abstract: Explanation mechanisms are increasingly used to support transparency and trust in vision-language models (VLMs), particularly in settings where model decisions require human oversight. However, the robustness of these explanations remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate whether explanation heatmaps in VLMs, particularly CLIP-based models, faithfully reflect model reasoning under adversarial conditions. We show that explanation maps can be systematically manipulated while preserving the model's original prediction, revealing a disconnect between predictive behavior and explanation faithfulness. To study this vulnerability, we introduce X-Shift, a novel grey-box attack that perturbs patch-level visual representations to redirect explanation heatmaps toward semantically irrelevant regions without altering the predicted output. Unlike conventional adversarial attacks that aim to induce misclassification, X-Shift specifically targets the integrity of the explanation process itself. The attack operates without modifying model parameters and generalizes across multiple CLIP architectures and explanation methods. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-1k, MS-COCO, and Flickr30K, demonstrating consistent degradation in explanation alignment under imperceptible perturbations while maintaining prediction stability. Furthermore, standard prediction-oriented adversarial attacks fail to reproduce the same explanation-shifting behavior even under substantially larger perturbation budgets. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of current explanation mechanisms in VLMs and raise concerns about their use as reliable indicators of model trustworthiness in high-impact applications.

</details>


### [52] [Multi-Object Tracking Consistently Improves Wildlife Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16672)
*Mufhumudzi Muthivhi,Jiahao Huo,Fredrik Gustafsson,Terence L. van Zyl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多目标跟踪（MOT）模型融合时间序列上的分类概率，以纠正野生动物监测中单帧分类的噪声错误，在三个数据集上提升了2.0%~5.1%的加权F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野生动物分类模型在高质量数据集上准确率高，但面对真实环境（如时间序列推理）时预测不稳定，同一目标的标签在帧间频繁跳变，亟需利用时序信息提升分类鲁棒性。

Method: 采用标准多目标跟踪（MOT）模型对连续帧中的检测进行关联形成轨迹，然后利用轨迹融合各帧的softmax分类概率，得到一致的共识类别标签，替代单帧噪声预测。

Result: 在三个MOT数据集上，最佳MOT模型相比独立分类器分别提升加权F1分数5.1%、3.1%和2.0%，所有数据集和所有指标均优于基线。

Conclusion: 融合时序轨迹的分类概率能有效纠正单帧误分类，提升野生动物监测模型的稳定性和准确性。

Abstract: Camera traps have become a common tool for wildlife monitoring efforts in ecological research and biodiversity conservation. Wildlife classification models have benefited from the increase in wildlife visual data. These models reach high levels of accuracy on curated, high-quality datasets. However, their performance remains sensitive to real-world environmental constraints. They often produce inconsistent predictions when performing inference on temporally coherent sequences. The predicted label for a single individual shifts rapidly between frames. This study exploits the temporal nature of camera-trap data to augment inferred predictions from a wildlife classification model. Specifically, we adopt several standard Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) models to link detections across consecutive frames. The curated trajectories are used to fuse the softmax class probabilities. The fused probability score produces a single consensus class label estimate that overrides misclassifications caused by noise. The analysis of the experimental results shows that our proposed strategy improves over a standalone classifier over all datasets and for each metric. Specifically, the best-performing MOT models gain a weighted F1-Score of 5.1%, 3.1% and 2.0% over the classifier across three MOT datasets.

</details>


### [53] [GeoWorld-VLM: Geometry from World Models for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16713)
*Renjie Gu,Kaichen Zhou,Yan Luo,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWorld-VLM 通过将冻结的相机条件视频世界模型中的几何结构知识蒸馏到 VLMs 的视觉通路中，仅微调图像编码器和多模态投影器，在保持语言能力的同时提升了空间关系推理能力，在 What'sUp 和 VSR 基准上均提升约 4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLMs 在基本空间关系（如左、上、后、之间）上表现脆弱，根本原因在于视觉通路在特征提取阶段压缩或丢弃了关键 3D 结构线索，导致语言模型接收到的图像表示本身已不足以进行可靠的空间判断。

Method: 提出 GeoWorld-VLM 框架，利用冻结的相机条件视频世界模型作为教师，将单张静态图像通过采样的相机轨迹转换为合成的多视图空间信号；在 VLM 侧仅微调图像编码器和多模态投影器，通过空间答案监督、师生特征对齐和原始 VLM 保留锚点三个训练目标，使投影后的图像特征与中间世界模型表示对齐，主骨干保持冻结。

Result: 在两种不同 VLM 架构上均观察到一致的改进，在 What'sUp 和 VSR 空间推理基准上均提升约 4%，表明世界模型引导的视觉对齐在不同骨干结构和空间推理数据集上具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过在视觉通路层面注入 3D 几何结构知识而非修改语言模型，GeoWorld-VLM 有效提升了 VLMs 的空间关系推理能力，同时保留了原始模型的语言能力，验证了视觉表示增强对空间推理瓶颈的缓解作用。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong semantic recognition, yet remain brittle on elementary spatial relations such as left of, on, behind, and between. One cause of this failure arises before language reasoning begins: the visual pathway may compress or discard critical 3D structural cues during feature extraction, so the language model receives image representations that are already insufficient for reliable spatial judgment. We introduce GeoWorld-VLM, a VLM-side distillation framework that transfers geometric structure from frozen camera-conditioned video world models into VLMs. GeoWorld-VLM fine-tunes only the image encoder and multimodal projector, aligning post-projector image features with intermediate world-model representations while leaving the main backbone frozen. Given images, a prompt, and a sampled camera trajectory, the world-model teacher converts static visual input into a synthetic multi-view spatial signal. Training combines spatial answer supervision, teacher-student feature alignment, and a preservation anchor to the original VLM. Since the language model remains frozen, GeoWorld-VLM preserves the original model's linguistic capabilities while attributing spatial improvements to the enhanced visual pathway. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of the proposed method, we apply GeoWorld-VLM to two distinct VLM architectures and observe consistent improvements across both backbones. GeoWorld-VLM improves performance by approximately 4 percent on both the What'sUp and VSR benchmarks, suggesting that world-model-guided visual alignment generalizes across model structures and spatial reasoning datasets.

</details>


### [54] [MAVEN A Multi-Agent Framework for Multicultural Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16716)
*Shuowei Li,Yuming Zhao,Parth Bhalerao,Oana Ignat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAVEN是一个多智能体提示优化框架，通过将提示分解为人、动作、地点三个维度由专门智能体处理，显著提升了文本到视频生成在单文化和跨文化场景中的文化保真度，并在包含三种文化、243个提示和972个视频的基准测试中验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频生成在视觉保真度上进展迅速，但现有方法在单个提示中忠实呈现多种文化的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MAVEN多智能体提示优化框架，将提示分解为人、动作、地点三个维度，由专门智能体以并行或串行方式处理；并构建了包含243个文化提示和972个视频的基准数据集，涵盖三种文化、三种动作类别及单文化与跨文化场景。

Result: 基于CLIP指标、VLM评判和视频质量评估的综合评测表明，多智能体优化（特别是并行专业化）在保持视觉质量和时序一致性的同时，显著提升了文化相关性。

Conclusion: MAVEN框架有效提升了T2V生成的文化保真度，为跨文化视频生成提供了新的方法论和评估基准。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) generation has rapidly progressed in visual fidelity, yet its ability to faithfully represent multiple cultures within a single prompt remains underexplored. We introduce MAVEN, a multi-agent prompt refinement framework designed to improve cultural fidelity in both mono-cultural and cross-cultural T2V generation. MAVEN decomposes prompts into person, action, and location dimensions, handled by specialized agents operating in parallel or sequentially. To support systematic evaluation, we contribute a new benchmark of 243 culturally grounded prompts and 972 corresponding videos, spanning three cultures (Chinese, American, Romanian), three action categories, and both mono-cultural and cross-cultural scenarios. Evaluations combining CLIP-based metrics, VLM-as-judge assessments, and videoquality measures show that multi-agent refinement, particularly parallel specialization, significantly improves cultural relevance while preserving visual quality and temporal consistency. The dataset and code are available athttps://github.com/AIM-SCU/CRAFT

</details>


### [55] [Compositional Adversarial Training for Robust Visual Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16720)
*Anirudh Satheesh,Michael-Andrei Panaitescu-Liess,Andrew Xu,Georgios Milis,Heng Huang,Zikui Cai,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出组合对抗训练(CAT)，将水印鲁棒性建模为组合变换空间上的极小极大问题，通过学习可微的顺序对抗攻击选择策略，相比随机增强显著提升了水印容量和鲁棒性，在多个基准测试中最高提升63.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于随机后处理增强的水印训练方法无法覆盖现实攻击管道的组合空间，罕见但有破坏性的组合攻击很少被采样到，导致训练不稳定且样本效率低下。

Method: 提出CAT框架：将水印鲁棒性建模为结构化组合变换空间上的极小极大问题；学习一个顺序可微对抗器，观察当前水印图像并逐步选择攻击族以最大程度破坏消息恢复；采用直通Gumbel-Softmax攻击选择结合熵正则化，实现端到端可微的反向传播并聚合跨攻击族的梯度信息。

Result: 在VideoSeal 0.0/1.0、PixelSeal和WMAR等多个水印方法上，CAT在单步攻击设置下最多提升63.5%的水印容量，组合设置下提升13.0%；自回归设置下在困难几何变换上TPR@FPR=1%平均提升12%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的视觉水印应当针对自适应组合对抗器进行训练，而非独立的随机干扰，CAT提供了一种有效的即插即用解决方案。

Abstract: Robust watermarking is typically trained with random post-processing augmentation, but random sampling under-covers the combinatorial space of realistic attack pipelines and rarely encounters the rare compositions that actually break detection. This leads to unstable training and poor sample efficiency. We instead formulate watermark robustness as a min-max problem over a structured space of compositional transformations. We propose Compositional Adversarial Training (CAT), a plug-in framework that learns a sequential differentiable adversary that observes the current watermarked image and selects an attack family at each step to maximally disrupt message recovery. CAT combines a straight-through Gumbel-Softmax attack selection with entropy regularization, allowing the backward pass to be end-to-end differentiable and aggregate gradient information across attack families, yielding faster, smoother convergence without collapsing to a single attack mode. We evaluate CAT on post-generation watermarks VideoSeal 0.0, VideoSeal 1.0, and PixelSeal and in-generation WMAR under both single-step and two-step attack suites, on in-distribution and multiple out-of-distribution image and video benchmarks. CAT consistently outperforms random-augmentation baselines trained with the same augmentation budget, with the largest gains on hard composed attacks and OOD evaluations; improving overall watermark capacity by up to $63.5\%$ in the single-step attack setting and $13.0\%$ in the compositional setting. In the autoregressive setting, CAT improves the TPR@FPR$=1\%$ by $12\%$ on average on difficult geometric transformations. These results show that robust visual watermarking benefits from training against adaptive compositional adversaries rather than independent random corruptions.

</details>


### [56] [DiRotQ: Rotation-Aware Quantization for 4-bit Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16732)
*Sayeh Sharify,Mahsa Salmani,Hesham Mostafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiRotQ提出了一种W4A4后训练量化框架，通过PCA感知的旋转激活量化，在DiTs模型上实现了比现有方法更好的图像生成质量（FID 15.9, PSNR 19.1 dB），并设计了VLM-as-a-Judge评估协议，同时在12B FLUX.1-dev模型上实现了2.1x内存减少和2.3x速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)在推理时存在巨大的内存和计算开销，虽然4-bit后训练量化（PTQ）可以显著提升效率，但会导致严重的质量退化。现有方法（平滑、混合精度、旋转、低秩残差等）只能部分缓解问题，与FP16/BF16性能之间仍存在明显差距。

Method: DiRotQ框架包含三个核心组件：1) 通过PCA识别主导激活方差所在的低秩子空间，对该子空间系数保持高精度，其余分量量化至4-bit；2) 推理时将激活旋转到PCA基中（通过校准得到的正交变换），同时将逆旋转离线融合到层权重中；3) 结合GPTQ进行权重量化。此外还提出了VLM-as-a-Judge评估协议，首次在扩散模型量化场景中使用VLM进行感知质量和提示对齐的评估。

Result: 在PixArt-Σ模型的MJHQ-30K数据集上，DiRotQ在INT W4A4设定下达到FID 15.9和PSNR 19.1 dB，优于SVDQuant（FID 18.9, PSNR 17.6）。在12B FLUX.1-dev模型上，内存减少2.1x，速度提升2.3x（RTX 4090 GPU）。

Conclusion: DiRotQ通过旋转感知的激活量化有效缩小了4-bit PTQ与全精度性能之间的差距，在生成质量和推理效率上均超越现有方法，并且首次引入VLM-as-a-Judge评估协议，为扩散模型量化提供了更全面的评估手段。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image generation quality but incur substantial memory and computational costs at inference. While aggressive Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) to 4-bit precision offers significant efficiency gains, it typically results in severe quality degradation. Existing approaches, including smoothing-based methods, mixed-precision schemes, rotation techniques, and low-rank residual methods, partially mitigate this issue but still leave a noticeable gap to FP16/BF16 performance. In this work, we introduce DiRotQ, a W4A4 PTQ framework that mitigates this degradation through rotation-aware activation quantization. DiRotQ identifies a low-rank subspace capturing dominant activation variance via Principal Component Analysis (PCA), preserving coefficients in this subspace at higher precision while quantizing the remaining components to 4-bit. Activations are rotated into the PCA basis at inference time using calibration-derived orthogonal transformations, while the inverse rotation is fused into the layer weights offline. Combined with GPTQ-based weight quantization, DiRotQ achieves an FID (lower is better) of 15.9 and PSNR (higher is better) of 19.1 dB on PixArt-Σ over the MJHQ-30K dataset, outperforming the prior state-of-the-art SVDQuant (FID 18.9, PSNR 17.6) under the same INT W4A4 setting. Beyond standard metrics, we introduce a VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation protocol for diffusion model quantization, the first such evaluation in this setting, providing a more holistic assessment of perceptual quality and prompt alignment under aggressive compression. On the systems side, we implement a Triton-based custom kernel to enable efficient end-to-end inference, reducing memory usage of the 12B FLUX.1-dev model by 2.1x and delivering 2.3x speedup over the BF16 baseline, on a 24 GB RTX 4090 GPU.

</details>


### [57] [CAB: Accelerating Flow and Diffusion Sampling via Rectification and Corrected Adams-Bashforth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16736)
*Anuska Roy,Pravin Nair*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAB是一种无需训练的采样加速方法，通过将流/扩散模型转换到统一坐标系并结合Adams-Bashforth预测器与修正项，在6-20步的低步数区间内显著提升图像质量与计算效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型和扩散模型虽然能生成高保真、高分辨率图像，但采样时需要大量函数评估（NFE），现有加速方法（蒸馏需要额外训练、高阶求解器会降低样本质量）在低NFE预算下效果不佳。

Method: CAB首先将采样动力学变换到统一的整流坐标系，然后应用多步Adams-Bashforth预测器，并基于过去的速度评估添加一个简单的修正项（不增加额外NFE）。该方法具有三阶局部截断误差和二阶全局误差。

Result: 在类条件生成和文生图等多个预训练模型上，CAB在6-20 NFE的低步数区间改善了质量-NFE权衡，在高步数下仍与强基线方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: CAB是一种简单、统一、无需训练的采样加速器，能同时适用于流模型和扩散模型，在低步数场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion models achieve high-fidelity, high-resolution image synthesis, but often require many function evaluations (NFEs) at sampling time. Existing acceleration methods either require additional training through distillation or rely on training-free high-order solvers, and both can degrade sample quality at low NFE budgets. We propose CAB (Corrected Adams-Bashforth), a training-free sampler that accelerates both flow and diffusion models. CAB first transforms the sampling dynamics to a common rectified coordinate system, and then applies a multistep Adams-Bashforth predictor augmented with a simple correction term based on past velocity evaluations and therefore incurs no additional NFEs. The resulting method is simple, has the same algorithmic form across model classes, and has at least third-order local truncation error and second-order global error. Experiments on pretrained flow and diffusion models, including class-conditional and large-scale text-to-image benchmarks, show that CAB improves quality-NFE trade-offs in the low-step regime of 6-20 NFEs. It also remains competitive with strong training-free samplers at higher step counts across most tested models. The official implementation is available at https://github.com/Anuska-Roy/CAB.

</details>


### [58] [TRACE: Evidence Grounding-Guided Multi-Video Event Understanding and Claim Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16740)
*Pengyu Yan,Akhil Gorugantu,Mahesh Bhosale,Abdul Wasi,Vishvesh Trivedi,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE提出了一种"先定位后推理"的证据引导框架，通过构建结构化文本可搜索时间线，利用纯文本LLM进行查询感知的证据定位，再引导LVLM进行跨视频事件推理，在MAGMaR 2026上显著提升了事实完整性和归因保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在处理多视频事件理解时存在两个关键问题：一是上下文预算快速耗尽，二是难以精确定位具有证据重要性的视频片段，经常遗漏如广播图形、字幕和记分牌等密集信息线索。

Method: TRACE采用"先定位后推理"（ground-before-reasoning）策略：首先利用OCR和物体检测为每个视频构建结构化的文本可搜索时间线；然后通过纯文本LLM进行查询感知的证据定位，在视觉推理之前选择相关时刻；最后利用检索到的帧及其定位摘要引导LVLM进行声明生成和跨视频引用整合。

Result: 在MAGMaR 2026验证集上，TRACE将macro-average MiRAGE F1从0.705提升至0.811，引用召回率从0.440提升至0.628。在WikiVideo数据集上同样验证了结构化定位对事实完整性和归因保真度的提升效果。该方法在MAGMaR 2026官方排行榜上取得了当前最优结果。

Conclusion: 结构化证据定位能够显著提升多视频事件理解中的事实完整性和归因保真度，证明了"先定位后推理"策略在长视频异构语料推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-video event understanding demands models that can locate and attribute query-relevant evidence scattered across long, heterogeneous video corpora. Existing large vision-language models (LVLMs) often underperform in this regime because they quickly exhaust their context budget and struggle to precisely localize evidentially important segments, frequently missing dense informational cues such as broadcast graphics, subtitles, and scoreboards. We introduce TRACE, an evidence grounding-guided framework that follows a ground-before-reasoning strategy for multi-video event reasoning. Our approach first builds a structured, text-searchable timeline for each video using OCR and object detection. A text-only LLM then conducts query-aware evidence localization, selecting relevant moments prior to any downstream visual reasoning. The retrieved frames and their grounding summaries are subsequently used to steer LVLM-based claim generation and cross-video citation consolidation. Experiments on MAGMaR 2026 and WikiVideo demonstrate that structured grounding markedly boosts factual completeness and attribution fidelity. On the MAGMaR validation split, TRACE raises macro-average MiRAGE F1 from 0.705 to 0.811 compared to an unguided Qwen3-VL-30B baseline, with especially strong improvements in citation recall from 0.440 to 0.628. The method also attains state-of-the-art results on the official MAGMaR 2026 leaderboard.

</details>


### [59] [EVA01: Unified Native 3D Understanding and Generation via Mixture-of-Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16745)
*Zongyuan Yang,Mingjing Yi,Wanli Ma,Chenzhuo Fan,Bocheng Li,Baolin Liu,Yuke Lou,Yingde Song,Yongping Xiong,Zhengdong Guo,Shimu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVA01框架，基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构，将3D网格作为原生模态集成到多模态大语言模型中，实现了文本到3D生成、长上下文多轮几何编辑等功能，无需中间2D表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法或基于扩散的统计重建模型将语义理解与几何推理分离，或将3D模态作为外部输出而非原生组件，缺乏对几何流形与MLLM特征空间对齐的系统分析。

Method: 基于Mixture-of-Transformers架构，将模型解耦为预训练的Understanding Expert和结构镜像的Generation Expert，通过共享全局自注意力与硬模态路由耦合，使MLLM骨干的语义潜在空间与几何流形对齐。

Result: 在原生文本到3D生成保真度上达到SOTA，并实现了统计重建管道无法实现的长上下文多轮几何编辑与身份保持能力。

Conclusion: EVA01成功将3D网格作为原生模态集成到MLLM中，为2D基础模型与3D任务融合提供了架构设计见解，指导了3D原生多模态系统的设计。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of integrating 3D meshes as a native modality within Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Diffusion-based large reconstruction models decouple semantic understanding from geometric reasoning, operating as stateless reconstructors conditioned on dense 2D pixel priors. Recent MLLM-based methods treat the 3D modality as an external output rather than a native component of the multimodal sequence, making incremental adaptations without a systematic analysis of how geometric manifolds align with MLLM feature spaces. We introduce EVA01, a unified framework that extends the modality boundary of MLLMs to natively incorporate 3D mesh understanding, generation, and context-aware editing. Built upon a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, EVA01 decouples the model into a pre-trained Understanding Expert ($E_{\mathrm{und}}$) and a structurally mirrored Generation Expert ($E_{\mathrm{gen}}$), coupled through shared global self-attention with hard modality routing. This design aligns the semantic latent space of the MLLM backbone with the geometric manifold, enabling direct transfer of multimodal priors without intermediate 2D representations. Results show that EVA01 achieves state-of-the-art native text-to-3D generation fidelity and unlocks robust long-context multi-turn geometric editing with identity preservation, a capability fundamentally inaccessible to stateless reconstruction pipelines. Our findings further offer architectural insights for integrating 2D foundation models with 3D tasks, informing the design of 3D-native multimodal systems. Project Page: https://www.seeles.ai/research/pages/EVA01

</details>


### [60] [Axial-Relation Guided Fusion State Space Model for Optical-Elevation Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16768)
*Feng Gao,Zhilin Jin,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong,Qian Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARG-Mamba 是一个基于状态空间模型的多源遥感图像语义分割框架，通过多尺度状态空间模块和轴向关系引导融合模块，在 ISPRS Vaihingen 和 Potsdam 数据集上取得了超越现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多源遥感图像语义分割中难以充分建模多尺度上下文信息，且跨模态特征融合效果不佳，限制了在高分辨率复杂场景中的表现。

Method: 提出了 ARG-Mamba 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 多尺度状态空间模块，以线性计算复杂度捕获细粒度局部细节和全局上下文依赖；(2) 轴向关系引导融合模块，沿水平和垂直轴显式建模全局跨模态相关性，实现光学与高程模态的高效特征融合。

Result: 在 ISPRS Vaihingen 和 Potsdam 数据集上的大量实验表明，ARG-Mamba 在保持良好计算效率的同时，持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ARG-Mamba 通过状态空间模型有效解决了多尺度上下文建模和跨模态特征融合问题，为光学-高程遥感图像分割提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of multi-source remote sensing images is a fundamental task for Earth observation applications. Existing methods often struggle with insufficient multi-scale context modeling and suboptimal cross-modal feature fusion, limiting their performance in complex high-resolution scenes. To this end, we propose Axial-Relation Guided Fusion Mamba (ARG-Mamba), a state space model-based framework for optical-elevation remote sensing image segmentation. Specifically, we introduce a Multi-Scale State Space Module to capture both fine-grained local details and global contextual dependencies with linear computational complexity. Moreover, an Axial-Relation Guided Fusion Module is designed to explicitly model global cross-modal correlations along horizontal and vertical axes, enabling efficient feature fusion between optical and elevation modalities. Extensive experiments conducted on the ISPRS Vaihingen and Potsdam datasets demonstrate that our ARG-Mamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods while maintaining favorable computational efficiency. The code will be made publicly available at \url{https://github.com/oucailab/ARG-Mamba}.

</details>


### [61] [GLT-PEFT: Gated Lie-Tucker Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis with Hippocampal Segmentation Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16769)
*Guanghua He,Hancan Zhu,Gaohang Yu,An Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GLT-PEFT框架，结合Tucker分解和张量Lie群变换，实现3D卷积核的低秩几何感知微调，通过门控机制统一加性/乘性更新，在阿尔茨海默病诊断任务中以极少参数量实现了高效的跨任务迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法面向矩阵结构参数，不适用于医学影像模型中的高维卷积核；且依赖加性更新，缺乏对预训练参数几何结构的保持，乘性（几何感知）更新难以统一集成。

Method: 提出GLT-PEFT框架：(1) Tucker分解实现3D卷积核的张量感知低秩适配；(2) Lie群变换提供保持结构的乘性更新；(3) 门控机制协调加性与乘性更新形式，形成统一稳定的微调策略。

Result: 在海马体分割预训练模型向AD分类下游任务的跨任务迁移中，GLT-PEFT显著减少了可训练参数量，同时实现了有效的迁移性能。

Conclusion: GLT-PEFT为医学影像模型提供了一种高效且鲁棒的参数高效微调方案，验证了张量分解与Lie群几何变换在PEFT中的有效性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for adapting pretrained models under limited data conditions. However, most existing PEFT methods are designed for matrix-structured parameters and are not well suited for high-dimensional convolutional kernels in medical imaging models. Moreover, they typically rely on additive updates and lack mechanisms to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained parameters, while multiplicative (geometry-aware) updates are difficult to integrate within a unified framework. To address this issue, this paper proposes GLT-PEFT, a gated Lie-Tucker parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis. The proposed approach transfers a hippocampal segmentation pretrained model to a downstream classification task. Tucker decomposition enables tensor-aware low-rank adaptation of 3D convolutional kernels, while Lie group-based transformations provide structure-preserving multiplicative updates. A gating mechanism further reconciles additive and multiplicative update forms, resulting in a unified and more stable fine-tuning strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GLT-PEFT achieves effective cross-task transfer while significantly reducing trainable parameters, highlighting its effectiveness for efficient and robust adaptation in medical imaging models.

</details>


### [62] [CANSURF: An ASV-View Can Dataset and Benchmark for Detection and Tracking of Surface-Level Debris](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16774)
*Zaid Aljundi,Zahra F. Rahmatullah,Mostafa Elemam,Abdullah Moosa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向水面铝罐检测的ASV视觉系统和新数据集CANSURF，包含约7.3k原始图像并通过数据增强扩展至~57k张。实验表明，YOLOv11在该数据集上训练相比通用数据集性能提升12倍；YOLOv11+ByteTrack在多目标跟踪稳定性上最优，而YOLOv11+SAHI在远场检测召回率更高，适用于单罐抓取任务。该数据集填补了水面铝罐检测的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水面漂浮的铝罐等小型反光目标在强光、波纹、半淹没条件下难以被自动清理系统远程检测，现有公开数据集缺乏从水面视角拍摄的专门数据，制约了自主水面清理机器人的感知性能。

Method: 收集~7.3k原始图像并标注边界框，通过十种数据增强扩展至~57k训练/验证集；基于YOLOv11构建检测器和检测器-跟踪器流水线，对比评估了YOLOv11+ByteTrack和YOLOv11+SAHI等方案。

Result: 在CANSURF上训练的YOLOv11检测性能比通用数据集提升12倍；YOLOv11+ByteTrack在身份切换次数和多目标跟踪精度上最优；YOLOv11+SAHI在远场检测召回率更高，但全上下文输入时精度略低。

Conclusion: CANSURF数据集有效填补了水面铝罐检测的数据空白；针对单罐抓取任务，YOLOv11+SAHI在检测到最多罐子方面表现更优；该工作支持可重复评估，为自主水面清理提供了基准。

Abstract: Surface-level marine debris remains a practical bottleneck for autonomous clean-up, where small, reflective targets (e.g., aluminum cans) must be detected at distance under glare, ripples, and partial submersion. This paper presents, an ASV vision system and a new surface-can dataset. The dataset comprises ~7.3k raw images extracted from videos and annotated with bounding boxes, expanded via ten augmentation types to ~57k training/validation images spanning diverse lighting and water states. A family of detector and detector-tracker pipelines tailored to surface operations were benchmarked. Training YOLOv11 on CANSURF boosts performance 12x over generic datasets, highlighting the dataset's value. Experiments show that YOLOv11+ByteTrack yields the most stable tracks (fewer identity switches) and stronger multi-object accuracy under, while YOLOv11+SAHI increases recall on far-field cans at the cost of lower precision in full-context inputs. Given the mission profile, single-can pickup with approach and grab, YOLOv11 + SAHI proves better for detecting the maximum number of cans. No prior open dataset targets aluminum cans on water from a surface-level viewpoint; this dataset fills this gap and supports reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [63] [VolTA-3D: Self-Supervised Learning for Brain MRI using 3D Volumetric Token Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16775)
*Amy Makawana,Abhijeet Parida,Marius George Linguraru,Julia Ive,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Volta-3D是一个自监督3D Vision Transformer框架，通过联合对齐全局类标签令牌和局部补丁令牌以及细粒度结构重建，学习可迁移的体素表示，在脑MRI的多个下游任务（海马体分割、性别分类、阿尔茨海默病分类）中优于随机初始化基线，表现出更好的跨域迁移性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D脑MRI模型要么专用于分割要么专用于分类，缺乏跨数据集、成像协议和下游任务的泛化能力，限制了临床实用性。同时，脑MRI的语义多样性有限且解剖特征细微，现有SSL方法难以有效学习可迁移表示。

Method: 基于学生-教师范式的3D Vision Transformer，联合优化全局类令牌对齐（global class-style token alignment）和局部补丁令牌对齐（local patch token alignment），并引入细粒度结构重建损失，实现全局语义一致性与局部结构学习的协同预训练。

Result: 在多个分布外下游任务（海马体分割、性别分类、阿尔茨海默病vs健康对照分类）中，Volta-3D学到的表示在所有任务上均优于随机初始化基线，展现更强的迁移性和对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合全局语义一致性和局部结构学习的自监督预训练能够从无标签脑MRI数据中学到更广泛的概念，支持多任务下游性能，向通用且临床可行的3D模型迈进一步。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has advanced medical image analysis be enabling learning form large unlabelled data. However, in brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), most 3D models remain specialized for either segmentation of classification, limiting their ability to generalize across datasets, imaging protocols,, and downstream tasks. This lack of transferability constrains the clinical utility of 3D MRI models, despite the availability of unlabeled volumetric data. We present Volta-3D, a self-supervised 3D Vision Transformer framework designed to learn transferable volumetric representations. Volta-3D jointly aligns global class-style tokens and local patch tokens within a student-teacher paradigm and enforces fine-grained structural reconstruction. This combined global-local alignment addresses the limited semantic diversity and subtle anatomical characteristics of brain MRI, which challenges existing SSL approaches. We evaluate Volta-3D on multiple out-of-distribution downstream tasks, including hippocampal segmentation and classification of sex and Alzheimer's disease versus healthy controls. Across all tasks, representations learned by Volta-3D outperform randomly initialized baselines, demonstrating improved transferability and robustness under domain shift. Hence jointly enforcing global semantic consistency and local structural learning during pretraining enables broader concept learning from unlabeled brain MRI data. Overall VolTA-3D supports effective multi-task downstream performance with task-specific pertaining, a step towards generalizable and clinically viable 3D models.

</details>


### [64] [A Holistic Method for Superquadric Fitting Using Unsupervised Clustering Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16779)
*Mingyang Zhao,Sipu Ruan,Xiaohong Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无监督聚类视角的统一超二次曲面拟合方法，能够同时处理刚性和可变形超二次曲面，且对噪声和异常值具有鲁棒性。通过将点云数据视为聚类成员、潜在参数曲面采样视为聚类中心，建立了几何拟合与聚类动力学之间的原理性联系，并推导出成对计算与正交距离的关系，避免了耗时的表面采样过程。该方法提供了模糊隶属度向量和协方差矩阵的闭式解析解，确保高效迭代优化，并从理论上证明了收敛性及避免局部最小值的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超二次曲面拟合方法要么仅针对刚性或可变形超二次曲面的单一场景，要么存在鲁棒性差和数值不稳定的问题。本文旨在提出一个统一的、鲁棒的框架，能够同时拟合刚性和可变形超二次曲面，并有效处理噪声和异常值的干扰。

Method: 将问题重新定义为无监督聚类视角：点云数据作为聚类成员（members），潜在参数曲面上的采样点作为聚类中心（centroids）。通过聚类过程中中心位置的动态更新来优化超二次曲面参数。进一步推导出聚类中心与成员之间的成对计算与正交距离之间的关系，消除耗时的表面采样步骤。提供模糊隶属度向量和协方差矩阵的闭式解析解，并通过增加目标函数的凸性来避免局部最小值。

Result: 该方法在噪声和异常值污染的点云上实现了对刚性和可变形超二次曲面的鲁棒拟合，具有高效的迭代优化性能，并提供了收敛性的理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的聚类启发的超二次曲面拟合方法在统一框架下解决了刚性和可变形拟合问题，具有鲁棒性、数值稳定性和收敛保证，为形状建模提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: This work presents a novel method for fitting superquadrics to point clouds under the contamination of noise and outliers, which has many applications for shape modeling across diverse fields. Unlike prior approaches that either exclusively focus on fitting rigid or deformable superquadrics, or suffer from robustness and numerical instability issues, our method redefines the problem from a new unsupervised clustering perspective, enabling the holistic fitting of both rigid and deformable superquadrics within a unified framework. Central to our approach is a stable optimization function inspired by unsupervised clustering analysis, where we formulate the point cloud data and samples from the potential parametric surface as clustering members and centroids, respectively. Then, the clustering process with dynamic updates to centroid locations serves as a direct proxy for optimizing superquadric parameters, establishing a principled link between geometric fitting and clustering dynamics. We further derive the relationship between pairwise computations of clustering centroids and clustering members to orthogonal distances, effectively eliminating the need for the time-consuming surface sampling process. Moreover, our formulation provides closed-form analytical solutions for both the fuzzy membership degree vector and the covariance matrix, ensuring efficient iteration optimization and enabling more effective handling of geometric deformations. In addition, we provide a theoretical certificate of convergence analysis and demonstrate that the clustering-inspired fitting method can escape local minima by inherently increasing the convexity of the objective function. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/zikai1/SuperquadricFitting.

</details>


### [65] [Encoding Robust Topological Signatures for Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16785)
*Arpan Kusari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑引导的超维计算方法，通过提取二值化形状中的离散拓扑基元（尤其是空洞），结合RTS不变的形状描述符（Zernike矩和傅里叶描述子），构建稳健的超维向量表示。在MNIST和EMNIST上，该方法显著提升了在旋转、噪声、遮挡等分布偏移下的鲁棒性，在保持轻量级在线学习优势的同时，相较于传统HD基线和紧凑CNN表现出更优的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基于像素的HD编码器在面对旋转、噪声、遮挡等微小分布偏移时非常脆弱，准确率大幅下降。需要一种更鲁棒的HD编码方法以适应边缘学习场景。

Method: (1) 从二值化形状中提取离散拓扑基元（空洞）；(2) 对外部形状使用空间金字塔变体的Zernike矩构建RTS不变描述符，对每个空洞使用径向签名的本征傅里叶描述子及RTS规范的相对几何构建描述符；(3) 通过随机投影和角色绑定将每个基元映射为双极超向量，通过置换不变的捆绑聚合变基数空洞集；(4) 通过学习非负可靠性权重对Zernike和空洞通道进行后期融合。

Result: 在MNIST和EMNIST上，面对旋转、高斯噪声、椒盐噪声、cutout、缩放等损坏，该方法相比朴素HD基线显著提升了鲁棒性，保持高准确率；与紧凑CNN相比，在干净数据上达到竞争性准确率，同时在多种像素级损坏上表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式拓扑结构是实现鲁棒HD表示的一条实用路径，拓扑引导的HD计算能够在保持HD轻量级在线学习优势的同时，大幅提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional (HD) computing offers an attractive alternative to deep networks for edge learning due to its simplicity, fast prototype-based inference, and compatibility with online updates. However, standard pixel-based HD encoders are brittle: small distribution shifts such as rotation, noise, or occlusion can drastically reduce accuracy. We extract discrete topological primitives-most notably holes-from binarized shapes and pair them with rotation/translation/scale (RTS)-invariant shape signatures. Our method constructs RTS-stable descriptors for (i) the outer shape using a spatial-pyramid variant of Zernike moments and (ii) each hole using an intrinsic Fourier descriptor of its radial signature together with RTS-canonical relative geometry. Each primitive is mapped to a bipolar hypervector via randomized projection and role binding, and variable-cardinality hole sets are aggregated by permutation-invariant bundling to form a single image hypervector. To avoid over-weighting any cue, we learn nonnegative reliability weights for the Zernike and hole channels on a validation set via late fusion of cosine similarities. Experiments on MNIST and EMNIST under controlled corruptions (rotation, Gaussian noise, salt-and-pepper, cutout, zoom) show that Topology-guided HD computing substantially improves robustness compared with a naive HD baseline, maintaining high accuracy across multiple corruption families and benefiting from lightweight online training. Compared with a compact CNN trained on clean data, our method achieves competitive clean accuracy while offering markedly stronger robustness to several pixel-level corruptions, demonstrating that explicit topological structure is a practical route to robust HD representations. The code is provided at https://github.com/arpan-kusari/Topological-HDC.

</details>


### [66] [Accelerating Rectified Flow Models via Trajectory-Aware Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16789)
*Xiao Liu,Kai Liu,Naiyang Guan,Hongliang Lu,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Renjing Pei,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TACache，一种基于"跳过-补偿"范式的训练无关加速框架，通过对整流流轨迹的离散速度进行正交分解（平行分量+正交残差），离线设定跳过调度，在线重构跳过的速度，在文本到图像和视频生成任务上分别实现最高4.14倍和2.11倍加速，且保真度优于现有缓存方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和整流流模型需要大量迭代的速度场评估，计算成本高；现有缓存方法通过跳步加速，但粗粒度近似在长跳步间隔中累积误差，在激进加速下质量下降严重。

Method: 提出TACache框架，采用"跳过-补偿"范式：对RF轨迹上的离散速度加速度进行正交分解（平行分量与正交残差），分离每步近似误差的幅度和方向来源；离线阶段基于幅度和方向指标的累积变化阈值生成跳步调度并限定跳步间隔上限；在线阶段利用离线统计量与样本历史正交方向重构跳过的速度，无需额外模型评估。

Result: 在BAGEL、FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1-1.3B上，文本到图像生成加速达4.14倍，文本到视频生成加速达2.11倍，所有参考保真度指标均一致优于现有缓存方法。

Conclusion: TACache通过轨迹感知的正交分解和跳步补偿机制，有效缓解了缓存方法中长间隔近似误差累积问题，在加速采样同时保持生成质量，是一种训练无关的通用加速框架。

Abstract: Diffusion and rectified flow (RF) models generate high-fidelity images and videos, but their iterative velocity-field evaluations are computationally expensive. Existing caching methods accelerate sampling by skipping timesteps, yet their coarse approximations introduce accumulated errors over long skip intervals and degrade quality under aggressive acceleration. We propose TACache (Trajectory-Aware Cache), a training-free acceleration framework following a skip-then-compensate paradigm. TACache performs an orthogonal decomposition of discrete velocity acceleration along the RF trajectory into a parallel component and an orthogonal residual, isolating the magnitude and directional sources of per-step approximation error. The framework operates in two stages: offline, cumulative variation thresholds on the magnitude and direction indicators yield the skip schedule and bound how far each skip interval may extend; online, at each skipped step the offline statistics are combined with the sample's historical orthogonal direction to reconstruct the skipped velocity without additional model evaluations. Experiments on BAGEL, FLUX.1-dev, and Wan2.1-1.3B show that TACache achieves up to 4.14 speedup on text-to-image generation and 2.11 speedup on text-to-video generation, with consistent improvements over prior cache-based methods on all reference-based fidelity metrics. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [67] [EgoKit: Towards Unified Low-Cost Egocentric Data Collection with Heterogeneous Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16797)
*Liuchuan Yu,Erdem Murat,Beichen Wang,Yan Zeng,Tingting Luo,Huizhen Zhou,Shanghao Li,Huining Feng,Zhigen Zhao,Ning Yang,Ke Jing,Yunhao Liu,Ruoya Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoKit是一个统一的跨设备第一人称视频录制工具包，支持6种异构主机设备（Android/iOS手机、平板、智能眼镜、XR头显），提供标准化的录制交互、统一日志格式，并在XR头显上支持头部姿态和双手追踪，配合配套配件可实现手腕视角同步录制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前以自我为中心的视频（ego-centric video）在机器人学习、活动理解和具身AI研究中需求日益增长，但不同设备（Android/iOS手机、平板、智能眼镜、XR头显等）的SDK、摄像头访问策略和外设支持各不相同，导致自我视角和手腕视角的同步录制要么局限于单一平台，要么需要构建不能跨设备使用的一次性硬件装置，缺乏统一的跨设备解决方案。

Method: EgoKit设计了一套统一的录制工作流，跨越6种异构主机设备，保持一致的录制交互界面和本地存储格式。对于XR头显，额外记录头部姿态和OpenXR标准的26关节点手部追踪数据，并与视频流对齐。配套配件（两个手腕摄像头及支架、头带、USB-C集线器）无需定制硬件制造即可为任何支持的主机添加手腕视角录制功能。

Result: EgoKit成功在6种异构设备上实现了统一的自我中心视角录制体验，在XR头显上实现了视频与头部姿态、26关节点手部追踪数据的对齐，并通过通用配件实现了跨设备的手腕视角同步录制。

Conclusion: EgoKit填补了跨设备自我中心视频录制缺乏统一工具包的空白，为机器人学习、活动理解和具身AI研究提供了可跨平台复用的标准化数据采集方案。

Abstract: Egocentric video is increasingly used as a data source for robot learning, activity understanding, and embodied AI research, but collecting it at scale remains fragmented in practice: each candidate host device, such as an Android phone, iPhone, iPad, smart glasses, or extended reality (XR) headset, exposes a different SDK, a different policy on raw camera access, and different limitations on external USB cameras and on-device tracking. Synchronized ego-view and wrist-view capture is therefore typically obtained by either committing to a single proprietary platform or building one-off rigs that do not transfer across devices. To address this gap, we present EgoKit, a toolkit that exposes the same egocentric recording workflow across six heterogeneous host devices. Across all supported devices, EgoKit presents the same recording interaction and produces locally stored video with a uniform log format; on XR headsets, it additionally logs head pose and OpenXR-standard 26-joint hand tracking aligned to the video streams. The companion accessories, including two wrist cameras with mounts, a head strap, and a USB-C hub, add wrist-view capture to any supported host without custom hardware fabrication. EgoKit is available at \url{https://egokit.chuange.org/}.

</details>


### [68] [NeuroLiDAR: Adaptive Frame Rate Depth Sensing via Neuromorphic Event-LiDAR Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16805)
*Darshana Rathnayake,Dulanga Weerakoon,Meera Radhakrishnan,Archan Misra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuroLiDAR通过融合稀疏LiDAR数据与神经形态事件相机，实现了自适应深度感知，有效帧率达~66 Hz，深度重建误差降低约29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR受硬件限制，在范围、空间分辨率和帧率之间存在权衡，通常以低帧率（5-10 Hz）运行，难以快速响应场景变化。

Method: 提出NeuroLiDAR框架，融合时间上稀疏的LiDAR数据与时间上密集的事件相机输入，包含两个核心组件：基于事件的关键帧检测和事件引导的深度外推，动态调整感知速率以匹配场景动态。

Result: 在ELiDAR数据集（涵盖室外和室内场景）上，NeuroLiDAR将深度重建RMSE降低约29%，同时实现27.8-47.3 Hz的自适应帧率。

Conclusion: NeuroLiDAR有效突破了LiDAR硬件帧率限制，通过事件相机辅助实现了高帧率、低误差的自适应深度感知。

Abstract: LiDARs are widely used for 3D depth reconstruction, but their performance is often limited by inherent hardware constraints that impose trade-offs between range, spatial resolution, and frame rate. Many LiDAR systems typically operate at low frame rates (e.g., 5-10 Hz), prioritizing long-range sensing over responsiveness to rapid scene changes. We present NeuroLiDAR, an adaptive depth sensing framework that achieves effective frame rates of up to $\approx$66 Hz by fusing temporally sparse LiDAR data with temporally dense inputs from neuromorphic event cameras. NeuroLiDAR integrates two components: event-based keyframe detection and event-guided depth extrapolation, to dynamically adjust the sensing rate in response to scene dynamics. To evaluate our approach, we introduce ELiDAR, a dataset spanning outdoor and indoor scenarios, and show that NeuroLiDAR reduces depth reconstruction error by $\approx$29\% in RMSE while achieving adaptive frame rates between 27.8-47.3 Hz. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/darshanakgr/neurolidar.

</details>


### [69] [DecoRec: Decomposed 3D Scene Reconstruction from Single-View Images via Object-Level Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16807)
*Yuhan Ping,Yuan Liu,Xiaoxiao Long,Peng Wang,Junhui Hou,Jianyi Zheng,Jia Pan,Xin Li,Cheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecoRec是一个将单视图2D图像提升为分解的3D场景网格的新系统，通过分别重建物体再融合优化的方式实现高质量场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图场景重建方法依赖物体检索或粗粒度3D体素/表面回归，导致外观和几何精度不足；且缺乏高质量大规模场景级数据集，使得直接从单视图图像生成3D场景困难重重。

Method: 利用扩散模型分别重建单个物体，再通过可微渲染和扩散引导的精炼管线将重建物体有效融合，优化外观和几何质量。

Result: DecoRec在几何重建和新视角合成方面均实现了高质量的单视图场景重建。

Conclusion: DecoRec为室内设计等下游应用提供了显著优势，验证了分解式重建+精炼融合策略在单视图3D场景重建中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce \textit{DecoRec}, a novel system designed to elevate single-view 2D images to a decomposed 3D scene mesh. Current methods for single-view scene reconstruction typically rely on object retrieval or the regression of coarse 3D voxels or surfaces, leading to inaccuracies in capturing the appearance and geometry of the input image. The lack of high-quality large-scale scene-level datasets further complicates direct 3D scene generation from single-view images. To achieve high-quality 3D scene generation from a single-view image, DecoRec takes advantage of recent diffusion-based single-view object reconstruction methods to reconstruct individual objects separately. Subsequently, a refinement pipeline is proposed to effectively merge these reconstructed objects, enhancing appearance and geometry through a differentiable rendering technique and diffusion-guided refinement. Our results demonstrate that DecoRec facilitates high-quality single-view scene reconstruction in both geometry and novel synthesis, offering significant benefits for downstream applications like room interior design.

</details>


### [70] [Training-Free Occluded Text Rendering via Glyph Priors and Attention-Guided Semantic Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16810)
*Jingqi Hou,Hongtian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的框架，通过重启双流推理（Base Stream与Edit Stream）和频谱字形先验，在FLUX.1-dev上实现遮挡文本渲染，显著提升文本可读性与遮挡对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成器在处理遮挡文本时存在遮挡物偏离文本、文本扭曲或浮于遮挡物之上等问题，亟需一种无需微调的解决方案。

Method: 采用重启双流推理框架：Base Stream提供干净排版参考和同步K/V特征，Edit Stream受遮挡提示条件化；结合FreeText的频谱字形先验稳定文本结构；通过推理阶段定位文本区域、估计文本带区域并推导锚点感知硬融合掩码；最终编辑阶段使用硬掩码引导的图像token K/V替换。

Result: 在代表性遮挡文本场景中，文本可读性显著提升，遮挡对齐具有竞争力，生成更稳定的对象-文本组合，且无需模型微调。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了遮挡文本渲染中的关键难题，为无训练文本图像生成提供了实用框架。

Abstract: We present a training-free framework for occluded text rendering with a pretrained FLUX.1-dev backbone. The task requires a model to render recognizable typography and place an occluding object over the intended text region. This setting remains difficult for existing text-to-image generators: the occluder often drifts away from the text, while the text may be distorted or appear to float on top of the occluding object. To address this problem, we propose a restarted dual-stream inference framework that decouples text-layout preservation from occluder insertion. A Base Stream provides a clean typographic reference and same-step key/value (K/V) features, while the Edit Stream is conditioned on the occlusion prompt. We further adopt the spectral glyph-prior idea from FreeText and adapt it to stabilize the target text structure during early-to-mid denoising. In the reasoning pass, our method localizes the target text, estimates a text-band region from token-conditioned attention and glyph support, and derives an anchor-aware hard fusion mask for the occluder. In the final edit pass, generation restarts from the same initial noise and applies hard mask-guided image-token K/V replacement at selected attention sites, preserving the Base layout outside the mask while injecting the occluder appearance from the Edit Stream inside the mask. Experiments on representative occluded text scenarios demonstrate substantially improved text readability and competitive occlusion alignment, yielding more stable object-on-text compositions without any model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [71] [Coarse Semantic Injection for LLM-Conditioned Structured Indoor Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16832)
*Shuliang Zhu,Tomiwa Adey,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向3D室内理解的点云语义增强方法，通过RGBB语义颜色编码和路由语义偏移模块，提升大语言模型对薄结构元素（门窗）和杂乱场景中家具实例的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为结构化解码器处理3D点云时，点云编码器经体素化和稀疏池化后，薄结构元素（门窗）和杂乱场景中的单个家具实例容易被欠表示或遗漏。

Method: 1) 将语义证据简化为四组编码（家具、墙壁、开口、其他），编码为RGBB点接口（红/绿/蓝/黑），附加到原始点属性后共享稀疏分词路径；2) 引入轻量级路由语义偏移模块（带辅助训练头），增强稀疏池化后的语义线索。

Result: 在Structured3D、SpatialLM数据集和ARKitScenes上，所提指标均有提升，尤其在开口定位和杂乱场景中逐实例家具检测方面效果显著。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了语义来源、颜色编码、token融合和偏移注入各自的作用，同时表明颜色/熵效应仍不可忽视。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been used as structured decoders for indoor understanding from 3D point-token inputs. However, point cloud encoders often under-represent thin structural elements such as doors and windows after voxelization and sparse pooling, and may miss individual furniture instances in cluttered scenes. We propose an interface-preserving semantic augmentation for LLM-conditioned structured decoding. The key idea is to associate semantic evidence with the point-cloud representation, reduce it to a coarse four-group code (furniture, walls, openings, and others), and encode it as an RGBB point interface: red for furniture, green for walls, blue for openings, and black for others, where RGBB denotes four semantic color states represented in three RGB channels rather than an additional fourth channel. This semantic color code is appended to the original raw point attributes before tokenization, so geometry and semantics share the same sparse tokenization path while the downstream language model decoder and output serialization remain unchanged. We further introduce a lightweight routed semantic shift module, with an auxiliary head used only for training-time ratio/budget regularization and analysis, to strengthen semantic cues after sparse pooling. The overall pipeline can use RGB-derived semantic evidence. Under these controlled semantic-source settings, the reported metrics improve across Structured3D, the SpatialLM dataset, and ARKitScenes, especially for opening localization and per-instance furniture detection in cluttered scenes. Ablations clarify the roles of semantic source, color coding, token fusion, and shift injection, while also showing that color/entropy effects remain nontrivial.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Relative Representations for Fine-Grained Multimodal Alignment with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16834)
*Shiwon Kim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于相对表示的后对齐方法，通过学习锚点来建模 token 级跨模态结构，在零样本分类、跨模态检索和零样本分割任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后对齐方法主要关注全局表示对齐，忽略了 patch-token 关系，难以迁移到需要细粒度跨模态匹配的任务。

Method: 通过学习可训练锚点，利用 token 级相似度构建相对表示，在无需额外投影层的情况下诱导匹配对的跨模态相似性模式一致性。

Result: 在零样本分类、跨模态检索和零样本分割任务上以较大优势持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 建模细粒度跨模态结构对于在有限配对数据下实现有效的后对齐至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal pre-training demonstrates strong generalization performance, but this paradigm is often impractical in domains where paired data are scarce. A promising alternative is post-hoc multimodal alignment, which aligns separately pre-trained unimodal encoders using a limited number of paired examples. However, existing methods focus primarily on aligning global representations, missing patch-token relations. This may hinder transfer to tasks that require fine-grained cross-modal matching beyond coarse sample-level semantics. To address this issue, we propose a post-hoc alignment method that learns token-level cross-modal structure using relative representations. Specifically, we represent images and texts through their token-level similarities to a set of learnable anchors in each modality space, which are trained to induce consistent cross-modal similarity patterns for matched pairs. Despite learning only the anchors without heavy projection layers, our approach consistently outperforms existing methods in zero-shot classification, cross-modal retrieval, and zero-shot segmentation by a substantial margin. This highlights the importance of modeling fine-grained cross-modal structure for effective post-hoc multimodal alignment with limited paired data.

</details>


### [73] [Thinking with Patterns: Breaking the Perceptual Bottleneck in Visual Planning via Pattern Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16848)
*Yichang Jian,Boyuan Xiao,Zhenyuan Huang,Yifei Peng,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的规划策略，通过将"用图像思考"（TWI）形式化为逐步构建和反思内部世界模型的过程，使VLMs能够解决超出其初始感知能力的复杂任务。同时，为提升效率，提出了模式推理（Pattern Inference）和模式归纳（Pattern Induction）策略，实现从经验中自主发现和重用视觉模式，在准确性与效率之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在处理超出其单步感知能力的复杂原始视觉输入时，规划能力存在瓶颈。尽管VLMs在通用TWI能力上训练良好，但在规划领域的感知瓶颈依然存在。

Method: 1) 将TWI形式化为逐步构建和反思内部世界模型的工具；2) 提出Pattern Inference，让VLMs主动识别已知视觉模式并直接推断局部世界模型结构；3) 提出Pattern Induction，一种在线归纳学习策略，将视觉模式视为可组合、可重用的专家，从经验中自主发现和优化。

Result: 在FrozenLake、Crafter和CubeBench三个领域的实验表明，所提方法在准确性和效率之间实现了理想的平衡。

Conclusion: 训练自由的TWI规划策略使VLMs能够解决远超其初始能力的任务，但过多TWI操作会显著增加计算开销；模式推理和模式归纳策略有效提升了效率。
