Abstract: Planning from raw visual input remains a significant challenge for current Vision-Language Models (VLMs), when the complexity of input is beyond their one-step perception capability. Motivated by recent advances in Thinking with Images (TWI), a reasonable solution is to decompose the perception process into simpler steps by iteratively acquiring and incorporating local visual evidence. However, even though current VLMs are well-trained in general TWI ability, their perceptual bottleneck in the planning domain remains. To tackle this challenge, we formulate TWI as a tool to gradually build and reflect an accurate internal world model. We find that the resulting training-free planning strategy enables VLMs to solve tasks that are far beyond their initial capabilities, at the cost that too many TWI operations would significantly increase the computational overhead. To further improve efficiency, we propose Pattern Inference, a novel TWI strategy enabling VLMs to actively recognize known visual patterns in the new tasks and directly infer local world model structures. To obtain these patterns, we propose Pattern Induction, an online inductive learning strategy treating visual patterns as composite and reusable experts, which are autonomously discovered and optimized from experience. Experimental evaluations in FrozenLake, Crafter and CubeBench domains show that our approaches achieve a desirable balance between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [74] [VGGT-CD: Training-Free Robust Registration for 3D Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16859)
*Wei Zhang,Songhua Li,Yihang Wu,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-CD是一种无需训练的三维变化检测管线，通过粗-细两阶段策略解耦跨时间配准与动态变化干扰，在11场景基准上将绝对轨迹误差降低44%-59%，配准速度提升6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维变化检测方法主要在2D域操作，视点变化易被误判为物理变化且缺乏深度信息；独立逐时期重建面临尺度歧义、配准-变化悖论（场景变化破坏配准）以及边缘飞点噪声等根本性障碍。

Method: 提出无需训练的VGGT-CD管线：粗阶段通过稀疏关键帧联合推理建立统一度量空间并获取Sim(3)先验；细阶段通过隔离静态背景对应关系净化稠密重建，采用闭式质心对齐锁定尺度和旋转并优化平移，辅以残差自检机制数学保证非退化。

Result: 在World Across Time数据集的11场景基准上，室外绝对轨迹误差降低44%，室内降低59%；配准速度提升6倍以上，生成高纯度三维变化图且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: VGGT-CD有效解决了跨时间三维配准中的尺度歧义、配准-变化悖论和飞点噪声问题，为无训练的三维变化检测提供了高效高精度的新范式。

Abstract: 3D change detection from multi-view images is essential for urban monitoring, disaster assessment, and autonomous driving. However, existing methods predominantly operate in the 2D domain, where viewpoint variations are mistaken for physical changes and depth is unavailable. While visual geometry foundation models like VGGT rapidly produce dense point clouds from unposed images, independent per-epoch reconstruction encounters fundamental obstacles: unpredictable inter-epoch scale ambiguity, registration-change paradox where scene changes corrupt alignment, and pervasive edge-flying noise. To address these challenges, we present VGGT-CD, a training-free pipeline decoupling cross-temporal registration from dynamic-change interference. In the Coarse Stage, sparse keyframe joint inference establishes a unified metric space and yields an initial Sim(3) prior. In the Fine Stage, dense reconstructions are purified by isolating static-background correspondences. A closed-form centroid alignment refines the translation while locking scale and rotation, using a residual self-check to mathematically guarantee non-degradation. Evaluated on an 11-scene benchmark from the World Across Time dataset, VGGT-CD reduces Absolute Trajectory Error by 44% outdoors and 59% indoors. It completes registration over 6 times faster, producing high-purity 3D change maps without task-specific training.

</details>


### [75] [Prefix-Adaptive Block Diffusion for Efficient Document Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16861)
*Mingxu Chai,Ziyu Shen,Chenyu Liu,Kaidi Zhang,Jiazheng Zhang,Dingwei Zhu,Zhiheng Xi,Ruoyu Chen,Jun Long,Jihua Kang,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PA-BDM通过前缀自适应因果去噪和动态KV缓存提交机制，解决了现有块扩散模型在文档解析中存在的块边界绑定、并行度受限和信息流不一致问题，在3B参数规模下实现了更高的识别分数和71.6%的推理吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散模型(BDMs)存在三个关键问题：(1) 块边界绑定——去噪和缓存提交固定在块边界上，块内去噪时并行度下降，生成的token需等待整个块完成后才能缓存；(2) 信息流不一致——块内双向去噪与块间自回归相互冲突，影响结构敏感识别任务。

Method: 提出PA-BDM，包含两个核心创新：(1) 用前缀到后缀的因果去噪替代块内双向去噪，将块大小视为最大候选范围而非固定提交单元；(2) Confidence-gated Structural Loss (CSL) 在低熵前缀上训练后扩展至更长续文；(3) Progressive Prefix Commitment (PPC) 在推理时动态将最长可靠前缀提交到KV缓存，并从更新后的前缀重置下一个候选范围，每步恢复大并行解码空间。

Result: 3B参数的PA-BDM在多个基准上取得更高的识别分数，相比2.5B的MinerU-Diffusion，推理吞吐量提升71.6%。

Conclusion: PA-BDM通过打破固定块边界和引入前缀自适应机制，有效解决了BDM在文档解析中的并行效率与信息流一致性问题，在提升识别质量的同时显著提高了推理吞吐量。

Abstract: Block Diffusion Models (BDMs) support parallel generation, flexible-length output, and KV caching, making them promising for efficient document parsing. However, existing BDMs bind denoising and cache commitment to fixed block boundaries: parallelism shrinks during intra-block denoising, while generated tokens cannot be cached until the whole block is completed. Moreover, intra-block bidirectional denoising conflicts with inter-block autoregression, creating inconsistent information flow that can challenge structure-sensitive recognition. We propose the Prefix-Adaptive Block Diffusion Model (PA-BDM), which replaces intra-block bidirectional denoising with causal denoising from prefix to suffix and treats the block size as a maximum candidate range rather than a fixed commitment unit. PA-BDM uses Confidence-gated Structural Loss (CSL) to build low-entropy prefixes before extending training to longer continuations. During inference, Progressive Prefix Commitment (PPC) then dynamically commits the longest reliable prefix into the KV cache and resets the next candidate range from the updated prefix, restoring a large parallel decoding space at each step. Experiments show that the 3B PA-BDM achieves higher recognition scores on several benchmarks and improves inference throughput by 71.6\% over the 2.5B MinerU-Diffusion.

</details>


### [76] [Metric-Guided Feature Fusion of Visual Foundation Models for Segmentation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16864)
*Yachan Guo,JoseLuis Gomez Zurita,Danna Xue,Yi Xiao,AntonioManuel Lopez Pena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无标签度量的引导方法，通过结构一致性和边缘保真度两类评估指标，从不同视觉基础模型（VFMs）中选择互补的特征对，并采用主-辅融合策略进行聚合，无需复杂架构改动即可在多个密集预测任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉基础模型在语义理解上表现优异，但在实例级密集预测任务中仍存在不足。不同VFMs在表征上存在不同偏置（如SAM2关注细粒度区域边界，DINOv3强调物体级结构），如何有效利用其互补特征提升下游任务性能尚未得到充分探索，且缺乏可解释的融合原则。

Method: 设计了一套无标签度量体系，从结构一致性（Structural Coherence）和边缘保真度（Edge Fidelity）两个维度评估VFM编码器的特征质量；基于评分识别互补的边缘强和结构强编码器对，并通过主-辅融合（master-auxiliary fusion）方案进行集成，该方案无需复杂架构改动，仅需单阶段训练。

Result: 在多个密集预测任务上相比基线方法取得一致的性能提升，体现在更好的物体级语义和更精确的边界定位。

Conclusion: 提出的度量引导的多VFM特征选择与融合方法有效且通用，无需复杂架构改动即可提升密集预测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Although large-scale visual foundation models (VFMs) achieve remarkable performance in semantic understanding, they still underperform in instance-aware dense prediction tasks. They exhibit different biases in representation: for instance, promptable segmentation models (e.g., SAM2) focus on fine-grained region boundaries, while self-supervised models (e.g., DINOv3) emphasize object-level structure. This observation highlights the potential of combining complementary features from different VFMs to enhance downstream dense prediction tasks. However, naive multi-VFM fusion seldom leads to reliable gains, and interpretable principles for leveraging their complementary features are still underexplored. In this work, we propose a metric-guided approach that effectively selects and aggregates complementary features from different VFMs based on explicit assessment scores. Specifically, we design a suite of label-free metrics in feature space across two aspects, Structural Coherence and Edge Fidelity, to assess features of VFM encoders. Guided by these scores, we identify complementary edge-strong and structure-strong encoder pairs, and integrate them via a master-auxiliary fusion scheme. This feature fusion requires no complex architectural changes and is trained only in a single stage. Our model shows consistent performance gains across multiple dense prediction tasks compared with the baselines, with better object-level semantics and more accurately localized boundaries. The code is available at {https://github.com/gyc-code/metric-guided-fusion}.

</details>


### [77] [Zero-Shot Faithful Textual Explanations via Directional-Derivative Influence on Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16877)
*Toshinori Yamauchi,Hiroshi Kera,Kazuhiko Kawamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaithTrace通过测量文本解释在分类器特征空间中沿文本诱导方向对类别logit的方向导数作为影响力分数，生成更忠实的零样本文本解释，并提出了相应的忠实度评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本文本解释方法往往遗漏真正驱动预测的特征，导致解释对模型决策背后证据的忠实度有限。

Method: 提出FaithTrace，通过计算类别logit沿文本诱导方向在分类器特征空间中的方向导数作为影响力分数，衡量解释诱导的表征对类别logit的改变程度，并将该分数扩展为定量评估指标。

Result: 实验表明FaithTrace生成的解释比基线方法更忠实，有助于更准确地理解模型。

Conclusion: FaithTrace通过直接度量解释对预测的影响来提升忠实度，并填补了文本解释忠实度评估的空白。

Abstract: Zero-shot textual explanations aim to make image classifiers more transparent by probing their internal representations, without relying on task-specific supervision or LVLMs. However, existing methods often miss the features that truly drive the prediction, resulting in limited \textit{faithfulness} to the evidence underlying the model's decision. To address this, we propose FaithTrace. Motivated by the idea that faithful explanations should describe concepts that strongly influence the prediction, FaithTrace directly measures how much the representation induced by the explanation changes the class logit. We introduce an influence score, computed as the directional derivative of the class logit along the text-induced direction in the classifier's feature space, and use it as a proxy for faithfulness. Moreover, we extend this influence score into quantitative evaluation metrics, helping fill the gap in faithfulness evaluation for textual explanations. Experiments show that FaithTrace yields more faithful explanations than baselines, facilitating a more accurate understanding of the model. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [78] [Towards Generalized Image Manipulation Localization via Score-based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16879)
*Yunfei Wang,Bo Du,Zhe Yang,Xin Liu,Zhiyu Lin,Tianxin Xu,Ji-Zhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffIML，一种基于分数生成式建模的图像篡改定位框架，通过学习评分函数而非决策边界来捕获掩码分布的几何拓扑结构，结合轻量级VAE、解耦架构、边缘监督和误差先验解决扩散模型效率与稳定性问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像篡改定位方法多为判别式，学习固定决策边界，容易过拟合训练集中的特定伪造痕迹，难以泛化到未知篡改类型。

Method: DiffIML采用分数生成式建模，通过学习评分函数（对数似然的梯度）替代直接估计硬决策边界，以捕获掩码分布的内在几何拓扑；利用扩散模型作为评分函数的数值求解器；通过轻量级掩码专用VAE实现快速潜空间处理，采用解耦架构与轻量级去噪UNet提升效率；引入边缘监督和误差先验缓解采样过程中的误差累积。

Result: 在八种非生成式基准和三种生成式基准上的两个不同协议实验中，DiffIML一致优于现有最优方法，在多种未见数据集上展现出显著的泛化性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于分数的生成式建模为图像篡改定位提供了超越传统判别式方法的新范式，有效提升了模型对未知篡改类型的泛化能力。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of synthetic media, Image Manipulation Localization (IML) has emerged as a critical component in multimedia forensics for ensuring the integrity of digital content. However, generalization remains a core challenge, as existing discriminative methods typically learn a fixed decision boundary that tends to overfit to specific training artifacts and fails to adapt to unseen manipulation types. To address this, we propose DiffIML, a novel framework that introduces score-based generative modeling to IML. Diverging from the direct estimation of hard boundaries, DiffIML approximates the score function, the gradient of the log-likelihood, to capture the intrinsic geometric topology of mask distributions. This paradigm leverages structural priors to iteratively recover coherent masks from noise, thereby circumventing the brittleness associated with discriminative models. Under this formulation, diffusion models serve as an effective numerical solver for the learned score function.To ensure practicality, we respectively resolve the efficiency and stability bottlenecks of standard diffusion by: (1) utilizing a Lightweight Mask-Specific VAE for fast latent-space process and a decoupled architecture with a lightweight denoising UNet, (2) edge supervision and error prior to mitigate error accumulation during sampling. Extensive experiments of two distinct protocols on eight non-generative and three generative benchmarks demonstrate that DiffIML consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding remarkable generalization improvements on diverse unseen datasets. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [79] [Controlling Decision Drift in Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16889)
*Chenglizhao Chen,Yuchen Cao,Xinyu Liu,Mengke Song,Guisheng Zhang,Xiaomin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态情感分析中模态缺失和质量不平衡问题，提出两层级参考对齐框架：第一层利用完整模态样本约束表征并对齐共享情感空间，第二层通过原型检索和投票抑制不可靠模态以增强决策一致性。在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上全模态输入时ACC达86.28%和85.88%，F1达86.24%和85.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析中，真实数据常出现模态缺失与质量不平衡问题。现有方法从可用模态生成缺失模态特征，但不同模态的表达机制和情感动态差异可能导致生成特征偏离真实分布、误导预测；同时，不可靠模态可能在融合中占主导，引起跨模态组合的表征偏移和不稳定情感表示。

Method: 提出两层级参考对齐框架（Two-level Reference Alignment Framework）。第一级：特征表示层参考对齐，利用完整模态样本约束表征，将不同模态组合对齐到共享情感空间；第二级：情感决策层参考对齐，通过原型检索和投票抑制不可靠模态，增强跨模态一致性。

Result: 在CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI上，多种模态缺失设置下均取得一致性提升。全模态输入时达到最优性能：CMU-MOSI上ACC 86.28%、F1 86.24%；CMU-MOSEI上ACC 85.88%、F1 85.86%。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不同缺失模态模式下能保持稳定可靠的情感预测，有效增强了多模态情感分析在模态缺失场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis relies on textual, acoustic, and visual signals, yet real-world data often suffer from modality missing and quality imbalance. Existing methods generate features for modality missing from available ones, but differences in expression mechanisms and sentiment dynamics across modalities may cause the generated features to deviate from true distributions and mislead prediction. In addition, unreliable modalities may dominate fusion, resulting in representation shift across modality combinations and unstable sentiment representations. To address these challenges, we propose a two-level reference alignment framework. The framework introduces stable references at the feature representation and sentiment decision levels to improve robustness under modality missing. First-level reference alignment leverages complete-modality samples to constrain representations and align different modality combinations into a shared sentiment space. Second-level reference alignment enforces cross-modal consistency at the decision level by suppressing unreliable modalities through prototype retrieval and voting. As a result, the framework maintains stable and reliable sentiment predictions under diverse missing-modality patterns. Experiments on CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI show consistent improvements across various missing-modality settings. Under full-modality input, the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ACC of 86.28% and 85.88%, and F1 of 86.24% and 85.86%.

</details>


### [80] [DriveSafe: A Framework for Risk Detection and Safety Suggestions in Driving Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16892)
*Sainithin Artham,Shankar Gangisetty,Avijit Dasgupta,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveSafe提出利用结构化自然语言描述（空间定位字幕+多模态上下文）进行风险感知场景理解，通过轻量适配器微调注入领域知识，在DRAMA基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本MLLM在细粒度、空间定位的风险评估任务上表现不如领域专用方法，需弥合这一差距。

Method: 首先生成包含运动、空间和深度线索的空间定位字幕；然后基于这些字幕进行下游风险评估，识别危险对象、位置及不安全行为，并给出安全建议；最后通过字幕-风险配对微调轻量适配器模块，将领域知识注入基础LLM。

Result: 在DRAMA基准上达到SOTA性能，显著优于零样本MLLM和先前的领域专用基线。

Conclusion: 显式基于语言场景表示的条件风险评估可有效提升自动驾驶场景理解的安全性。

Abstract: Comprehensive situational awareness is essential for autonomous vehicles operating in safety-critical environments, as it enables the identification and mitigation of potential risks. Although recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown promise on general vision-language tasks, our findings indicate that zero-shot MLLMs still underperform compared to domain-specific methods in fine-grained, spatially grounded risk assessment. To address this gap, we propose DriveSafe, a framework for risk-aware scene understanding that leverages structured natural language descriptions. Specifically, our method first generates spatially grounded captions enriched with multimodal context, including motion, spatial, and depth cues. These captions are then used for downstream risk assessment, explicitly identifying hazardous objects, their locations, and the unsafe behaviors they imply, followed by actionable safety suggestions. To further improve performance, we employ caption-risk pairings to fine-tune a lightweight adapter module, efficiently injecting domain-specific knowledge into the base LLM. By conditioning risk assessment on explicit language-based scene representations, DriveSafe achieves significant gains over both zero-shot MLLMs and prior domain-specific baselines. Exhaustive experiments on the DRAMA benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our key design choices. Project page: https://cvit.iiit.ac.in/ research/projects/cvit-projects/drivesafe

</details>


### [81] [LASAR: Towards Spatio-temporal Reasoning with Latent Cognitive Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16899)
*Jinzhou Tang,Sidi Liu,Waikit Xiu,Weixing Chen,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LASAR架构（双记忆系统）和ST-CRL对比学习目标，通过时空上下文表征学习构建智能体的内在认知地图，在VLN-CE和VSI-Bench基准上实现了2%-3.5%的零样本泛化提升，并证明了认知地图具有高自一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI方法（如基于动作的VLN和基于推理的EQA）缺乏迫使智能体编码细粒度空间关系（如拓扑或距离）的学习信号，导致无法验证智能体是否真正构建了空间结构的内部模型，而非仅仅模仿专家轨迹。

Method: 1) 提出LASAR架构，包含双记忆系统（情景记忆+语义认知地图）；2) 提出ST-CRL对比学习目标，利用仿真中带注释的时空上下文生成认知查询，构建样本对来训练架构，从而从智能体经验中形成内部认知地图。

Result: 在VLN-CE和VSI-Bench基准上，零样本泛化能力提升2%-3.5%；所提出的认知地图表现出高自一致性。

Conclusion: 通过ST-CRL训练的LASAR双记忆系统能够有效促使智能体编码细粒度空间关系，形成自一致的内部认知地图，在零样本泛化场景下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in embodied AI is verifying if agents build internal models of spatial structure or merely learn to mimic task-specific expert trajectories. This is critical as foundational approaches rooted in action-centric tasks (e.g., VLN) and reasoning-centric tasks (e.g., EQA) often share a common limitation: they lack a learning signal that forces them to encode fine-grained spatial relationships (like topology or distance) over long-range, fragmented experiences. To address this, we first propose LASAR, an architecture featuring a dual-memory system designed to maintain both episodic experiences and a semantic cognitive map. We then introduce Spatio-temporal Contextual Representation Learning (ST-CRL), a contrastive objective designed to train this architecture. ST-CRL leverages spatio-temporal cues from cognitive queries generated through annotated spatio-temporal context in simulation to build sample pairs, thereby forming the internal cognitive map from the agent's experiences. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves 2\%-3.5\% gains in both zero-shot generalization on standard VLN-CE and VSI-Bench benchmarks. We also demonstrate that our proposed cognitive map has high self-consistency.

</details>


### [82] [CAR-SAM: Cross-Attention Reconstruction for Post-Training Quantization of the Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16901)
*Houji Wen,Jiangyong Yu,Jun Li,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CAR-SAM统一量化框架，通过MatMul感知补偿（MAC）和联合交叉注意力重建（JCAR）两种策略，解决SAM在低比特量化下注意力消散和重建振荡问题，在4比特精度下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM模型计算和内存需求高，难以部署在资源受限设备上；现有PTQ方法未考虑SAM解码器中的交叉注意力架构，导致性能严重下降。

Method: 提出CAR-SAM框架：(1) MatMul-Aware Compensation (MAC)机制，将MatMul引起的激活量化误差转移到前序线性权重；(2) Joint Cross-Attention Reconstruction (JCAR)策略，联合重建耦合的注意力分支以抑制振荡并促进稳定收敛。

Result: 在4比特精度下，CAR-SAM在SAM-B和SAM-L上分别超越现有方法14.6%和6.6% mAP。

Conclusion: CAR-SAM能够稳健地将SAM模型量化至4比特精度，有效解决了低比特量化下的注意力消散和重建振荡两大挑战。

Abstract: Segment Anything Models (SAMs) are extensively used in computer vision for universal image segmentation, but deploying them on resource-constrained devices is challenging due to their high computational and memory demands. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) is a widely used technique for model compression and acceleration. However, existing PTQ methods fail to consider the cross-attention architecture in the SAM decoder. This degradation primarily stems from the unique challenges posed by SAMs: (1) Attention dissipation, where the attention information in the decoder, which is crucial for representing segmentation masks, collapses into a diffuse and non-semantic form under low-bit quantization; and (2) Reconstruction oscillation, where bidirectional coupling within the two-way transformer introduces cross-branch error interference and destabilizes convergence. To tackle these issues, we propose CAR-SAM, a unified quantization framework tailored for SAMs. Firstly, to mitigate attention dissipation, we introduce MatMul-Aware Compensation (MAC) mechanism that transfers activation-induced quantization errors from MatMul to preceding linear weights. Secondly, to mitigate oscillation in decoder optimization, we develop a Joint Cross-Attention Reconstruction (JCAR) strategy that jointly reconstructs coupled attention branches, suppressing oscillatory behavior and promoting stable convergence. Extensive experiments show that CAR-SAM robustly quantizes SAM models down to 4-bit precision, surpassing existing methods by 14.6% and 6.6% mAP on SAM-B and SAM-L respectively.

</details>


### [83] [WOW-Seg: A Word-free Open World Segmentation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16903)
*Danyang Li,Tianhao Wu,Bin Li,Zhenyuan Chen,Yang Zhang,Yuxuan Li,Ming-Ming Cheng,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WOW-Seg是一种无需文本提示的开放世界图像分割模型，通过Mask2Token视觉提示模块和级联注意力掩码机制，在仅使用八分之一参数量的情况下，在LVIS数据集上达到89.7语义相似度和82.4语义IoU，超越此前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统闭集分割方法难以适应开放世界场景，而SAM等基础分割模型存在强分割能力与弱语义理解之间的显著差距。为弥合这一差距，本文提出无需文本提示的开放世界分割模型WOW-Seg。

Method: 1) 提出Mask2Token视觉提示模块，将图像掩码转换为视觉token并与VLLM特征空间对齐；2) 引入级联注意力掩码（Cascade Attention Mask）解耦不同实例间的信息，减少实例间干扰；3) 构建包含7,662类别的Region Recognition Dataset (RR-7K) 开放世界区域识别基准。

Result: 在LVIS数据集上达到89.7语义相似度和82.4语义IoU，超越此前SOTA，且参数量仅为前者的八分之一。

Conclusion: WOW-Seg展现了强大的开放世界泛化能力，验证了视觉提示模块和级联注意力机制在开放世界分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Open world image segmentation aims to achieve precise segmentation and semantic understanding of targets within images by addressing the infinitely open set of object categories encountered in the real world. However, traditional closed-set segmentation approaches struggle to adapt to complex open world scenarios, while foundation segmentation models such as SAM exhibit notable discrepancies between their strong segmentation capabilities and relatively weaker semantic understanding. To bridge these discrepancies, we propose WOW-Seg, a Word-free Open World Segmentation model for segmenting and recognizing objects from open-set categories. Specifically, WOW-Seg introduces a novel visual prompt module, Mask2Token, which transforms image masks into visual tokens and ensures their alignment with the VLLM feature space. Moreover, we introduce the Cascade Attention Mask to decouple information across different instances. This approach mitigates inter-instance interference, leading to a significant improvement in model performance. We further construct an open world region recognition test benchmark: the Region Recognition Dataset (RR-7K). With 7,662 classes, it represents the most extensive category-rich region recognition dataset to date. WOW-Seg attains strong results on the LVIS dataset, achieving a semantic similarity of 89.7 and a semantic IoU of 82.4. This performance surpasses the previous SOTA while using only one-eighth the parameter count. These results underscore the strong open world generalization capabilities of WOW-Seg. The code and related resources are available at https://github.com/AAwcAA/WOW-Seg-Meta.

</details>


### [84] [VGGT-Occ: Geometry-Grounded and Density-Aware Gated Fusion for 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16911)
*Xun Chen,Tianchen Deng,Rui Wang,Fangjinhua Wang,Junyi Ma,Hongming Shen,Hesheng Wang,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-Occ通过在整个3D语义占用预测流程中嵌入几何令牌，特别是投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA）机制，结合视图质量语义门控和粗到细的渐进式解码器，在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语义占用预测方法中，2D到3D特征提升仅限制在初始投影阶段，后续的偏移学习、注意力加权和跨相机聚合等操作忽略了几何约束，缺乏物理一致性的引导。

Method: 提出VGGT-Occ框架：1）投影感知可变形注意力（PA-DA），将3D偏移投影回图像平面并利用投影雅可比矩阵作为加性偏差抑制不可靠观测；2）视图质量语义门控实现跨视图一致性；3）粗到细的渐进式解码器配合门控融合，低分辨率特征逐步细化为高分辨率，按信息密度分配计算量。

Result: 在SurroundOcc-nuScenes数据集上，T=1时达到33.00% IoU和21.08% mIoU，T=2时达到33.64% IoU和21.43% mIoU，占用头仅约41M可训练参数，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 在3D语义占用预测中嵌入几何令牌到整个流程是有效且高效的，方法在性能和计算成本之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires accurate 2D-to-3D feature lifting, yet current methods restrict camera geometry to initial projections. Subsequent operations like offset learning, attention weighting, and cross-camera aggregation remain geometry-agnostic, ignoring essential physical constraints. We propose VGGT-Occ, a framework that embeds geometric tokens throughout the entire pipeline. We introduce Projection-Aware Deformable Attention (PA-DA) to inject geometry into all attention stages. PA-DA projects 3D offsets back to image planes and leverages the projection Jacobian as an additive bias to suppress unreliable observations. Features are then integrated through a view-quality semantic gate for cross-view consistency. To optimize both efficiency and performance, we employ a sequential coarse-to-fine decoder with gated fusion, where low-resolution features are refined into higher resolutions, allocating computation by information density while substantially reducing decoder cost. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our approach. On SurroundOcc-nuScenes, VGGT-Occ achieves 33.00\% IoU and 21.08\% mIoU ($T{=}1$), and 33.64\% IoU and 21.43\% mIoU with $T{=}2$ inference, outperforming existing methods, with only ${\sim}41$M trainable parameters in the occupancy head. Code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [85] [HighSync: High-Quality Lip Synchronization via Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16918)
*Saeed Firouzi Daghigh,Majid Iranpour Mobarekeh,Mostafa Alavi,Mehdi Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HighSync是一个端到端的扩散模型框架，用于高保真唇部同步，可在512×512分辨率下生成本地与任意音频对齐的逼真说话人脸视频，通过识别并消除数据泄漏问题，在图像质量和同步精度上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唇部同步方法难以兼顾图像质量与同步精度，要么生成视觉退化的输出，要么产生时间不一致的唇部运动。此外，此前工作中存在一个未被识别的数据泄漏问题，削弱了模型对音频信号的真正依赖。

Method: 提出端到端的扩散框架HighSync，识别并系统性消除了先前工作中破坏时序建模的数据泄漏现象，使模型真正依赖于音频信号。该模型在原生512×512分辨率下运行。

Result: 在感知质量和同步精度两个维度的全面评估中，HighSync均达到SOTA性能，并且是首个在512×512原生分辨率下运行的唇部同步模型，适用于影视广播等专业制作环境。

Conclusion: HighSync通过解决数据泄漏问题，在图像质量与同步精度之间取得了更好的平衡，为专业级唇部同步应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We present HighSync, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework for high-fidelity lip synchronization that generates photorealistic talking-face videos aligned with arbitrary input audio. Existing approaches consistently struggle to reconcile image quality with synchronization accuracy, producing either visually degraded outputs or temporally inconsistent lip movements. HighSync addresses both challenges simultaneously and, to our knowledge, is the first lip sync model to operate natively at 512*512 resolution, positioning it as a viable solution for professional production environments such as the film and broadcast industries. Central to our approach is the identification and systematic elimination of a data leakage phenomenon that has silently undermined temporal modeling in prior work, preventing models from developing a genuine dependence on the audio signal. Comprehensive evaluations across both perceptual quality and synchronization accuracy metrics confirm that HighSync achieves state-of-the-art performance on both fronts. Source code, pre-trained models, and supplementary video results are publicly available at: https://github.com/saeed5959/high_sync

</details>


### [86] [Motion Cues from Image-based Point Tracking for LiDAR Scene Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16922)
*Youngdong Jang,Gyeongrok Oh,Jong Wook Kim,Hyunju Ryu,Hyung-gun Chi,SeungHyeon Kim,Seungryong Kim,Jonghyun Choi,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackCue利用点跟踪获取密集图像空间轨迹，通过视觉运动补偿和运动线索提升来解决LiDAR场景流估计中静态-动态分类噪声问题，显著提升自监督场景流估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督LiDAR场景流估计方法依赖稀疏几何观测进行静态-动态分类，易受数据稀疏性和遮挡影响，产生的噪声标签提供错误的运动引导，退化场景流学习效果。

Method: 1) 利用点跟踪获取LiDAR点锚定的密集图像空间轨迹；2) 视觉一致性运动补偿策略，在图像平面比较跟踪轨迹与自车诱导刚体轨迹，分离真实物体运动；3) 视觉运动线索提升，将自车补偿后的图像轨迹与LiDAR点关联，用于静态-动态标签精炼。

Result: TrackCue显著提升了动态标签的精度和F1分数，在自监督场景流估计中取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 引入跨模态运动线索（图像跟踪轨迹）可有效改善LiDAR场景流估计中静态-动态分类的准确性，为自监督学习提供更可靠的监督信号。

Abstract: LiDAR scene flow estimation is essential for autonomous driving, as it provides 3D motion for each point. Self-supervised approaches use static-dynamic classification to mitigate the imbalance between static and dynamic points, deriving targeted supervision. However, existing methods rely on sparse geometric observations for this classification, making them vulnerable to data sparsity and occlusions. The resulting noisy labels provide incorrect motion guidance and degrade scene flow learning. To address this, we introduce TrackCue, a tracking-guided framework for improving dynamic object representation in LiDAR scene flow estimation. In particular, TrackCue repurposes point tracking to obtain dense image-space trajectories anchored to LiDAR points, providing motion cues beyond sparse geometric observations. Furthermore, we present a visually consistent motion compensation strategy that compares the tracked trajectories with ego-induced rigid trajectories in the image plane, effectively isolating true object motion from ego-induced apparent motion. To transfer these isolated motion cues back to the LiDAR domain, we perform visual motion cue lifting, which associates ego-compensated image trajectories with LiDAR points for static-dynamic label refinement. As a result, TrackCue produces more accurate static-dynamic classification and provides more reliable supervision for scene flow learning. Experimental results show that TrackCue significantly improves the precision and F1 score of dynamic labels, leading to performance gains in self-supervised scene flow estimation.

</details>


### [87] [Neuroscience-inspired Staged Representation Learning with Disentangled Coarse- and Fine-Grained Semantics for EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16923)
*Xiang Gao,Hui Tian,Yanming Zhu,Xuefei Yin,Alan Wee-Chung Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种受神经科学启发的阶段式表示学习框架，将EEG视觉解码重构为阶段特定的表示分解问题，包含低层视觉、高层语义和融合表示三个互补阶段，并引入多模态双级语义学习机制和语义隐式通道，在THINGS-EEG基准上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法主要学习单一全局EEG嵌入进行跨模态对齐，忽视了人类视觉处理的分阶段、层次化特性。

Method: 提出阶段式表示学习框架，将EEG表示学习组织为低层视觉表示学习、高层语义表示学习和融合信息表示学习三个阶段；引入多模态双级语义学习机制，分离粗粒度标签级语义和细粒度图像级语义信息；引入语义隐式通道作为从视觉EEG信号中生成的计算表示通道，扩展通道级语义表示空间。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG基准上，该方法在依赖主体的零样本评估中取得优越性能，在独立主体的零样本评估中改进精确检索。层内检索、时间累积、多图像检索和消融实验进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模分阶段的感知、语义和融合表示为基于EEG的视觉解码提供了一种有效的受神经科学启发的框架。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from electroencephalography (EEG) signals remains a fundamental challenge in brain-computer interfaces and medical rehabilitation. Existing EEG visual decoding methods mainly focus on learning a single global EEG embedding for cross-modal alignment, but they largely overlook the staged and hierarchical characteristics of human visual processing. To address this limitation, we propose a neuroscience-inspired staged representation learning framework that reformulates EEG visual decoding as a stage-specific representation decomposition problem. The proposed framework organizes EEG representation learning into three complementary phases: low-level visual representation learning, high-level semantic representation learning, and integrative information fusion. To strengthen semantic modeling, we further introduce a multimodal dual-level semantic learning mechanism that separates coarse label-level semantics from fine image-level visual-semantic information. In addition, semantic latent channels are introduced as computational representation channels generated from observed visual EEG signals, expanding the channel-level semantic representation space for structured semantic abstraction and cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments on the THINGS-EEG benchmark demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance under subject-dependent zero-shot evaluation and improved exact retrieval under subject-independent zero-shot evaluation. Additional analyses, including layer-wise retrieval, temporal accumulation, expanded multi-image retrieval, and ablation studies, further support the effectiveness of staged decomposition and structured semantic modeling. These results suggest that explicitly modeling staged perceptual, semantic, and integrative representations provides an effective neuroscience-inspired framework for EEG-based visual decoding.

</details>


### [88] [P2GS: Physical Prior-guided Gaussian Splatting for Photometrically Consistent Urban Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16925)
*Kota Shimomura,Hidehisa Arai,Tsubasa Takahashi,Takayoshi Yamashita,Hironobu Fujiyoshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2GS提出了一种物理一致的3D高斯泼溅框架，通过联合分解视角无关的线性HDR辐射场、每视角曝光参数和色调映射函数，仅从LDR图像中实现跨视角光度一致性，解决了自动驾驶中多相机曝光不一致和动态光照导致的伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3DGS隐式假设各视角间曝光和色调映射一致，而真实驾驶数据因异构相机流水线和动态户外光照违反此假设，导致辐射场中嵌入曝光差异和传感器噪声，在静态背景上产生伪影和不一致光照，影响闭环模拟器和感知模型的可靠性。

Method: 提出P2GS框架，基于物理成像过程联合优化：1）视角无关的线性HDR辐射场；2）每视角曝光缩放因子；3）色调映射函数。仅需LDR图像，无需HDR监督，通过相对曝光一致性约束和HDR域辐射正则化实现统一优化。

Result: 在真实和模拟驾驶场景中，P2GS在LDR重建质量上匹配或超越先前方法，同时在光度一致性、曝光归一化和物理连贯光照方面显著提升。

Conclusion: P2GS有效解决了自动驾驶中跨视角光度不一致问题，在保持标准3DGS实时渲染效率的同时，获得了鲁棒于相机间光照差异的辐射场，为闭环模拟和高保真感知提供了更可靠的场景表示。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently emerged as a powerful explicit representation enabling fast, high-fidelity rendering, making it a promising foundation for closed-loop simulators and perception models in autonomous driving. However, conventional 3DGS implicitly assumes consistent exposure and tone mapping across views. Real driving data violates this assumption due to heterogeneous camera pipelines and dynamic outdoor illumination, baking exposure discrepancies and sensor noise into the radiance field and producing artifacts and inconsistent illumination especially in static backgrounds crucial for realistic simulation. These issues are amplified in autonomous driving, where sparse viewpoints, varying exposures, and outdoor lighting interact, while prior work mainly targets dynamic-object reconstruction and overlooks cross-view photometric consistency. To address this limitation, we introduce P2GS, a physically consistent Gaussian Splatting framework that jointly decomposes a view-invariant linear HDR radiance field, per-view exposure scales, and tone-mapping functions from only LDR images without HDR supervision. P2GS employs a unified optimization strategy grounded in the physical image-formation process, enforcing relative-exposure consistency and HDR-domain radiance regularization. This yields a radiance field robust to inter-camera illumination differences while preserving the real-time efficiency of standard 3DGS. Experiments across real and simulated driving environments show that P2GS matches or surpasses prior methods in LDR reconstruction while providing substantially improved photometric consistency, reliable exposure normalization, and physically coherent illumination across diverse scenes.

</details>


### [89] [DEVIS-GRPO: Unleashing GRPO on Dynamic Extreme View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16937)
*Yi Zuo,Huimin Wu,Lingling Li,Fang Liu,Licheng Jiao,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DEVIS-GRPO框架，通过GRPO在线策略梯度方法和ADEVIS渐进累加采样策略，实现大视角轨迹可控的视频生成，无需昂贵的成对视频数据，在多个数据集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹可控视频生成方法在小视角相机运动下表现良好，但在大视角运动下性能严重下降；而现有的极端视角合成方法需要专门的成对视频数据，标注成本高昂。

Method: 提出DEVIS-GRPO框架，包含两个核心创新：1) ADEVIS采样策略，通过渐进累加小视角增量实现大视角相机运动，无需预热策略模型收集昂贵的成对大视角视频；2) 多层级一致性-质量奖励函数，用于选择高质量样本进行模型优化。

Result: 在Kubric-4D数据集上，非遮挡区域PSNR提升21.57%、SSIM提升7.31%；在iPhone数据集上，LPIPS降低18.56%。

Conclusion: DEVIS-GRPO是首个用于极端视角视频生成的在线策略梯度方法，通过ADEVIS策略和奖励函数设计，有效解决了大视角运动下的视频生成问题，提升了训练效率和采样多样性。

Abstract: Trajectory-controlled video generation has become essential for controllable video generation. While current methods perform well under small-view camera motions, they degrade significantly with large-view motions. Existing solutions for extreme-view synthesis typically require dedicated video pairs, demanding substantial annotation effort. To address these limitations, we propose Dynamic Extreme VIew Synthesis-GRPO (DEVIS-GRPO), a GRPO-based framework for trajectory-controlled video generation, the first online policy gradient method for extreme view video generation. Central to our approach is a novel sampling strategy: Accumulative Dynamic Extreme VIew Synthesis (ADEVIS), which achieves large-view camera motions by progressively accumulating small-view increments. This method delivers two key advantages: 1) enhanced training efficiency, as it eliminates the need to warm-start the policy model by collecting expensive paired large-view videos, and 2) increased sampling diversity, achieved by flexibly varying trajectory configurations. Finally, we designed a multi-level consistency-quality reward function to select high-quality samples for model optimization. Experiments on the Kubric-4D, iPhone, and DL3DV datasets demonstrate our method's superiority. On Kubric-4D, we achieve relative improvements of 21.57% in PSNR and 7.31% in SSIM over the second-best method in non-occlusion areas. On iPhone, LPIPS is reduced by 18.56%.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Point-Wise Matching: Structural Representation Alignment for Accelerating Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16949)
*Shaodong Xu,Zhendong Wang,Litong Gong,Zexian Li,Wengang Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: sREPA提出一种结构性的表示对齐框架，通过约束特征图之间的空间关系几何而非逐点匹配，从而更有效地将预训练视觉模型中的空间拓扑结构迁移到扩散Transformer中，实现更快的收敛和更优的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法（如REPA、iREPA）多采用逐点匹配目标或隐式架构调整，无法显式建模预训练视觉表征中蕴含的空间关系几何，导致对空间拓扑结构的捕捉不充分。

Method: 提出sREPA（结构式表示对齐框架），通过强制特征图之间在关系几何上的一致性原则，而非仅匹配单个特征点，来约束模型学习预训练特征的完整空间布局和结构相关性。

Result: 相比最先进的对齐策略，sREPA实现了更快、更稳定的收敛，并提升了生成样本质量。

Conclusion: 将表示对齐从逐点匹配升级为结构性约束，能更有效地捕捉视觉表征中的空间拓扑信息，从而显著提升扩散Transformer的训练效率与生成效果。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) demonstrate that aligning noisy latent states with well-trained semantic features-as pioneered by Representation Alignment (REPA)-can substantially accelerate training and improve generation fidelity. Subsequent analysis(e.g., iREPA) suggests that these gains arise primarily from transferring spatial structure contained in pre-trained vision representations. However, mostly existing alignment methods employ point-wise matching objectives or rely on implicit architectural tweaks, which fail to explicitly model the spatial relational geometry inherent in vision foundation models. We argue that such element-wise supervision is insufficient to capture the rich spatial topology of visual representations, and that effective alignment for generation should instead be formulated as an explicit structural constraint. To this end, we propose sREPA, a structural REPresentation Alignment framework to enforce consistency in the relational geometry of feature maps, rather than merely matching individual feature points. By encouraging the model to internalize holistic spatial layouts and structural correlations from pre-trained features, sREPA achieves faster and more stable convergence, along with improved sample quality, compared to state-of-the-art alignment strategies. Our code and models will be released.

</details>


### [91] [Edit-GRPO: A Locality-Preserving Policy Optimization Framework for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16951)
*Shaodong Xu,Zexian Li,Zhendong Wang,Litong Gong,Tiezheng Ge,Wengang Zhou,Bo Zheng,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Edit-GRPO，一种通过将优化信号按空间区域解耦来保持图像编辑局部性的策略优化框架，在提升编辑效果的同时避免非编辑区域的上下文失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的图像编辑方法将图像视为整体，采用全局策略更新，导致编辑意图的局部性与全局优化信号之间的不匹配，引发上下文失真和边界不一致等伪影。

Method: 提出Edit-GRPO框架，显式解耦编辑目标和保留目标，为编辑区域和非编辑区域分配区域特定的优化信号，使策略更新与编辑任务的空间结构对齐。

Result: 在多种图像编辑场景中，Edit-GRPO在保持强编辑性能的同时，显著提升了局部性保持能力，减少了上下文失真和边界不一致等伪影。

Conclusion: Edit-GRPO通过空间解耦的优化策略有效解决了编辑局部性问题，验证了该框架的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in image editing lies in preserving spatial locality: edits should improve targeted content without inadvertently altering surrounding regions. However, most optimization-based editing approaches treat images as holistic entities, causing global policy updates that undermine locality and introduce undesired context changes. We observe that this issue stems from a mismatch between localized editing intent and globally applied optimization signals. Motivated by this insight, we propose Edit-GRPO, preserving Locality while optimizing image editing, a locality-preserving policy optimization framework that explicitly decouples editing and preservation objectives. By assigning region-specific optimization signals to edit and non-edit areas, Edit-GRPO aligns policy updates with the spatial structure of editing tasks, enabling localized improvements while maintaining global visual coherence. This design effectively suppresses common artifacts such as context distortion and boundary inconsistency. Extensive experiments across diverse image editing scenarios demonstrate that Edit-GRPO significantly improves locality preservation while maintaining strong editing performance compared to existing optimization-based methods, validating the generality and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [92] [Latent Action Control for Reasoning-Guided Unified Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16961)
*Fuxiang Zhai,Sixiang Chen,Yingjin Li,Shuaibo Li,Jianyu Lai,Tengjun Huang,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在动作控制（LAC），通过在统一生成器的隐藏流中注入可学习的连续动作轨迹，将推理能力转化为对图像生成过程的直接控制，显著提升组合式与知识驱动生成的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型虽能同时编码视觉理解与图像生成，但理解并不自动转化为控制能力：模型可以推断对象、关系或知识线索，却无法在生成的图像中实例化这些信息。

Method: LAC将推理表示为生成器内部隐藏连续动作，通过角色结构化的潜在轨迹进行规划、内部视觉预览、诊断和优化；利用先验引导的变分潜在动作对齐（从训练时渲染的语义先验、草稿图像特征和监督停止信号学习）以及Latent-Flow GRPO将潜在到图像的展开与终端视觉反馈对齐。

Result: 在BAGEL-7B-MoT上实例化后，LAC在GenEval、WISE和T2I-CompBench上持续改进了组合式和知识驱动生成，在空间关系、属性绑定和世界知识敏感提示上提升最大。

Conclusion: 消融研究和潜在干预实验表明，学习到的动作轨迹被生成器所消耗，说明统一生成中理解不仅需要被编码，更需要在生成过程中变得可操作。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models can encode visual understanding and image generation within a shared backbone, yet understanding does not automatically translate into control: models may infer objects, relations, or knowledge cues but fail to instantiate them in the generated image. We propose Latent Action Control (LAC), which makes reasoning actionable by representing it as hidden continuous actions inside a unified generator. Given a prompt, LAC rolls out a role-structured latent trajectory for planning, internal visual drafting, diagnosis, and refinement, and injects these actions into the hidden stream that conditions flow-based generation, without producing reasoning tokens or intermediate images. Since such action trajectories are unobserved, LAC learns them through prior-guided variational latent action alignment from training-only rendered semantic priors, draft image features, and supervised halting signals, followed by Latent-Flow GRPO to align the latent-to-image rollout with terminal visual feedback. This provides a control path from inferred relations, bindings, and knowledge cues to the generation process. Instantiated on BAGEL-7B-MoT, LAC consistently improves compositional and knowledge-grounded generation across GenEval, WISE, and T2I-CompBench, with the largest gains on spatial relations, attribute binding, and world-knowledge-sensitive prompts. Ablations and latent interventions show that the learned action trajectory is consumed by the generator, suggesting that unified generation benefits when understanding is not only encoded, but made actionable during generation.

</details>


### [93] [OmniVL-Guard Pro: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Omnibus Vision-Language Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16962)
*Jinjie Shen,Zheng Huang,Yuchen Zhang,Yujiao Wu,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Shengeng Tang,Tianrui Hui,Nan Pu,Zhun Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniVL-Guard Pro，一种工具增强的智能体框架，将视觉语言伪造检测与定位从封闭世界预测扩展到开放世界线索驱动推理。通过集成多种工具（实时事件搜索、局部裁剪缩放、边缘异常筛查、人脸检测、视频帧提取、SAM3分割）、树结构自演化工具轨迹生成（FSTR数据集）以及检查器引导的智能体强化学习（CGARL），在多项任务上达到SOTA性能并展现出强零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言伪造检测与定位方法基于封闭世界范式，依赖模型自身完成验证。但自包含的MLLMs受限于有限参数知识、静态训练语料和有限感知分辨率，在动态开放世界取证中存在实际瓶颈，尤其需要外部线索和细粒度局部操作审查的实时事件验证与伪造分割任务。

Method: 1) OmniVL-Guard Pro 工具增强智能体框架：集成实时事件搜索、局部裁剪缩放、边缘异常筛查、人脸检测、视频帧提取、SAM3分割等多工具环境。2) 树结构自演化工具轨迹生成（Tree-Structured Self-Evolving Tool Trajectory Generation）：通过种子引导、无引导者自演化和弱提示难样本合成生成多样化轨迹，构建全谱工具推理（FSTR）数据集。3) 检查器引导的智能体强化学习（CGARL）：提供过程级监督，惩罚答案正确但推理扭曲的情况。

Result: 在多个任务上达到SOTA性能，展现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 从扩展自包含模型转向借助工具超越模型自身限制，是解决开放世界取证问题的有效方向。FSTR数据集和代码将公开。

Abstract: Existing vision-language forgery detection and grounding methods operate under a closed-world paradigm, assuming verification can be completed by the model alone. However, self-contained MLLMs are constrained by finite parametric knowledge, static training corpora, and limited perceptual resolution, creating a practical ceiling in dynamic open-world forensics -- particularly for real-time event verification requiring external clues and forgery segmentation demanding fine-grained scrutiny of local manipulations. To address these limitations, we shift from scaling up the self-contained model toward reaching beyond it. We propose \textbf{OmniVL-Guard Pro}, a tool-augmented agent that extends unified forensics from closed-world prediction to open-world clues-driven reasoning. OmniVL-Guard Pro integrates a tool environment spanning real-time event search, local cropping and zooming, edge-anomaly screening, face detection, video frame extraction, and SAM3-based segmentation. To generate high-quality tool-reasoning trajectories, we introduce \textbf{Tree-Structured Self-Evolving Tool Trajectory Generation}, which produces diverse trajectories through seed guidance, guider-free self-evolution, and weakly-hinted hard sample synthesis, yielding the Full-Spectrum Tool Reasoning (FSTR) dataset for training. We further propose \textbf{Checker-Guided Agentic Reinforcement Learning} (CGARL), which provides process-level supervision to penalize cases where the answer is correct but the reasoning is distorted. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniVL-Guard Pro achieves state-of-the-art performance across various tasks, and exhibits strong zero-shot generalization. The FSTR dataset and code for OmniVL-Guard Pro will be publicly released at \url{https://github.com/shen8424/OmniVL-Guard-Pro}.

</details>


### [94] [SHED: Style-Homogenized Embedding Alignment for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16973)
*Kai Gan,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHED是一种基于CLIP的域泛化方法，通过对齐风格同质化嵌入（去除域特定风格中心）而非原始表示，来解决图像与文本嵌入之间的信息不对称问题，在五个基准测试上达到SOTA，例如在DomainNet上比标准微调提升4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型直接将图像与文本嵌入对齐存在固有信息不对称：图像编码包含类别语义和域特定风格，而文本提示仅提供基本类别线索，这种不对称阻碍了模型在未见域上的泛化能力。

Method: 训练时，SHED从每源域计算的图像嵌入中移除域特定风格中心，并从多样化模板平均后的文本嵌入中移除全局中心；推理时，将多样化文本域中心投影到视觉空间，通过隶属度加权聚合预测。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，显著优于先前方法（例如在DomainNet上比标准微调提升4.0%）。

Conclusion: 通过对齐风格同质化嵌入而非原始表示，SHED有效解决了CLIP中的信息不对称问题，显著提升了域泛化性能。

Abstract: Domain generalization aims to enhance model robustness against unseen domains with embedding distribution shifts. While large-scale vision-language models like CLIP exhibit strong generalization, their direct image-text embedding alignment suffers from inherent information asymmetry: images encode both class semantics and domain-specific styles, whereas text prompts primarily convey basic class cues. This asymmetry hinders generalization to novel domains in realistic scenarios. To address this, we propose Style-Homogenized Embedding alignment for Domain-generalization (SHED), a novel CLIP-based method that aligns style-homogenized embeddings instead of raw representations from encoders in CLIP. During training, SHED removes domain-specific style centroids from both image embeddings computed per source domains and text embeddings which are averaged across diverse prompt templates and stripped of a global centroid. For inference, considering the lack of target domain information, SHED projects diverse textual domain centroids into the visual space and aggregates predictions via membership weighting. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks show SHED achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming prior methods significantly (e.g., +4.0\% on DomainNet vs. standard fine-tuning).

</details>


### [95] [Statistical Hand Shape Modeling from Clinical CT Scans Using Deep Learning and Implicit Skinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16980)
*Gokce Guven,Hasan Fehmi Ates,Deniz Karasahin,Kaan Erdogan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI辅助的手部解剖重建流水线，利用Pix2Pix-GAN去除CT图像中的石膏和背景伪影，结合3D Slicer提取皮肤和骨骼掩膜，通过隐式蒙皮和非刚性配准（GBCPD++）建立跨被试的密集对应关系，最后用PCA量化解剖形状变异性，在1271例肘到手CT扫描上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部解剖的精确分割和统计形状建模对医学诊断、人机工效学和生物力学具有重要意义，但现有方法在处理含石膏和背景伪影的CT数据时存在困难，需要一种自动化的预处理和重建流水线。

Method: 1) 使用Pix2Pix条件GAN去除CT体积中的石膏和背景伪影；2) 在3D Slicer中提取皮肤和骨骼掩膜，转换为封闭曲面网格模型；3) 利用分割的骨骼网格构建骨骼表示，通过隐式蒙皮将所有手部模型对齐到标准化解剖配置；4) 采用基于测地线的相干点漂移算法（GBCPD++）进行非刚性配准建立逐点对应；5) 对配准后的模型进行PCA分析以量化形状变异性。

Result: Pix2Pix预处理阶段在测试集上达到Dice系数0.9856和IoU 0.9720；对90例手指完全可见且解剖分离的扫描进行统计建模，所得形状分布与美国陆军人体测量调查（ANSUR II）高度一致。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在生物力学建模、人机工效学优化、假肢设计和精准医疗诊断方面的显著潜力，重建模型的解剖有效性得到验证。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation and statistical shape modeling of hand anatomy have significant implications for medical diagnostics, ergonomics, and biomechanics. This study proposes an AI-assisted reconstruction pipeline for segmenting and analyzing hand anatomy from 1,271 elbow-to-hand (e2h-CT) computed tomography scans. A Pix2Pix-based conditional generative adversarial network is first employed to remove plaster cast and background artifacts from CT volumes. The cleaned scans are then processed in 3D Slicer to extract skin and bone masks, which are converted into closed-surface mesh models. Segmented bone meshes are used to construct skeletal representations, enabling implicit skinning to align all hand models into a standardized anatomical configuration. Subsequently, non-rigid registration is performed on the hand skin surfaces using the Geodesic Based Coherent Point Drift++ (GBCPD++) algorithm to establish point-wise correspondence across subjects. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is then applied to the registered models to quantify anatomical shape variability. The Pix2Pix preprocessing stage achieved a Dice coefficient of 0.9856 and an IoU of 0.9720 on the held-out test set. Statistical modeling was performed on a subset of 90 scans in which the fingers were fully visible and anatomically separated. The resulting statistical shape distributions demonstrate strong agreement with the U.S. Army Anthropometric Survey (ANSUR II), supporting the anatomical validity of the reconstructed models. The proposed methodology demonstrates significant potential for advancing biomechanical modeling, ergonomic optimization, prosthetic design, and precision medical diagnostics.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [The Scaling Laws of Skills in LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16508)
*Charles Chen,Qiming Yu,Yuhang Gu,Zhuoye Huang,Hanjing Li,Hongyu Liu,Simin Liu,Jinhao Liu,Dengyun Peng,Jiangyi Wang,Zheng Yan,Fanqing Meng,Ethan Qin,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了智能体技能库的规模定律：路由精度随库规模对数衰减，执行正确性可提升下游决策约4倍，且路由衰减斜率耦合了这两条定律。基于此的优化将路由准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，劫持率从22.4%降至4.1%，并改善了执行基准测试的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统扩展时，技能库规模日益增大，但其缩放定律尚未被充分理解，需要系统性地研究技能库规模对路由和执行性能的影响。

Method: 基于15个前沿大语言模型、1,141个真实世界技能和超过300万次路由或执行决策，通过大规模实验拟合路由精度与库规模的对数衰减关系，分析执行定律的乘性效应，并验证路由衰减斜率对两条定律的耦合作用。

Result: 路由精度随库规模对数衰减（R²>0.97），错误类型从局部竞争演进到跨族漂移和"黑洞技能"捕获；正确执行可提升困难下游决策约4倍；路由衰减斜率b耦合两条定律；优化后路由准确率从71.3%提升至91.7%，劫持率从22.4%降至4.1%，ClawBench通过率从49.3%提升至61.6%，ClawMark从28.4%提升至34.5%。

Conclusion: 智能体性能不仅取决于模型能力，也显著受技能库的结构、粒度和暴露策略影响，所发现的定律具有可操作性和跨场景迁移性。

Abstract: As agent systems scale, skills accumulate into large reusable libraries, yet their scaling laws remain poorly understood. Across 15 frontier LLMs, 1,141 real-world skills, and over 3M routing or execution decisions, we identify two coupled laws. Routing law: single-step routing accuracy decays logarithmically with library size ($R^2{>}0.97$ for all models), with errors progressing from local skill competition to cross-family drift and capture by overly general "black-hole skills". Execution law: before state realization, joint routing is approximately multiplicative, whereas correct execution can improve difficult downstream decisions by about $4{\times}$. A single parameter, the routing logarithmic decay slope $b$, couples the two laws: routing-side fits predict execution-side rescue across models, showing that the same library property controls both pre-execution collapse and downstream recoverability. The laws are actionable: law-guided optimization raises held-out routing accuracy from 71.3% to 91.7%, reduces hijack from 22.4% to 4.1%, and transfers directionally to downstream ClawBench and ClawMark execution settings, improving mean pass rate from 49.3% to 61.6% on ClawBench and from 28.4% to 34.5% on ClawMark. These results show that agent performance depends not only on model capability, but also on the structure, granularity, and exposure policy of the skill library.

</details>


### [97] [Scaling Accessible Mathematics on arXiv: HTML Conversion and MathML 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16562)
*Deyan Ginev,Brian Caruso,Bruce Miller,Jeff Sank,Jacob Weiskoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: arXiv的HTML论文项目自2023年上线以来持续发展，2025-2026年主要进展包括：社区驱动的HTML保真度改进（处理约半数6000份用户报告）、语料库级转换达到75%无错误HTML（目标90%）、初步支持MathML 4意图注释以实现无障碍语音输出、正在进行的LaTeXML Rust移植以降低计算成本并加快预览速度。该项目仍处于实验阶段，但正逐步成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升arXiv上TeX/LaTeX提交论文的HTML呈现质量，改善读者阅读体验，降低计算成本，并支持无障碍访问（语音输出）。

Method: 社区驱动的错误报告与修复机制、语料库规模的大批量HTML转换、MathML 4意图注释集成、将LaTeXML用Rust语言重写以优化性能。

Result: 解决了约3000份用户报告；HTML无错误率从0%提升至75%（目标90%）；初步实现MathML 4意图注释；Rust移植正在进行中，已展现计算成本降低和预览速度提升的效果。

Conclusion: arXiv HTML论文项目虽仍为实验性质，但通过社区反馈、技术标准采用和编程语言优化，正逐步成熟，更好地服务读者需求。

Abstract: We report on the ongoing development of arXiv's HTML Papers offering, available on every new TeX/LaTeX submission since its initial release in 2023.
  The main highlights from 2025 and early 2026 are:
  (i) community-driven improvements to HTML fidelity and service health, with roughly half of 6,000 user reports resolved;
  (ii) corpus-scale conversion work aimed at 90% error-free HTML (currently 75%);
  (iii) initial MathML 4 Intent annotations for accessible speech output;
  (iv) an in-progress Rust port of LaTeXML, reducing compute costs and enabling faster previews on submission.
  The arXiv HTML Papers project remains experimental, but is gradually maturing as we better understand the needs of arXiv's readers and the technical opportunities presented by new standards and by advances in programming languages and AI.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Sentiment Classification: A Generative Framework for Emotion Intensity Evaluation in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16613)
*Francesco A. Fabozzi,Dasol Kim,William N. Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从情感识别转向情感强度评估的新方法，构建了情感强度评分数据集，微调生成式语言模型输出0-100的连续值，在金融等领域展现出优于分类基线的表达力和泛化能力，并揭示了向情感和唤醒度等相关构念的迁移效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散情感分类在金融等应用领域存在局限性，无法捕捉情感的程度差异，而这对于决策至关重要。作者希望将NLP从情感分类重新定位为情感强度评估，以更好地服务于需要量化情感程度的领域。

Method: 构建情感强度评分数据集，对开放权重的生成式语言模型进行微调，使其输出0-100范围内的连续数值，实现从分类任务到回归/评估任务的转换。

Result: 该方法在性能上超越了情感分类基线，展现出令人惊讶的泛化能力，并且向情感（sentiment）和唤醒度（arousal）等相关构念存在迁移效应。

Conclusion: 情感强度评估作为分类的替代方案，更符合金融等领域对情感程度进行解读和决策的实际需求，为NLP的跨学科重构做出了贡献。

Abstract: We introduce a novel approach to emotion modeling that shifts the focus from
  identification to evaluation, addressing the limitations of discrete classification in
  applied domains such as finance. By constructing a dataset of emotional intensity
  scores and fine-tuning open-weight generative language models to output continuous
  values from 0-100, we demonstrate a more expressive, generalizable framework for
  sentiment and emotion analysis. Our findings not only outperform classification
  baselines but also reveal surprising generalization capabilities and transfer effects
  to related constructs such as sentiment and arousal. This work contributes to the
  interdisciplinary recontextualization of NLP by introducing emotion intensity
  evaluation as an alternative to classification, arguing that this shift better aligns
  with the needs of domains--such as finance--where the degree of emotional content is
  central to interpretation and decision-making.

</details>


### [99] [SKG-Eval: Stateful Evaluation of Multi-Turn Dialogue via Incremental Semantic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16650)
*Avijit Shil,Suman Samui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SKG-Eval 提出了一种基于语义知识图谱的准确定性且可解释的多轮对话评估框架，通过增量构建实体-关系-承诺图并计算局部相关性、历史一致性和逻辑连贯性三个信号，在多个基准上实现了与人类判断更高的相关性，并能有效检测长程矛盾与话题漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估器（包括 LLM-as-a-judge 和基于嵌入的指标）大多依赖扁平化或单轮孤立的表示方式，难以有效检测多轮对话中的长程问题（如矛盾、话题漂移和实体不一致），亟需一种能显式建模对话状态演变的评估框架。

Method: SKG-Eval 将对话建模为随时间演进的语义知识图谱，通过结构化三元组提取增量更新图结构，并计算三个互补信号：(i) 局部相关性（与当前提示和对齐）；(ii) 历史一致性（基于图和嵌入信号评估新信息与历史上下文连接程度）；(iii) 逻辑连贯性（通过几何矛盾引擎检测跨轮冲突，不依赖 NLI 模型或 LLM 裁判）。最后通过适应性融合和基于近因加权的趋势分析聚合为长度不变的会话分数。

Result: 在多个基准上，SKG-Eval 与人类判断的相关性更高，显著提升了对长程不一致性的检测能力，并能生成显式的矛盾证明和确定性评分，实现可重复和可审计的评估。

Conclusion: 通过语义知识图谱进行结构化的外部状态追踪，为基于隐式推理的 LLM 对话评估器提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Evaluating multi-turn dialogue systems remains challenging because response quality depends not only on the current prompt, but also on previously established entities, claims, and conversational commitments. Existing automatic evaluators, including LLM-as-a-judge frameworks and embedding-based metrics, largely rely on flat or turn-isolated representations, making them less effective at detecting long-range issues such as contradiction, topic drift, and entity inconsistency. To address this, we propose SKG-Eval, a quasi-deterministic and interpretable framework that models dialogue as an evolving Semantic Knowledge Graph (SKG) of entities, relations, and commitments across turns. The framework incrementally updates the graph through structured triple extraction and computes three complementary signals: (i) local relevance, measuring alignment with the current prompt and optional reference; (ii) historical consistency, evaluating how newly introduced information connects to prior conversational context using graph-based and embedding-driven signals; and (iii) logical coherence, assessed by a geometric contradiction engine that detects cross-turn conflicts without relying on NLI models or LLM judges. These signals are adaptively fused and aggregated into a length-invariant session score via recency-weighted trend analysis. Across multiple benchmarks, SKG-Eval achieves higher correlation with human judgments and substantially improves detection of long-range inconsistencies in extended conversations. In addition, the framework produces explicit contradiction certificates and deterministic scores for fixed inputs, enabling reproducible and auditable evaluation. Overall, our results suggest that structured externalized state tracking through semantic knowledge graphs provides a scalable alternative to implicit reasoning in LLM-based dialogue evaluators.

</details>


### [100] [A Scalable Tool for Measuring Manner and Result Verbs in Developmental Language Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16654)
*Divyesh Pratap Singh,Dakshesh Gusain,Federica Bulgarelli,Alison Eisel Hendricks,John Beavers,Nathan M. Beers,Ifeoma Nwogu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型生成句子级标注、训练RoBERTa分类器的方法，用于大规模识别语境中的方式动词和结果动词，在三组黄金标准数据集上平均准确率达89.6%，为发展语言学等领域提供了可扩展的动词语义测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方式动词和结果动词在事件结构编码上存在差异，对早期动词习得研究具有潜在价值，但缺乏大规模标注资源来支持该区分的可量化测量。

Method: 利用语言学启发的提示词，通过大语言模型在MASC和InterCorp数据上生成句子级标注，覆盖VerbNet中436个类别；基于这些标注训练RoBERTa分类器，并在三组黄金标准数据集上评估。

Result: 在三组独立黄金标准数据集上，模型表现良好，平均准确率最高达89.6%。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个可扩展的测量工具，可支持发展语言学及其他语言数据集中的动词语义研究；但边缘案例、混合方式/结果动词以及下游发展应用仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Manner and result verbs encode different aspects of event structure and have been discussed in developmental work as a potentially informative distinction for studying early verb learning. However, this distinction remains difficult to measure at scale because large annotated resources for manner and result classification are not currently available. We present a computational approach for identifying manner and result verbs in sentence context. Using linguistically informed prompts, we generate sentence-level annotations with large language models over data drawn from MASC and InterCorp, extending coverage from previously annotated portions of VerbNet to 436 classes. We then train a RoBERTa-based classifier on these annotations and evaluate it on three held-out gold-standard datasets, including previously annotated items and a new expert-annotated set. Across these evaluations, the model shows promising performance, with average accuracy up to 89.6%. We present this work as a scalable measurement tool that can support future research on verb semantics in developmental and other language datasets, while noting that further validation is needed for borderline cases, mixed manner/result verbs, and downstream developmental applications.

</details>


### [101] [Language Acquisition Device in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16758)
*Masato Mita,Taiga Someya,Ryo Yoshida,Yohei Oseki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 受语言习得装置（LAD）假说启发，提出预预训练（PPT）于MP-STRUCT形式语言，该语言融合层级组合、特征依赖和长距离位移操作。仅500步PPT即可匹配强基线模型的token效率，并赋予模型类似人类的抗结构不合理语言能力。研究发现，有效PPT语言不依赖传统认为的高表达性与可学习性边界，而关键在于功能地标（functional landmarks）降低依赖解析歧义性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的数据效率远低于人类，现有使用合成语言（如k-Shuffle Dyck）进行预预训练的方法虽试图弥补这一差距，但对其有效性背后的机制理解不足。作者受语言习得装置假说启发，探索更具自然语言结构特征的形式语言能否提升PPT效果。

Method: 设计MP-STRUCT形式语言，包含MERGE（层级组合）、AGREE（特征依赖）和MOVE（长距离位移）三种操作。进行500步预预训练后微调，与k-Shuffle Dyck等强基线对比token效率与结构抗性。通过消融实验分析简化变体，并评估在C-RASP框架下的可定义性。

Result: MP-STRUCT在token效率上与强形式语言基线持平；在抗REVERSE等结构不合理语言方面表现更优。MP-STRUCT CORE虽无法在C-RASP中定义（超越transformer表达能力理论边界），但其性能仍优于k-Shuffle Dyck。功能地标是解释性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 有效的预预训练语言设计不仅取决于层级表达性，还取决于依赖解析的可及性（accessibility）。功能地标通过降低依赖解析的歧义性，成为驱动PPT效果的核心机制，这挑战了先前认为形式语言必须同时满足高表达性和电路可学习性的观点。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain substantially less data-efficient than humans. Pre-pretraining (PPT) on synthetic languages has been proposed to close this gap, with prior work emphasizing highly expressive formal languages such as $k$-Shuffle Dyck. Inspired by the Language Acquisition Device (LAD) hypothesis, which posits that innate constraints preemptively restrict the learner's hypothesis space to natural-language-like structure, we propose LAD-inspired PPT: pre-pretraining on MP-STRUCT, a formal language whose strings encode hierarchical composition, feature-based dependencies, and long-distance displacement via MERGE, AGREE, and MOVE. A brief 500-step PPT with MP-STRUCT matches strong formal-language baselines in token efficiency while additionally imparting a human-like resistance to structurally implausible languages (e.g., REVERSE). Analyzing simplified variants, we find that MP-STRUCT CORE outperforms $k$-Shuffle Dyck despite not being definable in C-RASP (a formal bound on transformer expressivity), challenging the prior hypothesis that effective PPT languages must be both hierarchically expressive and circuit-theoretically learnable. We show that functional landmarks, which reduce dependency resolution ambiguity, are a key driver, suggesting that effective PPT design depends not only on expressivity but also on the accessibility of dependency resolution.

</details>


### [102] [Retrieval-Based Multi-Label Legal Annotation: Extensible, Data-Efficient and Hallucination-Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16767)
*Li Zhang,Jaromir Savelka,Kevin Ashley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将多标签法律文本标注任务转化为基于冻结检索模型的最近邻检索问题，通过嵌入文档和标签描述并计算相似度来预测标签。在三个法律数据集上，该方法在准确率、数据效率和计算成本上均优于传统的参数化编码器和生成式大语言模型，且天然避免了标签幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签法律标注面临标签集庞大且动态变化、标注数据有限、文档冗长等挑战。参数化编码器需要为每个任务训练并在标签集变化时重新训练，而生成式大语言模型在标签空间增大时代价高昂且性能下降，同时存在标签幻觉问题。因此需要一种可扩展、高效且可靠的替代方案。

Method: 将法律标注重新定义为检索任务：使用冻结的检索模型将文档和标签描述嵌入到同一向量空间，通过k近邻搜索的方式预测标签。标签集更新时只需重新嵌入和重新索引，无需梯度回传的重新训练。

Result: 在ECtHR-A、ECtHR-B和Eurlex（100个标签）三个法律数据集上，检索方法取得了具有竞争力的准确率和强大的数据效率。在Eurlex上，Qwen-8B检索方法将Macro-F1从40.41（GPT-5.2零样本）提升至49.12，且计算成本比微调减少20-30倍。仅用100个训练样本时，检索方法在ECtHR-A上的Micro-F1几乎是层次化Legal-BERT的两倍（48.29 vs. 27.87）。GPT-5.2在0.12-0.9%的测试样本中产生了标签幻觉，而检索方法严格遵循标签集定义，从设计上消除了幻觉。

Conclusion: 基于检索模型的标注方法是高基数、快速变化的法律标签空间中一种实用且可部署的替代方案，兼具高准确率、数据效率、计算效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-label legal annotation requires assigning multiple labels from large, evolving taxonomies to long, fact-intensive documents, often under limited supervision. Parametric encoders typically require task-specific training and retraining when the label set changes, while prompting generative large language models becomes costly and degrades as the label space grows. We cast legal annotation as retrieval: we embed documents and label descriptions with a frozen retrieval model and predict labels via k-nearest neighbors in the embedding space, enabling updates by re-embedding and re-indexing rather than gradient-based backpropagation. Across three legal datasets (ECtHR-A, ECtHR-B, and Eurlex with 100 labels), retrieval achieves competitive accuracy and strong data efficiency; on Eurlex, Qwen-8B retrieval improves Macro-F1 from 40.41 (GPT-5.2, zero-shot) to 49.12 while reducing estimated compute by 20-30 times compared to fine-tuning. With only (N=100) training samples, retrieval nearly doubles Micro-F1 over hierarchical Legal-BERT on ECtHR-A (48.29 vs. 27.87). We also quantify a reliability failure mode of generative inference: GPT-5.2 hallucinates labels outside the provided taxonomy in 0.12-0.9% of test samples under deterministic decoding. In contrast, retrieval strictly respects defined label sets, eliminating hallucination by design. These results suggest retrieval-model-based annotators are a practical, deployable alternative for high-cardinality and rapidly changing legal label spaces.

</details>


### [103] [AgentKernelArena: Generalization-Aware Benchmarking of GPU Kernel Optimization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16819)
*Sharareh Younesian,Wenwen Ouyang,Sina Rafati,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Sharon Zhou,Ji Liu,Yue Liu,Yuchen Yang,Hao Li,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Vikram Appia,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了AgentKernelArena，一个用于评测AI编码智能体在GPU内核优化任务上的开源基准，包含196个任务、三种优化类型以及未见配置泛化测试，实验表明最强配置在PyTorch-to-HIP上达到6.89x加速，但PyTorch-to-HIP任务存在因硬编码形状假设导致的正确率下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内核基准仅评测单次LLM调用而非完整智能体工作流，且缺乏内核到内核优化以及未见配置泛化测试，无法全面衡量AI编码智能体在GPU内核优化上的实际能力。

Method: 构建了包含196个任务的基准，涵盖HIP-to-HIP优化、Triton-to-Triton优化和PyTorch-to-HIP翻译三种任务类型；设计隔离工作区、门控编译/正确性/性能检查、集中评分以及未见配置泛化协议来评测完整智能体工作流。

Result: 最强配置在PyTorch-to-HIP上平均加速6.89x，HIP-to-HIP上6.69x，Triton-to-Triton上2.13x；HIP-to-HIP和Triton-to-Triton优化可泛化到未见输入形状，但PyTorch-to-HIP存在显著的正确率下降。

Conclusion: AgentKernelArena是一个模块化、可扩展的框架，可用于对智能体GPU内核优化进行严格评测，并为未来工作提供了基础。

Abstract: GPU kernel optimization is increasingly critical for efficient deep learning systems, but writing high-performance kernels still requires substantial low-level expertise. Recent AI coding agents can iteratively read code, invoke compilers and profilers, and refine implementations, yet existing kernel benchmarks evaluate single LLM calls rather than full agent workflows, and none include both kernel-to-kernel optimization and unseen-configuration generalization testing. We present AgentKernelArena, an open-source benchmark for measuring AI coding agents on GPU kernel optimization. The benchmark contains 196 tasks spanning HIP-to-HIP optimization, Triton-to-Triton optimization, and PyTorch-to-HIP translation, and evaluates complete agent workflows in isolated workspaces using gated compilation, correctness, and performance checks, centralized scoring and an unseen-configuration generalization protocol that tests whether optimizations transfer to input configurations the agent never observed. Across production agents including Cursor Agent, Claude Code, and Codex Agent, we find near-perfect compilation and high correctness rates on most task categories, with the strongest configurations achieving mean speedups of up to 6.89x on PyTorch-to-HIP, 6.69x on HIP-to-HIP, and 2.13x on Triton-to-Triton tasks. Our unseen-configuration evaluation shows that HIP-to-HIP and Triton-to-Triton optimizations largely transfer to unseen input shapes, while PyTorch-to-HIP exhibits substantial correctness drops, indicating that agents generating kernels from scratch frequently hardcode shape-specific assumptions. AgentKernelArena is designed as a modular, extensible framework for rigorous evaluation of agentic GPU kernel optimization across agents, tasks, and hardware targets.

</details>


### [104] [Constrained Code Generation with Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16829)
*Lize Shao,Michael Cardei,Zichen Xie,Ferdinando Fioretto,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CDC（Constrained Diffusion for Code），一种无训练的神经符号推理框架，通过在离散扩散模型的逆向去噪过程中直接集成约束满足，来引导代码生成遵守功能正确性、安全性和语法约束。CDC利用约束感知的去噪算子，结合数学优化和程序分析，在中间程序状态中定位约束相关区域并局部调整去噪轨迹，在保持接近基础模型的同时生成可行程序。实验表明，CDC在多个代码生成基准上优于离散扩散和自回归基线，且计算开销更小、编辑更局部化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型作为代码生成的新兴范式，能在去噪过程中暴露全局程序状态，这为在最终代码提交前施加程序级功能和安全约束提供了自然干预点。现有方法缺乏在生成过程中有效整合约束的机制。

Method: CDC是一种无训练的神经符号推理框架，核心是约束感知的去噪算子。该算子结合数学优化与程序分析，识别中间程序状态中与约束相关的区域，并局部调整去噪轨迹，在保持与基础模型接近的同时将生成导向可行程序。

Result: 在代码生成基准上，CDC在功能正确性、安全性和语法约束满足方面持续优于离散扩散和自回归基线，且需要更少的矫正计算和更局部化的编辑。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型为代码生成中集成约束提供了独特的优势，CDC通过无训练的神经符号方法有效利用了这一优势，在多个约束维度上显著提升了代码质量。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are a powerful, emerging paradigm for code generation. They construct programs through iterative refinement of partially corrupted token sequences and enable parallel token refinement. Importantly, this paradigm exposes a global program state at each denoising step, which provides a natural intervention point for enforcing program-level functionality and security constraints, guiding the generation before the final code is committed. Building on this observation, the paper introduces Constrained Diffusion for Code (CDC), a training-free neurosymbolic inference framework that integrates constraint satisfaction directly into the reverse denoising process. CDC augments the base discrete diffusion sampler with constraint-aware denoising operators that combine mathematical optimization with program analysis to identify constraint-relevant regions of the intermediate program state and locally adjust the denoising trajectory, steering generation toward feasible programs while remaining close to the base model. Across code generation benchmarks, CDC consistently improves constraint satisfaction in functional correctness, security, and even syntax, outperforming discrete diffusion and autoregressive baselines with less corrective computation and more localized edits.

</details>


### [105] [CompactAttention: Accelerating Chunked Prefill with Block-Union KV Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16839)
*Jiwon Song,Dongwon Jo,Beomseok Kang,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CompactAttention提出基于块联合KV选择（Block-Union KV Selection）的分块预填充注意力机制，通过将二维块稀疏掩码转换为GQA感知的分组KV块表，在不进行显式KV压缩的情况下实现分块预填充下的高效注意力计算，在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct上RULER基准测试中保持接近密集注意力的准确性，同时在128K上下文长度下实现高达2.72倍的注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法主要针对一次性预填充设计，无法高效迁移到分块预填充场景：块稀疏核在查询长度受限于块大小时效率下降，细粒度模式搜索在每块累积KV缓存时计算代价过高；而直接针对分块预填充的QUOKA方法依赖查询子采样的token级KV选择，可能遗漏查询特定的KV条目并引入显式KV拷贝开销。

Method: CompactAttention将二维块稀疏掩码作为KV选择信号而非直接稀疏核执行计划，通过Q块联合和组内联合转换为GQA感知的每分组KV块表，在分页执行约束下生成保留输入掩码所选所有KV块的最小块表，实现原位访问选定KV块而无需显式KV压缩。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上，CompactAttention在RULER基准测试中保持接近密集注意力的准确性，同时在128K上下文长度的分块预填充下实现高达2.72倍的注意力加速。

Conclusion: CompactAttention通过块联合KV选择机制，在不损失准确性的前提下显著提升了分块预填充场景下的注意力计算效率，解决了现有方法在稀疏核效率和KV选择开销方面的局限性。

Abstract: Chunked prefill has become a widely adopted serving strategy for long-context large language models, but efficient attention computation in this regime remains challenging. Existing sparse attention methods are primarily designed for one-shot prefill and do not translate efficiently to chunked prefill: block-sparse kernels lose efficiency when the query length is limited by the chunk size, while fine-grained pattern search becomes costly when repeated over the accumulated KV cache at every chunk. QUOKA, a recent method that directly targets chunked prefill, avoids sparse-kernel overhead but relies on query-subsampled, token-level KV selection, which can miss query-specific KV entries and introduce explicit KV-copy overhead. To address these limitations, we propose CompactAttention, a chunked-prefill attention mechanism based on Block-Union KV Selection. CompactAttention treats 2D block-sparse masks as KV-selection signals rather than direct sparse-kernel execution plans, and converts them into GQA-aware per-group KV block tables through Q-block union and intra-group union. This construction produces the minimal block tables that preserve all KV blocks selected by the input masks under paged execution constraints, enabling selected KV blocks to be accessed in place without explicit KV compaction. On LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, CompactAttention maintains accuracy close to dense attention on the RULER benchmark while delivering up to 2.72$\times$ attention speedup at 128K context length under chunked prefill.

</details>


### [106] [RTI-Bench: A Structured Dataset for Indian Right-to-Information Decision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16843)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RTI-Bench，一个结构化的印度信息权法案（RTI）中央信息委员会（CIC）决策数据集，包含结果标签、豁免引用、IRAC式推理组件和程序时间线。数据集包含1,218个案例（来自指令-回复语料库）和298个PDF决策文档，标签覆盖率达89%（指令-回复语料库）和51%（PDF子集），标签精确率95.3%。零样本Mistral 7B基线在结果预测上取得57.3%准确率和37.0%宏F1，远超多数类基线（14.3%宏F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度RTI法案赋予公民向公共机构索取信息的权利，但CIC决策中使用密集的行政语言，使大多数公民难以理解或预测上诉是否值得提起。现有缺乏公开的结构化数据集来支持自动化分析和预测。

Method: 从两个来源构建数据集：(1) 1,218个案例来自公开指令-回复语料库，通过基于规则的提取添加结构字段；(2) 298个CIC决策PDF直接从委员会门户收集，涵盖五位委员和2023-2026年三档文档格式生成。对50个标注案例进行人工审核评估精确率。

Result: 指令-回复语料库标签覆盖率达89%，PDF子集首批发布覆盖率为51%。人工审核50个随机样本的标签精确率为95.3%。零样本Mistral 7B在100个案例上结果预测准确率57.3%，宏F1为37.0%，显著高于多数类基线14.3%宏F1。

Conclusion: RTI-Bench是首个公开的印度RTI行政决策结构化数据集，有助于推动该领域的自动推理和可访问性研究，数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: India's Right to Information Act, 2005 gives every citizen the right to demand information from public authorities, yet in practice most people cannot make sense of the dense administrative language used in Central Information Commission (CIC) decisions, let alone predict whether an appeal is worth filing. This paper introduces RTI-Bench, a structured dataset of CIC decisions with outcome labels, exemption citations, IRAC-style reasoning components, and procedural timelines. To the best of our knowledge it is the first publicly released structured dataset for Indian RTI administrative decisions. The dataset draws from two sources: 1,218 cases from a publicly available instruction-response corpus (with structured fields added through rule-based extraction), and 298 CIC decision PDFs collected directly from the Commission portal, spanning five commissioners and three document format generations from 2023 to 2026. Label coverage reaches 89% on the instruction-response corpus. For the PDF subset of 239 primary decisions, coverage is 51% in this first release. A random sample of 50 labelled cases was manually reviewed, yielding a label precision of 95.3%. A zero-shot Mistral 7B baseline on 100 cases gives 57.3% accuracy and 37.0% macro-F1 on outcome prediction, well above the majority-class baseline of 14.3% macro-F1. RTI-Bench is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/rti-bench

</details>


### [107] [MixSD: Mixed Contextual Self-Distillation for Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16865)
*Jiarui Liu,Lechen Zhang,Yongjin Yang,Yinghui He,Yingheng Wang,Weihao Xuan,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MixSD方法，通过将模型自身的专家条件分布与朴素条件分布混合构建动态监督信号，实现与模型原生生成分布对齐的知识注入，在保持近乎完美训练精度的同时，最多保留100%的预训练能力（标准SFT仅保留1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）在注入新知识时会严重退化模型的预训练能力（如推理和通用性能），本文论证这种遗忘源于微调目标偏离模型的自回归分布，迫使优化器模仿低概率的token序列。

Method: MixSD方法无需外部教师，通过混合基础模型自身的两个条件分布来动态构建监督信号：(1) 专家条件（观察注入事实的上下文）; (2) 朴素条件（反映模型原始先验）。混合后的监督序列既保留事实学习信号，又更接近基础模型的原始分布。

Result: 在合成语料和多个基准测试中，MixSD在不同模型规模下一致优于SFT和on-policy自蒸馏基线，实现了更好的记忆-保留权衡——保留高达100%的预训练能力且保持近乎完美的训练精度，而标准SFT仅保留1%。此外，MixSD产生更低的负对数似然监督目标，并减少了沿Fisher敏感参数方向的有害移动。

Conclusion: 将监督信号与模型的原生生成分布对齐是一种简单有效的知识注入原则，能够缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely used to inject new knowledge into language models, but it often degrades pretrained capabilities such as reasoning and general-domain performance. We argue this forgetting arises because fine-tuning targets from humans or external systems diverge from the model's autoregressive distribution, forcing the optimizer to imitate low-probability token sequences. To address this problem, we propose MixSD, a simple external-teacher-free method for distribution-aligned knowledge injection. Instead of training on fixed targets, MixSD constructs supervision dynamically by mixing tokens from two conditionals of the base model itself: an expert conditional that observes the injected fact in context, and a naive conditional that reflects the model's original prior. The resulting supervision sequences preserve the factual learning signal while remaining substantially closer to the base model's distribution. We evaluate MixSD on two synthetic corpora that we construct to study factual recall and arithmetic function acquisition in a controlled setting, together with established benchmarks for open-domain factual question answering and knowledge editing. Across multiple model scales and settings, MixSD consistently achieves a better memorization-retention trade-off compared to SFT and on-policy self distillation baselines, retaining up to 100% of the base model's held-out capability while maintaining near-perfect training accuracy, whereas standard SFT retains as little as 1%. We further show that MixSD produces substantially lower-NLL supervision targets under the base model and reduces harmful movement along Fisher-sensitive parameter directions. These results suggest that aligning supervision with the model's native generation distribution is a simple and effective principle for knowledge injection that mitigates catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [108] [PaliBench: A Multi-Reference Blueprint for Classical Language Translation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16881)
*Máté Metzger,Nadnapang Phophichit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PaliBench，一个巴利语-英语多参考翻译基准，以及一套将现有学术翻译转化为评估基础设施的可复用方法，适用于经典文本的多义性翻译评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字人文项目日益依赖机器翻译和大语言模型来扩展经典、宗教等未充分翻译文本的可及性，但现有翻译基准仅依赖单一参考译文，无法适应经典文本存在多种合理解读的特性。
