Abstract: Communication is a key component in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) for mitigating partial observability, yet prior approaches often rely on inefficient information exchange or fail to transmit sufficient state information. To address this, we propose LLM-driven Multi-Agent Communication (LMAC), which leverages an LLM's reasoning capability to design a communication protocol that enables all agents to reconstruct the underlying state as accurately and uniformly as possible. LMAC iteratively refines the protocol using an explicit state-awareness criterion, improving state recovery while narrowing differences in agents' knowledge. Experiments on diverse MARL benchmarks show that LMAC improves state reconstruction across agents and yields substantial performance gains over prior communication baselines.

</details>


### [255] [Learning to Solve Compositional Geometry Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18094)
*Mingfeng Fan,Jianan Zhou,Jiaqi Cheng,Yifeng Zhang,Jie Zhang,Guillaume Adrien Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiCon框架，通过微分注意力机制和双层对比学习解决组合几何路径规划问题（CGRP），该问题统一了包含点、线、面及混合几何任务的传统路径规划问题，有效应对了非点任务带来的不对称性、路径耦合和动作空间膨胀等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径规划问题主要关注点状任务，而现实场景中存在线状、面状及混合几何形状的任务需求。现有方法难以处理CGRP中非点任务带来的内在不对称性、路径与任务几何强耦合、以及可行动作空间过大导致的学习困难。

Method: 提出DiCon框架，包含两个核心组件：1）微分注意力机制，主动抑制低竞争力候选动作的概率质量；2）双层对比学习目标，促进鲁棒的全局实例表示并正则化几何感知的任务表示。DiCon作为即插即用框架，可集成到现有求解器中。

Result: 大量实验表明，DiCon在不同组成的CGRP实例上实现了强性能、广泛适用性和优越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DiCon有效解决了CGRP中非点任务带来的三大挑战（不对称性、路径耦合、动作空间膨胀），为复杂几何路由问题提供了统一的求解框架。

Abstract: We study the Compositional Geometry Routing Problem (CGRP), a unified superclass of traditional routing problems that covers point-only, line-only, area-only, and arbitrary hybrid task geometries, providing a broad abstraction for real-world routing scenarios. Beyond standard point-based routing, CGRP with non-point tasks can be inherently asymmetric, tightly coupled travel routes with the intrinsic path, and enlarges the action space with numerous feasible yet often irrelevant options, thereby posing significant challenges for both representation learning and decision-making. To address these challenges, we propose DiCon, a differential attention-assisted solver with contrastive learning, as a plug-and-play framework that tackles the problem from two complementary angles. First, we introduce a differential attention mechanism that actively suppresses the probability mass on less competitive candidate actions. Second, we design a double-level contrastive learning objective to promote robust global instance representations and regularize geometry-aware task representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiCon achieves strong performance, broad versatility, and superior generalization across diverse CGRP instances with different compositions.

</details>


### [256] [Safety Geometry Collapse in Multimodal LLMs and Adaptive Drift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18104)
*Jiahe Guo,Xiangran Guo,Jiaxuan Chen,Weixiang Zhao,Yanyan Zhao,Yutai Hou,Qianchao Wang,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）存在多模态安全差距，本文从表征几何角度发现并定义了"安全几何崩塌"现象：多模态输入压缩了沿拒绝方向的可分离性，导致安全机制失效。通过提出ReGap方法，利用自纠正机制自适应修正模态漂移，在无需训练的情况下显著提升多模态安全性，且不损害模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在文本模态中习得的安全能力无法有效迁移到语义等价的非文本输入（如图像），存在持续的多模态安全差距。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的表征层面理解，需要从几何视角揭示其内在机制。

Method: 1) 从表征几何角度分析文本对齐的"拒绝方向"与"模态诱导漂移方向"的相互作用；2) 提出"条件拒绝可分离性"指标量化安全几何崩塌；3) 通过固定强度的激活干预验证模态漂移的因果作用；4) 提出ReGap方法——一种无需训练、推理时自适应纠正模态漂移的方法，利用自纠正信号来识别每个输入的被感知危害性。

Result: 1) 多模态输入显著压缩了沿拒绝方向的可用分离性，导致安全几何崩塌；2) 更强的模态诱导漂移与更弱的拒绝可分离性和更高的攻击成功率一致相关；3) 抵消估计的模态漂移可恢复拒绝可分离性并提升多模态安全性；4) ReGap在多个多模态安全基准和通用能力基准上均表现出色，显著提升安全性且不损害通用能力。

Conclusion: 表征层面的模态对齐是提升多模态大语言模型安全性的关键方向，ReGap提供了一种实时、无需训练的有效安全改进方案，为构建更安全可靠的多模态模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often fail to transfer safety capabilities learned in the text modality to semantically equivalent non-text inputs, revealing a persistent multimodal safety gap. We study this gap from a representation-geometric perspective by analyzing a text-aligned refusal direction and a modality-induced drift direction. We show that multimodal inputs compress the usable separation along the refusal direction, making it no longer reliable for identifying and refusing harmful inputs. We refer to this failure mode as Safety Geometry Collapse. We quantify it through conditional refusal separability and show that stronger modality-induced drift is consistently associated with weaker refusal separability and higher attack success rates. We then validate the causal role of modality-induced drift through a fixed-strength activation intervention: counteracting the estimated drift restores refusal separability and improves multimodal safety. After drift correction, we further observe self-rectification, where the model recovers its ability to recognize and refuse harmful multimodal inputs during forward dynamics. This effect also provides an internal signal of the model's perceived harmfulness of each input. Motivated by this signal, we propose ReGap, a training-free inference-time method that adaptively corrects modality drift using self-rectification. Experiments across multiple multimodal safety benchmarks and utility benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ReGap, which significantly improves the safety of MLLMs without compromising general capabilities. Our findings highlight representation-level modality alignment as a crucial direction for real-time safety improvement and for building safer, more reliable MLLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Patchwork: A compact representation for 3D polygonal shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16266)
*Ruichen Zheng,Biao Zhang,Michael Birsak,Mikhail Skopenkov,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Patchwork提出了一种新的通用形状表示方法，能够用少量参数精确建模2D和3D几何，具有严格的数学框架、可证明的复杂度界限、任意精度逼近能力，以及通过渐进剪枝冗余元素实现高紧凑性的梯度优化方案，并原生支持内外分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的形状表示方法在参数效率、逼近精度和通用性之间存在权衡，缺乏一种既具有严格数学基础、又能以极少量参数高效表示2D和3D几何的通用表示方法。

Method: 1) 提出Patchwork形状表示，基于严格的数学框架，支持任意维度的任意精度逼近；2) 设计高效基于梯度的优化方案来拟合2D/3D数据；3) 提出新的正则化损失，通过渐进剪枝冗余元素实现高紧凑性。

Result: 快速拟合性能、相比现有方法所需参数数量极少、原生支持内外分类（inside-outside classification），实现了高紧凑性。

Conclusion: Patchwork是一种通用且紧凑的几何学习和重建任务表示方法，并在3D生成方面具有未来潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Patchwork, a new general-purpose shape representation capable of modeling 2D and 3D geometry with a small number of parameters. Patchwork is grounded in a rigorous mathematical framework, providing provable complexity bounds and the ability to approximate arbitrary shapes with arbitrary precision in any dimension. We propose an efficient gradient-based optimization scheme to fit Patchwork representations to 2D and 3D data, along with a novel regularization loss that progressively prunes redundant elements, yielding high compactness after convergence. Our approach offers fast fitting performance, a fraction of the required parameters compared to existing alternatives, and native support for inside-outside classification, making it a versatile and compact representation for geometric learning and reconstruction tasks, with future potential for 3D generation. Our implementation is available at: https://github.com/Ankbzpx/patchwork-experiment.

</details>


### [258] [Conformal tubular parameterization and toroidal bending of tube-like surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16305)
*Shunyu Yao,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对开放管状曲面（具有两个边界）的共形参数化框架，包括固定边界管状参数化、局部拟共形修正、自由边界变体以及两种共形环面弯曲映射，有效降低了畸变和接缝伪影，并对噪声边界具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管状曲面在几何处理、工程结构和医学解剖中广泛存在，但其固有的纵向和环向拓扑无法被传统的平面环形或矩形参数化域很好地保持。

Method: 1) 固定边界管状参数化：切割输入网格，计算圆盘到矩形的共形映射，提升到三维管状域；2) 局部拟共形修正：在环形域上对切割接缝附近的畸变进行校正；3) 自由边界变体：基于边界扩展和循环Laplacian平滑处理噪声/不规则边界；4) 两种共形环面弯曲映射：将管状参数化变换为环面几何，同时保留底层管状拓扑。

Result: 在合成管状网格和真实血管曲面上，该方法生成了低畸变参数化，有效减轻了接缝伪影，提高了对噪声边界的鲁棒性，并提供了灵活的管状和环面目标域。

Conclusion: 该框架为管状曲面的共形参数化提供了一套完整方案，解决了传统方法在拓扑保持、接缝畸变和边界噪声方面的不足。

Abstract: Tube-like surfaces are widely encountered in geometry processing, engineering structures, and medical anatomy, yet their intrinsic longitudinal and circumferential topology is not well preserved by conventional planar annular or rectangular parameterization domains. In this work, we propose a conformal parameterization framework for open tube-like surfaces with two boundary components. The proposed method first constructs a fixed-boundary tubular parameterization by cutting the input mesh, computing a disk-to-rectangle conformal map, and lifting the result to a three-dimensional tubular domain. To reduce residual distortion introduced near the cut seam, we further introduce a localized quasi-conformal correction scheme formulated on an annular domain, which improves conformality while leaving regions away from the seam unchanged. To handle noisy or irregular input boundaries, we also develop a free-boundary variant based on boundary extension and cycle-Laplacian smoothing, allowing the prescribed boundary constraints to be imposed on artificial outer rings rather than directly on the original surface. Finally, we derive two conformal toroidal bending maps that transform the tubular parameterization into toroidal geometries while preserving the underlying tube topology. Experiments on synthetic tube meshes and real vascular surfaces demonstrate that the proposed framework produces low-distortion parameterizations, effectively mitigates seam-induced artifacts, improves robustness for boundary-noisy inputs, and provides flexible tubular and toroidal target domains for downstream surface processing tasks.

</details>


### [259] [UVTran: Accurate Hole-Filling Parameterization with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16306)
*JunFeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出UVTran，一个基于Transformer的框架，通过预测辅助投影面并设计交叉注意力机制，解决了N边孔洞填充中因边界异质性导致的映射偏差和填充失败问题，在复杂边界条件下将公差满足率提升12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有N边孔洞填充方法将孔洞边界投影到附近平面或多边形建立对应关系，但忽略了边界异质性，导致映射偏差、公平性下降甚至填充失败。

Method: 提出UVTran框架：1) 基于Transformer预测辅助投影面以更好捕捉孔洞边界几何特征；2) 利用B样条局部性设计交叉注意力机制，使表面控制点偏向附近孔洞边界；3) 将控制点坐标体素化，将拟合问题转化为分类任务以降低对数值扰动和噪声的敏感性；4) 采用渐进分辨率训练策略，在粗分辨率下注入受控离散化误差以模拟分布偏移，缓解过拟合并提高高分辨率下的泛化能力。

Result: 在基准测试中，UVTran的容忍度满足率比工业和学术基线提升12%，且在复杂孔洞边界条件下始终生成公平的填充曲面。

Conclusion: UVTran能够在多种N边孔洞场景下产生更忠实的对应关系和更公平的修剪曲面。

Abstract: In industrial design, N-sided hole filling is typically formulated as the construction of a single trimmed B-spline surface by minimizing a fairness energy subject to geometric boundary constraints. This formulation requires an accurate parameter-space representation of the trimming curve on the filling surface. Most existing methods project the hole boundary onto a nearby plane or polygon to establish correspondence; however, they often neglect boundary heterogeneity, which can yield biased mappings, degrade fairness, and even cause filling failures. We propose UVTran, a transformer-based framework that predicts an auxiliary projection surface better to capture the geometric characteristics of the hole boundary. Exploiting B-spline locality, we design a cross-attention mechanism that biases each surface control point toward the nearby hole boundary, preserving local geometric detail. We voxelize control-point coordinates and formulate the fitting problem as a classification task, which reduces the model's sensitivity to small numerical perturbations and noise. We adopt a progressive-resolution training strategy that injects controlled discretization errors at coarse resolutions to mimic distribution shifts, thereby mitigating overfitting and improving generalization at high resolution. On our benchmark, UVTran outperforms both industrial and academic baselines: the tolerance-satisfaction rate improves by $12\%$, and it consistently produces fair filled surfaces even under complex hole boundary conditions. These results suggest that UVTran yields more faithful correspondences and fairer trimmed surfaces across a wide range of N-sided holes.

</details>


### [260] [Conformal Geometric Algebra as a Symbolic Interface for LLM-Driven 3D Scene Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16308)
*Manos Kamarianakis,Pandelis Sofianos,George Papagiannakis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文探讨在自然语言驱动的3D场景编辑中，LLM应输出何种符号格式才能可靠执行。实验对比了共形几何代数(CGA)紧凑符号接口、非CGA的紧凑SE3控制与冗长的Euclidean 4×4矩阵基线。主要发现：在序列指令链中，Simple CGA和Compact SE3均达到100%解析有效性，但CGA更可靠地保持了有序操作链的精确性（97.5% vs 90.0%, p=0.016），且完成令牌成本更低（112.6 vs 133.6 tokens）。在困难语义测试集上，紧凑表示（CGA 45.0%, SE3 42.0%, Shenlong 44.0%）均显著优于Euclidean基线（24.0%），但CGA与SE3之间无显著差异。结果表明紧凑符号接口能带来可靠性-成本收益，而CGA的代数结构在有序指令链上提供了额外优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM在自然语言3D场景编辑中应使用何种符号格式以实现可靠执行，特别关注代数结构（如CGA的几何积）是否在紧凑语法之外带来额外优势。

Method: 设计序列压力测试协议（20模板×6次试验，每方法n=120输出）和困难语义测试集（每方法n=100），对比Simple CGA、Compact SE3、Shenlong及Euclidean 4×4矩阵四种符号接口，使用受控提示和确定性几何执行评估解析有效性、操作链保真度和几何正确性。

Result: 序列压力测试：CGA和SE3均100%解析有效，但CGA操作链精确保真度更高（97.5% vs 90.0%, p=0.016），令牌成本更低（112.6 vs 133.6）。困难语义测试：紧凑表示（CGA 45.0%, SE3 42.0%, Shenlong 44.0%）均显著优于Euclidean基线（24.0%），但CGA与SE3差异不显著（p=0.7755）。

Conclusion: 紧凑符号接口能驱动可靠性-成本收益提升，CGA的代数组合结构在有序指令链上提供额外优势，超越紧凑性本身；解析有效性与几何正确性分离暴露了语法单一指标无法捕捉的优化空间。

Abstract: What symbolic format should an LLM emit for reliable 3D scene editing from natural language, and does algebraic structure help beyond compact syntax? We evaluate Conformal Geometric Algebra (CGA) as a compact symbolic interface against a verbose Euclidean 4$\times$4 matrix baseline and a non-CGA Compact SE3 control in a natural-language 3D editing pipeline with controlled prompting and deterministic geometric execution.
  Our primary result is compositional fidelity under sequential instruction chains. In a sequence-stress protocol (20 templates, 6 trials each; $\texttt{n=120}$ outputs per method), Simple CGA and Compact SE3 both achieve 100% parse validity, but Simple CGA preserves exact ordered operation chains more reliably (97.5% vs 90.0%, two-proportion $\texttt{p=0.016}$) with lower completion-token cost (112.6 vs 133.6 tokens). This pattern is consistent with algebraic expression form supporting compositional faithfulness beyond compactness alone.
  A second result is confirmatory in the powered hard semantic suite ($\texttt{n=100}$ per method): compact representations (Simple CGA 45.0%, Compact SE3 42.0%, Shenlong 44.0%) all exceed the Euclidean 4$\times$4 baseline (24.0%). Simple CGA vs Euclidean is +21 pp ($\texttt{p=0.0028}$) and Compact SE3 vs Euclidean is +18 pp ($\texttt{p=0.0103}$), while Simple CGA vs Compact SE3 is statistically close ($\texttt{p=0.7755}$). Separating parse validity from geometric correctness reveals substantial optimization headroom invisible to syntax-only metrics.
  Overall, compact symbolic interfaces appear to drive reliability-cost gains, with CGA motor composition providing an additional advantage on ordered instruction chains. These findings inform real-time natural-language editing in immersive and interactive 3D environments.

</details>


### [261] [Generative 3D Gaussians with Learned Density Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16355)
*Runjie Yan,Yan-Pei Cao,Peng Wang,Ding Liang,Yuan-Chen Guo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出密度采样高斯(DeG)表示法，通过可学习概率密度函数定义高斯中心，结合八叉树实现自适应密度控制；并提出VecSeq规范重索引机制解决无序集合潜变量的扩散收敛问题，在单图到3D生成任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯表示要么将高斯约束在固定体素网格/阵列中（限制适应性），要么依赖离散的稠密化和剪枝启发式（缺乏可微性）。DeG旨在桥接自适应渲染基元与可扩展生成建模之间的鸿沟，提供一种兼具结构适应性和训练稳定性的3D表示。

Method: 1) DeG表示：将高斯中心建模为八叉树定义的可学习概率密度函数的样本，联合优化空间密度和高斯属性；2) 可微分密度控制：提出渲染损失贡献梯度，作为标准高斯泼溅中离散稠密化和剪枝启发式的完全可微替代；3) VecSeq机制：将无序集合潜变量通过确定性3D Sobol序列进行规范重索引，转化为序列建模任务，加速扩散模型收敛。

Result: 在单图到3D生成任务上达到最先进质量，结合了非结构化基元的结构适应性和基于网格方法的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: DeG通过概率密度建模实现了可微分的自适应密度控制，VecSeq解决了集合潜变量的扩散收敛难题，为生成式3D建模提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We present Density-Sampled Gaussians (DeG), a novel 3D representation designed to bridge the gap between adaptive rendering primitives and scalable generative modeling. Unlike existing approaches that constrain 3D Gaussians to fixed voxel grids or arrays, DeG models Gaussian centers as samples from a learnable probability density function defined over an octree. This formulation provides a rigorous mathematical framework for adaptive density control: by jointly optimizing the spatial density and Gaussian attributes under rendering supervision, our model naturally concentrates primitives in regions of high geometric complexity. We achieve this via a new render loss contribution gradient that serves as a fully differentiable analogue to the discrete densification and pruning heuristics used in standard Gaussian Splatting. The resulting representation is highly flexible, supporting variable-resolution decoding from a single latent code by simply adjusting the sampling budget. To enable generative synthesis, we train a latent diffusion model on DeG. We identify a critical challenge in applying diffusion to unordered set-structured latents, which can significantly slow convergence, and propose VecSeq, a canonical re-indexing mechanism that anchors latent tokens to a deterministic 3D Sobol sequence. This transforms the ambiguous set-generation problem into a robust sequence modeling task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our pipeline achieves state-of-the-art quality in single-image-to-3D generation, combining the structural adaptivity of unstructured primitives with the training stability of grid-based methods.

</details>


### [262] [Ordered Front-to-back Any-Hit Traversal in RTX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16697)
*Ingo Wald*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在现有光线追踪管线约束下，实现有序从前往后任意命中遍历（FTB）的多种方法，并评估了不同实现方案。FTB要求遍历以严格从前到后的顺序迭代光线上的连续命中，且不跳过任何交点（即使距离相同）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光线追踪管线通常无法保证以严格从前到后的顺序遍历所有命中点，且可能跳过相同距离处的交点，这限制了某些需要有序命中列表的应用（如透明材质渲染、体积效应等）。

Method: 在现有光线追踪管线约束下，设计了多种实现有序FTB遍历的不同方案，并进行了比较评估。

Result: 完成了多种FTB实现方案的对比评估。

Conclusion: 提出了多种在现有管线约束下实现有序FTB遍历的有效方法，为该问题的解决提供了系统性的方案选择和评估依据。

Abstract: We look at the problem of Ordered Front-To-Back Any-Hit Traversal (FTB); i.e., a traversal that iterates through successive hits along a ray in a guaranteed front to back-sorted order, and without skip- ping any intersections even if they occur at the same distance. We describe multiple different ways of solving this problem within the constraints of the existing ray tracing pipeline, and evaluate the different realizations.

</details>


### [263] [Genflow Ad Studio: A Compound AI Architecture for Brand-Aligned, Self-Correcting Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16748)
*Debanshu Das,Lavi Nigam,Sunil Kumar Jang Bahadur,Gopala Dhar*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Genflow是一个复合AI系统，通过检索式"品牌DNA"提取模块和对抗性多智能体质量控制循环，将品牌合规视频生成率从42%提升至89%，解决了生成式视频模型在企业应用中存在的时序不一致和品牌错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式视频模型虽具有高视觉保真度，但在企业环境中受限于时序不一致和严重的品牌错位问题。现有单体架构难以执行严格的品牌约束，经常幻觉生成未批准的视觉资产。

Method: 提出Genflow复合AI系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) 检索式"品牌DNA"提取模块，根据企业品牌指南参数化生成过程；(2) 对抗性多智能体质量控制（QC）循环，采用评估智能体迭代批评生成帧与提取参数的一致性，驱动生成模型优化直至达到确定性共识。

Result: 品牌合规视频生成率从42%提升至89%。

Conclusion: 通过向多阶段、自我纠错流水线转变，Genflow为可扩展的企业级生成式系统建立了稳健框架。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative video models demonstrate high visual fidelity, yet their integration into enterprise environments is restricted by temporal inconsistencies and severe brand misalignment. Current monolithic architectures struggle to enforce rigid brand constraints, frequently hallucinating unapproved visual assets. We introduce Genflow, a Compound AI System designed to enforce brand consistency in generative media production. Our architecture integrates a retrieval-based 'Brand DNA' extraction module to parameterize generation according to established corporate identity guidelines. Furthermore, we implement an Adversarial Multi-Agent Quality Control (QC) loop. Instead of a single-pass generation, this pipeline employs evaluator agents to iteratively critique generated frames against the extracted parameters, prompting generator models to refine outputs until a deterministic consensus is reached. By transitioning to a multi-stage, self-correcting pipeline, Genflow improved the yield of brand-compliant video generations from 42% to 89%, establishing a robust framework for scalable, enterprise-grade generative systems.

</details>


### [264] [QuadLink: Autoregressive Quad-Dominant Mesh Generation via Point-Relation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16813)
*Yiheng Zhang,Zhe Zhu,Tingrui Shen,Zhuojiang Cai,Tianxiao Li,Zixing Zhao,Qiujie Dong,Zhiyang Dou,Jiepeng Wang,Le Wan,Yuwang Wang,Wenping Wang,Yuan Liu,Cheng Lin*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: QuadLink是一个三阶段统一框架，通过将点云中的点链接为结构化面来生成生产就绪的四边形主导网格，该方法采用混合质心条件顶点链接模型，先预测锚点（顶点和面质心），再学习质心条件链接，最后通过四边形优先策略组装多边形面，并引入Tri-to-Quad算子将艺术三角网格转换为训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从点云生成各向异性的四边形主导网格是3D内容创作的核心需求，但现有方法要么只能生成纯三角网格，要么只能生成各向同性密度的纯四边形网格，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出三阶段统一框架QuadLink：(1) 预测统一的锚点集（顶点和面质心）；(2) 学习质心条件链接，将顶点与面质心关联；(3) 采用四边形优先策略，结合鲁棒的几何验证方法组装多边形面。同时引入Tri-to-Quad算子，通过全局合并选择将艺术三角网格转换为四边形主导训练数据。

Result: 大量实验表明，QuadLink能从点云生成生产就绪的四边形主导网格，在几何保真度和拓扑质量上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: QuadLink原生支持混合多边形拓扑，无需架构修改即可泛化到任意n边形网格，实现了稀疏、各向异性的四边形主导网格生成，并保持连贯的边流。

Abstract: The generation of production-ready quad-dominant meshes is a cornerstone of modern 3D content creation. Generating anisotropic quad-dominant meshes from point clouds is challenging, as existing methods are typically limited to producing either pure triangular meshes or pure quadrilateral meshes with isotropic densities. In this paper, we present QuadLink, a unified framework consisting of three stages for quad-dominant mesh generation by linking points into structured faces. QuadLink formulates polygonal mesh generation as a hybrid centroid-conditioned vertex linking model: it first predicts a unified set of anchors (vertices and face centroids), then learns centroid-conditioned links that associate vertices with face centroids, and finally assembles polygonal faces with a quad-first strategy guided by robust geometric verification strategies. This link-based formulation enables efficient generation of sparse and anisotropic quad-dominant meshes with coherent edge flow and meanwhile supporting hybrid polygonal topology. To construct training data for this model, we further introduce a Tri-to-Quad Operator that converts artistic triangle meshes into quad-dominant training data via global merge selection. Extensive experiments show that QuadLink produces production-ready quad-dominant meshes from point clouds and achieves improved geometric fidelity and topological quality compared to prior baselines. Our method natively supports hybrid polygonal topology, generalizing to arbitrary n-gon meshes without architectural changes.

</details>


### [265] [A Single Atlas is All You Need: Decoder-Side Gaussian Splatting for Immersive Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17002)
*Dawid Mieloch,Stuart Perry*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出解码端高斯泼溅(DSGS)框架，用前馈3DGS推理替代传统解码端深度估计(DSDE)，利用有损压缩作为隐式低通滤波器稳定泼溅预测，在极稀疏视角下实现+5.79 dB BD-PSNR和+0.054 BD-SSIM提升，并将视角间Delta IV-PSNR从17.2 dB降至6.4 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式视频传输受限于像素率瓶颈，传统DSDE在处理复杂几何、视角间闪烁和非朗伯反射时效果不佳，而3DGS虽合成质量高但传输高斯泼溅的带宽成本过高且与标准视频编解码器不兼容。

Method: DSGS框架在解码端用前馈3DGS推理替代深度估计阶段，仅传输压缩后的纹理和元数据，在解码端优化体积场景。利用有损压缩的隐式低通滤波特性稳定泼溅预测。

Result: 相比DSDE基线，DSGS在极稀疏视角下实现+5.79 dB BD-PSNR和+0.054 BD-SSIM提升，最大视角间Delta IV-PSNR从17.2 dB降至6.4 dB，有损压缩比特流比无损质量提升十倍缩小。

Conclusion: 有损压缩可作为隐式低通滤波器稳定前馈泼溅预测，DSGS实现了压缩效率与合成质量的协同提升，显著缩小了传输视口与虚拟视口之间的域偏移。

Abstract: Immersive video delivery is bottlenecked by pixel-rate constraints, making the transmission of high-resolution depth maps or explicit 3D volumetric data expensive. Decoder-Side Depth Estimation (DSDE) shifts depth computation to the client, but struggles with complex geometries, inter-view flickering, and non-Lambertian reflections. Conversely, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers state-of-the-art view synthesis, but transmitting splats (or their projected 2D maps) incurs prohibitive bandwidth costs and is poorly aligned with standard video codecs. We propose Decoder-Side Gaussian Splatting (DSGS), a framework that natively replaces the depth-estimation stage of DSDE with feed-forward 3DGS inference, optimizing volumetric scenes entirely on the decoder side from compressed textures and metadata. A central, counterintuitive finding is that lossy compression acts as an implicit low-pass filter stabilizing feed-forward splat prediction: compressed bitstreams exceed lossless quality while shrinking tenfold. Under extreme view sparsity (one 2D atlas comprising 4 input views), DSGS achieves a +5.79 dB BD-PSNR and +0.054 BD-SSIM gain over the DSDE anchor while reducing maximum inter-view Delta IV-PSNR from 17.2 dB to 6.4 dB, minimizing the domain shift between transmitted and virtual viewports.

</details>


### [266] [Topo-GS: Continuous Volumetric Embedding of High-Dimensional Data via Topological Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17011)
*João Paulo Gois,Luis Gustavo Nonato*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Topo-GS 是一个利用3D高斯泼溅技术将高维数据降维为连续体积表示（而非传统离散点云）的框架，通过局部几何约束和拓扑感知策略保持数据流形的局部拓扑 fidelity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降维算法将高维数据映射为离散点云，存在视觉遮挡和人工不连续问题，无法表示底层流形的连续密度。现有方法缺乏对数据连续本质的建模能力。

Method: 将3DGS重新用于网格无体积重建过程，使用局部几何约束（而非光度损失）驱动优化；通过正交Procrustes目标强制执行As-Rigid-As-Possible先验，将每个高斯的空间协方差对齐到局部切空间；采用拓扑感知策略，针对不同内在维度（1D轨迹或2D曲面）定制损失函数。

Result: 将离散散点图成功转化为连续体积表示，投影失真显现为可观察的几何变化，同时保持与离散基线相当的局部拓扑 fidelity。

Conclusion: Topo-GS 实现了从离散点云到连续体积表示的有效转换，为高维数据可视化提供了更好的连续性和密度表达能力。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction algorithms map high-dimensional data into visualizable 2D or 3D spaces, but traditionally rely on a discrete point-cloud paradigm. This discrete abstraction is susceptible to visual occlusion and artificial discontinuities, often failing to represent the continuous density of the underlying manifold. To address these limitations, we introduce Topo-GS, a framework that repurposes 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to cast multidimensional projection as a meshless volumetric reconstruction process. Instead of standard photometric losses, Topo-GS is driven by local geometric constraints. By solving orthogonal Procrustes targets, the optimization enforces an As-Rigid-As-Possible prior while explicitly aligning the spatial covariance of each Gaussian to the local tangent space. Recognizing that unrolling data of varying intrinsic dimensionalities requires distinct spatial treatments, we utilize a topology-aware strategy that tailors the loss formulation to preserve either continuous 1D trajectories or cohesive 2D surfaces. Quantitative and visual evaluations demonstrate that Topo-GS successfully transforms discrete scatter plots into continuous volumetric representations, where inherent projection distortions explicitly manifest as observable geometric variations, while preserving local topological fidelity comparable to discrete baselines.

</details>


### [267] [Self-Improving CAD Generation Agents with Finite Element Analysis as Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17448)
*Guijin Son,Jehyun Park,Seyeon Park,Sunghee Ahn,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更贴近工业实践的CAD生成任务框架，要求模型从工程需求文本直接生成完整的可装配多部件STEP文件，并通过有限元分析（FEA）进行物理验证。实验表明，GPT-5.5和Claude Code等智能体在首次尝试中完全无法生成严格通过FEA验证的工件。作者引入文本蓝图模式和21视图渲染两种监督信号来模拟工程师的迭代过程，在S2O和Fusion360数据集上显著提升了几何重建质量（Box-IoU提升约0.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD生成方法将零件合成与装配视为两个独立步骤，仅以与参考模型的几何接近度作为评价标准，缺乏对工程物理需求的考量，无法满足工业设计中的迭代验证需求。

Method: 1) 引入新的任务范式：从自由形式工程需求文本生成完整多部件STEP文件，并通过FEA自动验证；2) 提出两种监督信号：文本蓝图模式（text-only blueprint schema）用于结构化需求描述，21视图图像渲染器辅助智能体的视觉检查；3) 采用GPT-5.5和Claude Code等智能体在S2O和Fusion360数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: 智能体首次尝试的严格通过率为零，最佳配置平均仅满足约20%的已定义需求；引入反馈工具后，GPT-5.5/xhigh在S2O上Box-IoU从0.444提升至0.592，在Fusion360上从0.397提升至0.505。

Conclusion: 仅凭几何视觉合理性不足以支撑工业级CAD生成，需要结合物理与结构验证的监督信号。所提出的蓝图和渲染反馈机制能有效对齐工程迭代实践，推动CAD程序向满足物理要求的工件发展。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) is the backbone of modern industrial design, yet learned CAD generators still fall short of real engineering pipelines: they neither iterate like engineers nor evaluate what engineering requires. Prior work has treated CAD generation as two disjoint steps, part synthesis and assembly, where the former is graded by proximity to a gold reference and the latter, when handled at all, is reduced to a separate constraint solving step. In this work, we introduce a more industry-native task formulation that requires a model to produce a fully assembled multi-part STEP file from a free-form engineering brief, which is then validated via finite element analysis (FEA). FEA validation reveals that Codex (GPT-5.5) and Claude Code (Opus-4.7) agents do not produce a single strict-passing artifact in the main first-attempt sweep, with the best configuration meeting only about 20% of typed requirements on average. Moreover, we introduce two additional supervision signals, a novel text-only blueprint schema and a 21-view image renderer that aids the agent's visual inspection, that better align the generation loop with how engineers iterate in practice. On S2O and Fusion360, the same feedback tools improve geometric reconstruction, with GPT-5.5/xhigh rising from 0.444 to 0.592 Box-IoU on S2O and from 0.397 to 0.505 on Fusion360. Together these signals move CAD programs toward artifacts that are not only visually plausible but also checked against physical and structural requirements.

</details>


### [268] [Real-Time Neural Hair Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17557)
*Chenghao Wu,Yuefan Shen,Tao Huang,Kai Yan,Zahra Montazeri,Kui Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级实时方法，从严重欠采样的光栅化输入中重建基于发丝的头发G-Buffer，通过神经空间重建、时间累积和切线引导完成位置重建，实现基于物理的延迟头发着色，在多种发型和动态场景下优于现有头发去噪技术和工业级神经重建方案（如DLSS、FSR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的头发重建方法在处理严重欠采样的光栅化输入时效果不佳，难以在实时场景中高质量地重建头发G-Buffer，而工业级通用方案（如DLSS、FSR）对头发这种具有复杂几何和透明度特征的材质缺乏针对性优化。

Method: 首先通过神经空间重建和时间累积恢复头发覆盖度（像素内的部分头发可见性）和切线信息；然后利用切线引导的重建步骤完成位置信息重建；最后基于物理的延迟着色进行头发渲染。

Result: 在直发、波浪卷发、非洲卷发、马尾辫等多种发型，以及静态和动态场景下进行评估，该方法在头发重建质量上优于现有的头发专用去噪技术和DLSS、FSR等通用工业神经重建方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量级实时头发G-Buffer重建方法能够有效处理严重欠采样的输入，在保证实时性能的同时实现高质量头发重建，为头发渲染提供了一种专门优化的高效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight real-time method for reconstructing strand-based hair G-Buffers from severely undersampled rasterized inputs. Our pipeline first applies neural spatial reconstruction and temporal accumulation to recover hair coverage, i.e., fractional hair visibility within a pixel, and tangent. It then uses a tangent-guided reconstruction step to complete the position, which is subsequently used for physically based deferred hair shading. We evaluate our method across a diverse set of hairstyles, including straight, wavy, afro, and ponytail styles, under both static and dynamic scenarios. Our method achieves higher hair reconstruction quality than existing hair-specific denoising techniques and general industrial neural reconstruction solutions such as DLSS and FSR.

</details>


### [269] [A real time lighting technique for procedurally generated 2d isometric game terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17666)
*Érick Oliveira Rodrigues,Esteban Clua*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对程序化生成等距地图的自动实时光照技术，通过2D物体的3D几何形状估计产生2.5D效果，并利用不透明度贴图解决几何估计问题，采用CPU-GPU协同方案，经多群体评估性能令人满意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对程序化生成的等距地图缺乏自动实时光照效果的问题，提出一种能从2D对象估算3D几何形状以实现光照交互的方法。

Method: 基于字符串种子生成场景，通过几何形状估计将2D物体视为3D以进行光照交互，利用不透明度贴图解决几何估计产生的伪影，采用CPU与GPU协同计算的耦合方案。

Result: 通过游戏玩家、程序员和设计师对视觉效果、游戏性和性能的评估，并在不同显卡和处理器上测试帧率，结果令人满意。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效实现程序化等距地图的自动实时2.5D光照效果，性能表现良好。

Abstract: This work proposes an automatic real time lighting technique for procedurally generated isometric maps. The scenario is generated from a string seed and the proposed lighting system estimates the geometrical shape of the 2D objects as if they were 3D for further light interaction, therefore producing a 2.5D effect. We employ opacity maps to overcome an issue generated by the geometrical shape estimation. The solution is a coupled approach between the CPU and GPU. The produced visuals, gameplay and performance were evaluated by gamers, programmers and designers. Furthermore, the performance, in terms of frames per second, was evaluated over distinct graphics cards and processors and was satisfactory.

</details>


### [270] [Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting using Tensor Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17855)
*Sheng Li,Yang Sui,Yue Wu,Zhuoran Song,Bo Yuan,Xulong Tang,Yue Dai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: TensorGS利用Tensor Core加速3D高斯溅射的渲染过程，通过将光栅化计算张量化并引入跨瓦片分组来提升高斯复用和Tensor Core利用率，在保持图像质量的同时实现了1.65倍的端到端渲染加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射(3DGS)的渲染成本在延迟敏感场景下仍然过高，尤其是光栅化阶段是计算密集型瓶颈；同时现代GPU的高吞吐Tensor Core在现有3DGS系统中完全闲置，而3DGS在FP16精度下质量损失可忽略，为Tensor Core加速提供了机会。

Method: 提出TensorGS框架：1) 将光栅化中每个高斯对像素贡献的计算张量化为兼容Tensor Core的矩阵运算；2) 引入跨瓦片分组(Cross-Tile Grouping)机制，提高高斯复用，分摊开销，增加Tensor Core利用率。

Result: 端到端渲染性能提升1.65倍，同时保持图像质量（FP16精度下质量退化可忽略）。

Conclusion: 通过将3DGS光栅化计算适配到Tensor Core的执行模型，并优化数据复用，可以显著加速渲染过程，为延迟敏感的实时渲染场景提供更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a leading technique for real-time neural rendering and 3D scene reconstruction, but its rendering cost remains too high for many latency-sensitive scenarios. In particular, the rasterization stage in 3DGS dominates end-to-end rendering time, during which the renderer repeatedly evaluates each Gaussian's contribution to each covered pixel, making this stage compute-bound. At the same time, modern GPUs provide high-throughput Tensor Cores for low-precision matrix operations, yet existing 3DGS systems execute rasterization entirely on CUDA cores and leave Tensor Cores idle. We find that 3DGS rendering can be executed in FP16 with negligible quality degradation, suggesting a promising opportunity for Tensor Core acceleration. However, exploiting Tensor Cores for 3DGS is non-trivial because rasterization does not naturally match their execution model. Existing 3DGS rasterization is expressed as irregular per-pixel scalar operations, whereas Tensor Cores require dense, regular, and reuse-rich matrix workloads. Moreover, conventional tile-by-tile execution fails to exploit Gaussian reuse across neighboring tiles, resulting in repeated data loading and thus high data movement overhead. To this end, we present TensorGS, a 3DGS acceleration framework using Tensor Cores. TensorGS tensorizes the dominant rasterization computation into Tensor-Core-compatible matrix operations and introduces cross-tile grouping to improve Gaussian reuse, amortize overhead, and increase Tensor Core utilization. Experimental results show that TensorGS improves end-to-end rendering performance by 1.65$\times$ while preserving image quality.

</details>


### [271] [Tangent Blow-Ups for Processing Non-Manifold Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18215)
*Alice Petrov,Mohammad Sina Nabizadeh,Ana Dodik,Justin Solomon*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出"切向爆裂"（tangent blow-up）方法，通过将几何数据从原始空间提升到环境空间与Grassmannian切平面的乘积空间，在奇点处恢复结构，并定义离散微分算子，应用于测地线计算、分割、参数化和曲率估计等几何处理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何处理流程隐含假设输入数据是流形或其采样，具有唯一的切平面，但实际几何数据常包含尖锐特征（边、角、自交、分支结点等奇点），使得标准方法在这些点处失效。因此需要一种能处理奇异性空间的几何处理方法。

Method: 受代数几何启发，提出"切向爆裂"表示：将几何数据提升到环境空间与Grassmannian（切平面格拉斯曼流形）的乘积空间中，使位置相同但切方向、曲率或高阶接触不同的点在提升空间中分离。为切向爆裂赋予乘积度量，并直接在提升域上定义离散微分算子（梯度、散度、拉普拉斯算子）。

Result: 在测地线计算、分割、曲面参数化和曲率估计等任务中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 切向爆裂方法能够将几何处理推广到包含奇异性的空间，通过在提升域中恢复结构并定义微分算子，使得原本在奇点处失效的标准方法得以适用。

Abstract: Many geometry processing pipelines implicitly assume their input data is a manifold, or is sampled from one, with a unique tangent plane at every point. Geometric data, however, routinely contains sharp features like edges, corners, self-intersections, branching junctions, and other singularities, rendering standard methods ill-defined at these points. To bring geometry processing to these and other singular spaces, we introduce the ``tangent blow-up,'' a representation inspired by algebraic geometry that restores structure at singularities by lifting to the product of the ambient space and the Grassmannian of tangent planes. After iterating this construction, points that coincide in position but differ in tangent direction, curvature, or higher-order contact become well-separated. We equip the tangent blow-up with a product metric and define discretized differential operators, such as the gradient, divergence, and Laplacian, directly in the lifted domain. We demonstrate our framework across geodesic computation, segmentation, surface parameterization, and curvature estimation.

</details>
